### [29] [Ask a Language Model for Lottery Numbers: Concentration in Repeated Six-of-49 Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00052)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 六个语言模型在六选一至49的随机整数请求上表现出显著的号码集中现象，有效多样性远低于独立均匀抽样基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验语言模型在"随机数生成"任务中是否真正具备均匀随机性，尤其在彩票类应用场景下的可靠性。

Method: 对六种语言模型配置进行测试，使用四种英文提示变体，共1,200次调用（1,184次返回有效彩票号码），并计算号码频率的有效多样性；对照独立均匀六选41-49的模拟第五百分位阈值，另用两个波兰彩票样本作物理彩票对照。

Result: 有效多样性为9.9-18.0，远低于模拟阈值46.6-46.7；各系统仅产生8-93个不同无序彩票组合，众数彩票占有效回答的22.5%-68.0%；实体彩票样本多样性为41.1和41.4。

Conclusion: 在测试的部署设置下，模型输出存在实质性集中，但未揭示其机制，也未确立在其他提示、温度或工具配置下的表现。

Abstract: We evaluate six language-model configurations on requests for six distinct random integers from 1-49. Across 1,200 attempted calls using four English prompt variants, 1,184 responses yielded valid tickets. Effective diversity of number frequencies ranged from 9.9 to 18.0, compared with simulated fifth-percentile thresholds of 46.6-46.7 under independent uniform six-of-49 sampling at the corresponding sample sizes. Systems produced 8-93 distinct unordered tickets, and their modal tickets accounted for 22.5-68.0% of valid responses. Two archived Polish Lotto samples provided a physical-lottery comparison, with effective diversities of 41.1 and 41.4 at smaller sample sizes. These results demonstrate substantial concentration under the tested deployment settings. They do not identify its mechanism or establish performance under other prompts, temperatures, or tool configurations.

</details>


### [30] [On-Device Commercial Intent Retrieval Under Size, Latency, and Privacy Constraints: A 3 MiB Retrieval System with Typed Egress Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00170)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在端侧约束下（载荷<3 MiB、p95<20 ms、无数据外传）构建6202叶商业分类检索路径，发现准确率损失集中在无锚点查询上，需任务自适应而非单纯扩大教师容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究完全在用户设备上运行的商业意图推断，需满足三项预先冻结的约束：下载载荷小于3 MiB、Tier-0推理p95低于20 ms、且原始文本、内容嵌入和稳定标识符不出设备。

Method: 在6020叶商业分类法上构建检索路径：使用从韩语句子Transformer蒸馏的静态嵌入表，量化为4位，无推理运行时。在AI-Hub购物评论（22,900条，韩语）上评测，对比教师模型、学生对比学习适配、锚点（叶子名子串）检测及基于排序分数边际的置信度筛选。

Result: 中类top-5为75.0%（基线18.4%）；有锚点83.5%，无锚点45.2%。大196.6倍教师无锚点提升+20.1pp但整体仅+0.1pp；同教师用学生对比对微调后达0.8586，有锚+10.6pp、无锚+20.9pp。拒绝最低置信五分之一可提升至0.8296。制造商型号在源类别固定效应下不显著（-4.0pp，p=0.51），锚点显著（+13.0pp）。载荷2,942,652字节，Tier-0 p95为A14 4.431 ms、A16 3.670 ms、Snapdragon 695平板5.080 ms。监督信号多为合成韩语话语。

Conclusion: 约束带来的准确率损失不均匀，但任何情况下都非零；无锚点时任务自适应无法弥补教师能力缺口，说明受限编码器缺的是容量。昂贵区间可在设备端通过排序分数边际检测并拒识。

Abstract: We study commercial intent inference that runs entirely on the user's device, under three constraints frozen before the work began: the downloaded payload under 3 MiB, Tier-0 inference under 20 ms at p95, and no raw text, content embedding, or stable identifier leaving the device. Under them we build a retrieval path over a 6,020-leaf commercial taxonomy: a static embedding table distilled from a Korean sentence transformer, quantized to 4 bits, no inference runtime.
  Our main result is where that constraint costs accuracy. On real Korean commerce text labelled by others (22,900 AI-Hub shopping reviews), mid-category top-5 on real product names is 75.0% against an 18.4% permutation baseline, but splits on one observable: a query containing some leaf name as a substring scores 83.5%, one containing none 45.2%. A generic 196.6x larger teacher seemed to localize the gap (+20.1 pp without an anchor, +0.1 with). That null was two effects cancelling: the same teacher fine-tuned on the student's own contrastive pairs reaches 0.8586 and beats the pure-encoder student by +10.6 pp with an anchor and +20.9 pp without. The cost is not uniform, but it is not free anywhere; where the anchor is absent, task adaptation buys the teacher nothing, so what the constrained encoder lacks there is capacity. The expensive regime is detectable on-device from the ranker's own score margin: declining the least confident fifth lifts the rest to 0.8296.
  A second axis we first reported, a manufacturer model code, does not survive source-category fixed effects (-4.0 pp, p=0.51); the anchor does (+13.0 pp).
  Payload is 2,942,652 bytes, all three library links measured. Tier-0 p95 is 4.431 and 3.670 ms on two iPhones (A14, A16) and 5.080 ms on a budget Android tablet (Snapdragon 695), all slower than three server CPUs on the same code. Taxonomy supervision is mostly synthetic Korean utterances.

</details>


### [31] [System Attribution in LLM Brand Recommendations: Single Responses Identify the System, Aggregated Brand Profiles Do Not Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00253)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究检验"AI可见性审计"中按系统汇总的品牌推荐画像是否真能描述该系统。对5个部署端点、3类查询的6,475条回答分析发现：单条回答的"表面形式"能以97.84%准确率识别系统，但聚合到模型×领域×条件单元后，十二个行为特征仅66.53%，且跨领域聚合画像失效，说明该类审计设计无法将系统与领域、采集工具分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI可见性审计通常把部署语言模型的品牌推荐汇总为"按系统的画像"，并默认该画像代表系统本身。本文质疑这一前提，检验这种画像究竟是稳定的系统属性，还是被查询领域与采集工具（harness）混淆的产物。

Method: 基于2025年12月至2026年2月采集的6,475条回答（6,324条可分析），来自礼物推荐、企业声誉、品类归属三类查询的5个部署端点。将每条回答截断为首800字符，用字符n-gram分类器（按提示属性交叉验证、30个划分种子）做单条回答的系统归属；再用50个"模型×领域×条件"单元、十二个行为特征做分组交叉验证，并以标签置换作零假设；另有检索增强臂改变采集工具做对照。

Result: 采集存在严重截断：Gemini 3 Flash在品类归属查询中83.1%的回答因1,024 token上限而句中截死。单条分类达97.84%（基线多数类31.5%）；仅长度特征降至24%，24个格式统计达95.79%，遮蔽品牌名与大写词仍为97.72%。留出查询条件加权97.43%、不加权88.0%；但在更换采集工具的检索增强臂中，没有任何Grok回答（0/120）被归为Grok。聚合层面，十二个行为特征仅66.53%（置换零假设均值33.71%、95分位46.0%）；跨领域聚合画像彻底失败，在品类归属单元上训练的森林把全部22个礼物单元判错，对应品牌提及量反转（礼物8.41 vs 0.94；品类3.01 vs 3.91），而单条回答迁移仍有89.92%平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 回答的表面形式（surface form）在所测查询领域间对系统有一定可迁移性，但聚合后的品牌行为不可迁移；因此当前这种未交叉（uncrossed）的审计设计无法把系统效应与查询领域、采集工具区分开，按系统的品牌画像并不能可靠代表该系统。

Abstract: Audits of AI visibility summarise the brand recommendations of deployed language models into per-system profiles. We test whether such a profile describes the system on one corpus of 6,475 stored responses (6,324 analysable) collected between December 2025 and February 2026 from five deployed endpoints across gift-recommendation, corporate-reputation and category-ownership queries. The collection harness cut many answers short: 83.1% of Gemini 3 Flash answers in category ownership end mid-sentence under a 1,024-token output cap. With every answer cut to its first 800 characters, a character n-gram classifier cross-validated by prompt attributes one response to GPT-5.2, Gemini 3 Flash, Gemini 3 Flash with search, Grok or Perplexity sonar-pro with 97.84% accuracy (5,028 responses, 383 prompts, majority class 31.5%, 30 split seeds). Length alone falls to the majority rate, 24 formatting statistics reach 95.79%, and masking brand names and capitalised tokens leaves 97.72%. Held-out query conditions keep 97.43% weighted by size and 88.0% unweighted; in a retrieval-grounded arm that changes the harness, no Grok answer is attributed to Grok (0/120). Aggregated into 50 model-by-domain-by-condition units, twelve behavioural features separate four systems at 66.53% under grouped cross-validation, against a label-permutation null with mean 33.71% and 95th percentile 46.0%. Across domains the aggregate profile fails: a forest trained on category-ownership units assigns all 22 gift units to the wrong system, consistent with a reversal in brand volume (8.41 against 0.94 brands per response in gifts, 3.01 against 3.91 in category ownership), while single responses transfer at 89.92% balanced accuracy. The surface form of an answer carries the system across the query domains tested; aggregated brand behaviour does not, and the uncrossed design cannot separate the system from the domain or the harness.

</details>


### [32] [A Shared Taste for Model-Written Text: The Generator-by-Selector Matrices of "AI-AI Bias" Show No Detectable Own-Model Premium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00369)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 重分析Laurito等（PNAS 2025）的5x5矩阵数据，发现模型对自身生成文本没有可靠的"自恋"偏好（gamma≈0.019，p=0.14），但主结果（模型共享对AI文本的品味）依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文强调"模型偏好AI写的文本"这一主效应，但其交叉设计（同一批模型既当生成器又当选择器）隐含一个未被headline的问题：选择器是否额外偏爱自己模型生成的文本？作者试图回答这个"自恋偏好"问题，并检验其统计功效与可检测下限。

Method: 从作者公开仓库重建三个5x5矩阵（21,828有效试次，逐格与已发表值一致），拟合含own-model项gamma的两向固定效应模型，并用对selector的120种重标记做精确置换检验；分析位置偏差、去除顺序驱动项，并评估检验功效与最小可检测效应；另用Tan等（ACL 2024）的4x4矩阵做验证。

Result: 自模型溢价gamma：产品+0.013（p=0.24）、论文摘要-0.010（p=0.74）、电影+0.054（p=0.07）、合并+0.019（p=0.14，95%区间-0.008至0.046）；GPT-3.5/GPT-4同厂项在三个数据集均为负。位置偏差可使单格移动最多0.42个百分点，去除顺序驱动项后对比不变。对0.05溢价功效为82%/88%/42%/97%（合并），80%功效下最小可检测效应为0.034（合并）。Tan等矩阵给出gamma=+0.148（最小可能p）。

Conclusion: Laurito等的主结论成立：模型共享对模型写作文本的偏好（GPT-4描述被各选择器选中77%-95%）。但数据并未显示模型能识别并偏好自己的文字；该零效应在约0.04份额点以上是有信息量的，低于此则无法判断。

Abstract: Laurito et al. (PNAS 2025) showed that large language models choosing between two descriptions of the same product, paper or film prefer the description written by a language model over the one written by a person, by a wide margin over what human judges do. Their design crosses five generators with the same five models as selectors, which permits a second question the paper does not headline: does a selector prefer text from its own model beyond what the generator and selector main effects predict? We rebuild the three 5x5 matrices from the per-item counts in the authors' public repository (21,828 valid trials; every cell matches the published value) and fit a two-way fixed-effects model with an own-model term gamma, tested by the exact permutation test over the 120 relabellings of the selectors. The premium is +0.013 on products (exact one-sided p = 0.24), -0.010 on paper abstracts (p = 0.74), +0.054 on films (p = 0.07) and +0.019 pooled (p = 0.14; 95% interval -0.008 to 0.046). The same-vendor term for the GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 pair is negative in all three datasets. Position bias moves single cells by up to 0.42 share points in either direction, and the own-model contrast is unchanged once order-driven items are removed. The design would have detected a premium of 0.05 with 82% (products), 88% (papers), 42% (films) and 97% (pooled) power; the minimum detectable effect at 80% power is 0.034 pooled. The absence is informative down to about 0.04 share points and silent below that. The 4x4 matrix of Tan et al. (ACL 2024) gives gamma = +0.148 at the smallest p its 24 relabellings allow, with a same-family term of the same size. The main result of Laurito et al. stands: models share a taste for model-written text, with GPT-4's descriptions chosen 77% to 95% of the time by every selector on products. What these data do not show is a model recognising and favouring its own prose.

</details>


### [33] [Comparison of Common Crawl News & GDELT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00587)
*Ameir El Ouadi,David Beskow*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文比较了全球新闻语料库 GDELT 与 Common Crawl News 两个数据集，从内容与覆盖范围分析各自优势与局限，发现两者在新闻来源地理分布上差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球新闻语料库对自然语言处理、知识图谱、大语言模型等技术任务至关重要，也有助于理解每日互动的人物、地点、组织与事件。因此需要厘清现有主流新闻数据集各自的特点与不足。

Method: 对 GDELT 与 Common Crawl News 两个新闻数据集进行对比分析，考察其内容与覆盖范围。其中 GDELT 依赖全球广播、印刷及网络新闻，Common Crawl 则侧重通过网页爬取收集的全球新闻站点。

Result: 分析揭示了两个数据集在新闻来源采集地区上存在相当大的差异。

Conclusion: 该研究凸显了两个数据集各自的优势与局限，为相关技术任务选择合适新闻语料提供了参考依据。

Abstract: The corpus of worldwide news is important for natural language processing, knowledge graphs, large language models, and other technical efforts. Additionally, this corpus is important for understanding the people, places, organizations, and events that interact in real-time every day. This paper compares two news datasets used for these tasks today, namely the Global Database of Events, Language, and Tone (GDELT) and Common Crawl News. Our research highlights the strengths and limitations of each dataset, analyzing their content and coverage. Notably, while GDELT relies on broadcasts, prints, and web news from across the globe, Common Crawl focuses on news sites from around the world gathered through web crawling. Our analysis revealed considerable differences in where the two datasets gather their news sources.

</details>


### [34] [CANOPY: Adaptive-Granularity Evidence Compression for Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00923)
*Hyojeong Yun,Jueun Kim,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CANOPY是一种面向多模态RAG的自适应粒度检索后证据压缩框架，将检索项表示为层级结构，用微调节点编码器对区域打分，并通过父相对细化在不同粒度上选择多个区域，无需LLM进行节点级剪枝；同时引入批评器在证据不足时触发定向补充检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态RAG中，检索粒度无法决定每个条目内应保留多少上下文：粗粒度会包含无关内容，而统一细粒度选择又可能删除理解证据所需的上下文。现有压缩器依赖模态特定机制，缺少一种能在异构条目间逐区域自适应调整保留范围的通用流程。

Method: CANOPY把检索项表示为层级结构，使用在金标准证据上微调的节点编码器，针对查询为各区域打分；通过父相对细化比较分数，在不同粒度上选择多个区域，且节点级剪枝不调用LLM。由于压缩无法恢复未被检索到的证据，系统使用批评器判断累积证据是否不足，并在需要时请求定向补充检索；新检索项先压缩再加入。

Result: 在覆盖3300万条异构语料的五个问答基准上，CANOPY的平均答案准确率高于所评估的检索基线。消融实验表明，额外检索是多跳问答准确率提升的主要来源。在未路由的Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct设置中，与不含压缩的相同迭代流程相比，压缩将读者输入证据token减少14.2–27.7%，同时答案准确率相当。

Conclusion: CANOPY提供了一种跨异构多模态条目、按区域自适应确定保留范围的事后证据压缩方案，在不依赖LLM进行节点级剪枝的情况下，结合批评器驱动的补充检索，可在保持答案准确率的同时显著降低读者输入证据token，并在多个问答基准上取得优于基线检索方法的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal RAG retrieves text, tables, images, and videos, but choosing a retrieval granularity does not determine how much context to retain within each item. Coarse units include irrelevant content, while uniformly fine selection can remove context needed to interpret the evidence. Existing compressors address this trade-off with modality-specific mechanisms, leaving open a shared procedure for adapting the retained extent region by region across heterogeneous items. We introduce CANOPY (Canonical Projection over Hierarchy), a framework for adaptive-granularity post-retrieval evidence compression. CANOPY represents retrieved items as hierarchies and uses a node encoder fine-tuned on gold evidence to score regions against the query. Parent-relative refinement compares these scores to select multiple regions at different granularities without LLM calls for node-level pruning. Because compression cannot recover evidence that was never retrieved, a critic requests targeted follow-up retrieval when it judges the accumulated evidence insufficient; newly retrieved items are compressed before being added. Across five QA benchmarks over a 33M-item heterogeneous corpus, CANOPY achieves higher average answer accuracy than the evaluated retrieval baselines. Ablations indicate that additional retrieval drives the main accuracy gains on multi-hop QA. In the unrouted Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct setting, compression reduces reader-input evidence tokens by 14.2-27.7% relative to the same iterative pipeline without compression, with comparable answer accuracy.

</details>


### [35] [RPTune: Learned Context Curation for LLM Catalog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00964)
*Chuxuan Hu,Hejie Cui,Norman Huang,Shubham Kumar Bharti,Wang-Chiew Tan,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RPTune 框架，研究面向中小商户的全目录上下文检索，通过"学习式目录编排"与"LLM 后训练"的耦合来提升商品选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对商品目录能装入长上下文窗口的中小商户，直接用完整目录提示可作为大规模多阶段检索的替代方案。但把整个目录塞进上下文并不保证模型能有效利用，因为 LLM 对长上下文的利用并不均匀。因此需要研究如何编排呈现目录，以及如何让 LLM 适应基于编排上下文的商品选择。

Method: 提出 RPTune 端到端框架，将学习式目录编排与 LLM 后训练结合，并使用自动生成的、基于目录的监督信号。其中编码器-重排器式的编排器（encoder-reorganizer curator）在 LLM 下游反馈的引导下对商品进行排序与剪枝；而产生的编排后目录反过来通过上下文相对奖励（context-relative reward）提升 LLM 后训练的效果。

Result: 在 7 个真实商户（覆盖不同零售垂直领域）上评测，每个商户使用 100 条复杂对话式查询。RPTune 在闭源与开源权重的 LLM 上均持续提升搜索准确率：上下文编排带来最多 31.4 个百分点的提升，后训练平均再增加 10.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 对于中小商户，在长上下文提示下结合目录编排与后训练的 RPTune 能有效提升对话式商品搜索准确率，且该方法在多种 LLM 上具有通用性。

Abstract: For small merchant businesses (SMBs) whose catalogs fit within a long-context LLM, full-catalog prompting offers a compelling alternative to multi-stage retrieval designed primarily for large marketplaces with millions of items. However, fitting the full catalog into the context window does not ensure that the model can use it effectively, since LLMs do not exploit long contexts uniformly. We therefore study in-context catalog search through two complementary questions: (1) how to curate and present catalogs to the LLM, and (2) how to adapt the LLM for product selection on curated contexts.
  We propose RPTune, an end-to-end framework that couples learned catalog curation with LLM post-training using automatically generated, catalog-grounded supervision. An encoder-reorganizer curator orders and prunes products guided by downstream LLM feedback, while the resulting curated catalogs in turn improve the effectiveness of LLM post-training with a context-relative reward. We evaluate RPTune on 7 real merchants spanning distinct retail verticals, using 100 complex conversational queries per merchant. RPTune consistently improves search accuracy across both proprietary and open-weight LLMs, with context curation yielding gains of up to 31.4 percentage points and post-training adding a further 10.3 points on average.

</details>


### [36] [Do Multilingual Encoders Produce Language-Consistent Semantic IDs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01139)
*Abhinav Bohra,Anuj Bohra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文检验多语言编码器能否让同一商品的不同语言版本获得语言一致的语义ID（SID），发现翻译在SID空间中与英文源明显分离，且多语言或语言均衡的量化器拟合并不能保证跨语言SID一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索用离散语义ID压缩商品嵌入，但实际商品常以多语言形式出现。作者关注一个尚未被验证的问题：多语言编码器是否足以让同一商品的不同语言表述映射到语言一致的SID，以及残差量化是否对翻译带来的嵌入偏移异常敏感。

Method: 使用Amazon ESCI商品数据，将同一商品渲染为英语、西班牙语和日语三种版本。基于多语言E5嵌入，比较翻译与其英文源的距离、测试残差量化对翻译偏移的敏感性，并对比多语言与语言均衡两种量化器拟合方式下的SID一致性，同时设置距离匹配的商品导向对照。

Result: 多语言E5下翻译与英文源存在可测距离：在英文占主导的拟合中，日语翻译仅7.7%保留英文对应的首个SID码，而英文改写为89.0%。距离匹配对照与翻译表现出几乎相同的全SID不匹配率，说明量化器并未选择性放大语言方向。均衡拟合使码本利用更均匀，但跨语言前缀一致性反而下降：西班牙语首码一致性从28.3%降至6.6%，而纯英文拟合仍能对67.6%的西班牙语翻译保持一致。

Conclusion: 仅靠多语言暴露和均衡的码本使用并不能保证语言一致的SID；翻译引起的嵌入偏移在量化过程中被保留而非被放大，跨语言SID一致性需要额外的对齐或训练策略。

Abstract: Semantic IDs (SIDs) compress item embeddings into discrete code sequences used in generative retrieval. We ask whether a multilingual encoder is sufficient for different-language renderings of the same product to receive language-consistent SIDs. Using Amazon ESCI listings rendered in English, Spanish, and Japanese, we test whether translations remain close to their English source, whether residual quantization is unusually sensitive to translation-induced movement, and whether multilingual or language-balanced quantizer fitting improves SID agreement. Multilingual E5 places translations measurably apart: under an English-heavy fit, a Japanese translation preserves the first SID code of its English counterpart in only 7.7% of cases, compared with 89.0% for an English rewording. Distance-matched product-directed controls produce nearly the same full-SID mismatch as translation, providing no evidence that the quantizer selectively amplifies language directions. Balancing the fitting mixture makes codebook use more uniform but further reduces cross-lingual prefix agreement: Spanish first-code consistency falls from 28.3% to 6.6%, while an English-only fit preserves it for 67.6% of Spanish translations. These results show that multilingual exposure and balanced codebook use alone do not guarantee language-consistent SIDs.

</details>


### [37] [Learning to structure data from user-generated thematic corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01463)
*Elishay Avram,Oren Glickman,Elad Yom-Tov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种全自动迭代框架，利用LLM从主题语料中发现并抽取领域特定属性schema，无需预定义本体，在5个健康类Reddit社区验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体等主题语料含大量非结构化文本，蕴含可结构化的个人属性、行为与经历等信息。但相关属性往往隐含、依赖领域且事先未知，传统抽取方法难以应对，亟需无需预定义本体的自动化方案。

Method: 基于LLM的全自动迭代框架：归纳候选属性，顺序合并语义重叠属性，并赋予结构类型；据此构建本体并从语料中填充值。框架同时支持使用较小LLM进行值抽取，并评估相对大LLM的精度损失。在5个健康相关Reddit社区上评估。

Result: 发现的属性与人工识别属性一致率达61%，接近独立标注者之间的62%一致率；多数情况下算法在少于10次迭代内收敛到稳定属性集；结构类型分配准确率82%；值抽取相对人工标注F1达0.8。跨4个LLM系列，较小指令微调模型随规模增大在相对高容量参考LLM评估下表现显著提升，支持精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 自动发现的属性可与人工识别属性相媲美，能经济、大规模地构建高质量结构化数据集。去除预定义本体需求后，迭代式模型驱动schema归纳为主题语料挖掘提供了实用且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Thematic corpora, such as social media communities, contain unstructured text describing data that could be made structured. These include, for example, personal attributes, behaviors, and experiences mentioned in social media data. Extracting structured data is challenging as relevant attributes are often implicit, domain-dependent, and unknown in advance. We propose a fully automated, iterative framework for discovering and extracting domain-specific attribute schemas without a predefined ontology. Using large language models (LLMs), the framework induces candidate attributes, sequentially consolidates semantically overlapping attributes, and assigns a structural type. These enable creating an ontology and populating it with values from the corpus. The framework also enables the use of smaller LLMs for value extraction with estimable accuracy loss compared to large LLMs. We evaluate the framework on 5 health-related Reddit communities. Discovered attributes achieved 61% agreement with human-identified attributes, close to the 62% agreement between independent annotators. In most cases, the algorithm converges to a stable attribute set in fewer than 10 iterations. Structural type assignment achieves 82% accuracy, and value extraction reaches an F1 score of 0.8 compared to human annotations. Across four LLM families, smaller instruction-tuned models show statistically significant improvements in extraction performance with model scale when evaluated against a high-capacity reference LLM, supporting informed accuracy-cost trade-offs. These results show that attributes comparable to those identified by humans can be discovered automatically, enabling the creation of high-quality structured datasets economically and at scale. By removing the need for predefined ontologies, iterative model-driven schema induction offers a practical and scalable foundation for mining thematic corpora.

</details>


### [38] [From Rules to Neural Graphs: Scalable Structured Prediction for Patent Prior Art Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01553)
*Nikolai Zenovkin,Sebastian Björkqvist*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经解析器，将依存句法分析中的双仿射注意力（biaffine attention）迁移到专利发明图的预测上，通过局部注意力机制将复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n·w)，可在短序列上训练、在超长文档（>40000 token）上部署，性能超过其教师模型且推理成本降低3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利检索的文档常超过数万token，而多数神经检索方法只能处理截断输入，效果受限；图检索方法虽能缓解该问题，但依赖脆弱的规则解析器来构建发明图。作者希望用神经网络直接、鲁棒地从专利文本预测发明图。

Method: 提出神经解析器（neural parser）：借鉴依存句法分析中的双仿射注意力，直接预测发明图；采用局部双仿射注意力，将成对打分限制在滑动窗口内，复杂度由O(n²)降为O(n·w)；由于局部与全局打分共享权重，模型可在短序列上训练并推广到超长文档而无需重训练；模型从100万份规则解析文档中蒸馏得到。

Result: 该模型在推理成本降低3倍的情况下仍超过其教师模型；在下游Graph Transformer检索系统中，神经图使短查询的引用召回率提升0.5%，在完整文档上提升1.1%。

Conclusion: 神经解析器为专利发明图的构建提供了比规则解析更高效、更鲁棒的替代方案，并能在超长文档上以更低成本取得优于教师模型的检索效果。

Abstract: Patent search requires processing documents routinely exceeding tens of thousands of tokens. Most neural retrieval approaches operate on truncated inputs, limiting their effectiveness. Graph-based retrieval addresses this by representing each patent as a structured invention graph, but constructing these graphs relies on brittle rule-based parsers. We present the neural parser, which adapts biaffine attention from dependency parsing to predict invention graphs directly from patent text. Our local biaffine attention restricts pairwise scoring to a sliding window, reducing complexity from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n \cdot w)$. Since local and global scoring share the same weights, the model trains on short sequences and deploys on documents exceeding 40,000 tokens without retraining. Distilled from 1 million rule-parsed documents, it surpasses its teacher at 3$\times$ lower inference cost: neural graphs improve citation recall by 0.5% on short queries and 1.1% on full documents in a downstream Graph Transformer retrieval system.

</details>


### [39] [AgentWebRec: Compact Evidence Fusion over the Agent Web for Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01705)
*Haoran Qiang,Guannan Liu,Liang Zhang,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentWebRec，将基于 LLM 个人智能体网络（agent web）的推荐问题重构为有限证据预算下的"任务时证据获取与融合"问题，通过渐进式地向用户智能体及其邻居智能体获取并融合分布式证据，在保持智能体记忆本地化的同时提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 个人智能体正成为用户语义的持久载体以及用户与推荐平台之间的中介，在本地维护更丰富的用户知识。随着智能体之间相互交互，传统的"用户—平台"关系演变为"用户—智能体网络—平台"的信息通路，使分布式的用户侧信息能够补充物品侧信息。然而这一新通路对传统推荐提出了挑战：证据分散在彼此不透明的智能体之间，只能通过有界查询获取；其中仅一小部分与当前推荐决策相关；且不同智能体返回的响应在语义上异质。因此需要一种新的推荐范式来应对这些问题。

Method: 作者将智能体网络上的推荐重新表述为有限证据预算下的任务时证据获取与融合问题，即决定"问什么"和"保留什么"，而非从聚合数据中学习。提出 AgentWebRec，一个面向用户—智能体的框架：对每个用户—物品决策渐进式地获取并融合分布式证据，同时保持底层智能体记忆的本地化。具体地，它以平台提供的物品语义和目标用户智能体私有记忆中与任务相关的证据作为每次决策的基础，并在本地证据不足时，有条件地向邻近用户智能体查询互补的偏好模式。通过消融实验验证各证据层的贡献。

Result: 在四个 InstructRec 数据集上的实验表明，AgentWebRec 持续优于基线推荐器；消融实验验证了各证据层带来互补的增益。

Conclusion: AgentWebRec 有效解决了智能体网络中证据分散、相关性低、语义异质的推荐难题，验证了"用户—智能体网络—平台"信息通路下任务时证据获取与融合范式的有效性，为基于 LLM 个人智能体的推荐提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-based personal agents are emerging as persistent carriers of user semantics and intermediaries between users and recommendation platforms, maintaining richer user knowledge locally. As agents interact with one another, the conventional \textit{User--Platform} relation evolves into a \textit{User--Agent Web--Platform} information pathway, enabling distributed user-side information to complement item-side information. This new pathway, however, defies conventional recommendation: evidence is scattered across mutually opaque agents and reachable only through bounded queries, only a small portion of it is relevant to the current recommendation decision, and the responses returned by different agents are semantically heterogeneous. We therefore recast recommendation over the agent web as a \emph{task-time evidence acquisition and fusion} problem under a finite evidence budget by deciding what to ask and what to keep, rather than learning from aggregated data. We propose AgentWebRec, a user-agent-oriented framework that progressively acquires and fuses distributed evidence for each user-item decision while keeping underlying agent memories local. It grounds each decision in platform-provided item semantics and task-relevant evidence from the target user agent's private memory, and conditionally queries neighboring user agents for complementary preference patterns when local evidence is insufficient. Experiments on four InstructRec datasets show that AgentWebRec consistently outperforms baseline recommenders, and ablations verify that the evidence layers contribute complementary gains.

</details>


### [40] [Optimizing Effective Training Time for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02057)
*Mingming Ding,Ruilin Chen,Yuzhen Huang,Hang Qi,Menglu Yu,San Tan,Damian Reeves,Boris Sarana,Kevin Tang,Satendra Gera,Gagan Jain,Sahil Shah,Vishwa Karia,Fuzail Khan,Yashasvi Makin,Edward Z. Yang,Oguz Ulgen,Jia Chen Ren,Laith Sakka,Mayank Garg,Meet Vadakkanchery,Aici Lin,Wei Sun,Mengjiao Zhou,Shuai Yang,Junqing Zhou,Max Leung,Apoorv Purwar,Musharaf Sultan,John Bocharov,Zhenyu Tang,Vivek Trehan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模推荐训练集群中的"生命周期开销"问题，提出ETT%作为运行框架来量化、定位并消除训练全流程中的时间浪费，并给出一系列全栈优化，使集群整体ETT%从约80%提升到90%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐训练任务每天在数千GPU上处理数百亿训练样本，但此前仅50-60%的端到端墙钟时间真正用于在新数据上推进训练。生命周期开销在不知不觉中消耗了加速器算力，因此需要系统性地量化并优化这部分开销。

Method: 以Effective Training Time（ETT%）作为运行框架来instrument丢失的时间、将其归因到各独立负责的基础设施组件，并揭露跨作业重启被重复执行的工作。基于该分析，实施覆盖整个训练栈的优化：trainer初始化阶段的通信消除与流水线重叠；动态形状处理、自动调优剪枝与可复用的PyTorch 2编译缓存；异步checkpointing；独立模型发布；以及降低恢复成本。

Result: 在代表性模型上评估并在训练集群中测量影响：每个benchmark的ETT%均有提升，平均提升15.5%，在最大工作负载上达到85%。部署后，集群范围的ETT%从约80%升至90%以上。

Conclusion: 通过ETT%框架对生命周期开销进行全栈instrument与归因，能够有效指导大规模推荐训练的系统优化，显著提升有效训练时间占比，在单任务和集群层面均取得明显收益。

Abstract: Lifecycle overhead silently consumes accelerator capacity across large-scale recommendation training fleets. Our largest recommendation workloads process tens of billions train- ing examples per day on thousands of GPUs. Before this work, only 50-60% of their end-to-end wall time advanced training on new data. We present a fleet-scale study of this lifecycle overhead and a set of optimizations spanning the full training stack. We use Effective Training Time (ETT%) as an operational framework to instrument lost time, localize it to independently owned infrastructure components, and expose work repeated across job restarts. This analysis guides optimizations like communication elimination and pipeline overlap during trainer initialization; dynamic-shape handling, autotuning pruning, and reusable Py- Torch 2 compilation caches; asynchronous checkpointing; stan- dalone model publishing; and reductions in recovery cost. We evaluate the optimizations on representative models and measure their impacts in our training fleet. ETT% improves on every benchmark, by 15.5% on average, and reaches 85% on our largest workload. Fleet-wide ETT% rose from about 80% to above 90% after deployment.

</details>

### [41] [Pushing CPU Speech Synthesis to the Wall: Extreme Inference Tuning under Serverless Architecture and Billing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00063)
*Pakorn Nathong,Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向按实例计费的Serverless CPU平台的"计费感知"神经TTS服务方案，通过请求粒度的并发推理与可回收实例生命周期，显著降低CPU-秒与GB-秒成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按实例计费的Serverless平台会为warm实例整个生命周期内的CPU和内存收费，因此空闲推理状态会直接产生服务成本。传统运行时以吞吐或延迟为目标，在此场景下不合适：逐请求并行会在并发时引发CPU争用，而warm实例会保留数GB的可计费推理状态与页缓存。因此需要以CPU-秒和GB-秒为优化目标，而非仅关注吞吐或延迟。

Method: 提出两项关键技术：(1) 请求规模并发推理（request-sized concurrent inference），限制单个请求的CPU并行度以缓解并发争用；(2) 可回收实例生命周期（reclaimable instance lifecycle），在空闲期释放推理状态与页缓存内存，同时保留服务进程与编译缓存。

Result: 在Kokoro-82M上，系统达到2.71音频秒/CPU-秒，而ONNX Runtime默认仅为0.89；每音频小时成本从PyTorch的$0.0631降至$0.0153，降低4.1倍。空闲计费内存从8.7 GB降至1.33 GB，恢复至首次出声仅需2.2秒，而PyTorch冷启动为7.7秒。在突发流量下，生命周期回收对于将推理效率转化为更低Serverless成本至关重要。

Conclusion: 在按实例计费的Serverless CPU平台上，通过计费感知的并发控制与实例生命周期管理，可大幅降低TTS服务的CPU-秒与GB-秒成本；尤其在突发流量场景下，回收空闲状态是实现成本收益的关键。

Abstract: Instance-billed serverless platforms charge for CPU and memory over the lifetime of a warm instance, making idle inference state a direct serving cost. We present billing-aware neural text-to-speech (TTS) serving on serverless CPUs, optimizing CPU-seconds and GB-seconds rather than throughput or latency alone. Conventional runtimes are poorly suited to this setting: per-request parallelism causes CPU contention under concurrency, while warm instances retain gigabytes of billable inference and page-cache state.
  We address these costs with request-sized concurrent inference, which bounds per-request CPU parallelism, and a reclaimable instance lifecycle, which releases inference state and page-cache memory after idle periods while retaining the server process and compile cache. On Kokoro-82M, our system achieves 2.71 audio-seconds per CPU-second versus 0.89 with ONNX Runtime defaults and reduces cost per audio-hour from $0.0631 with PyTorch to $0.0153, a 4.1x reduction. Idle billed memory falls from 8.7 GB to 1.33 GB, while restoration reaches first audio in 2.2 s versus 7.7 s for a PyTorch cold start. Under bursty traffic, lifecycle reclamation is essential for translating inference efficiency into lower serverless cost.

</details>


### [42] [MegaFlux: Skew-Resilient MoE Megakernels via Pipelined Expert Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00671)
*Jianzhu Yao,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Vignesh Balaji,Sana Damani,Insu Jang,Pramod Viswanath,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MegaFlux 将专家复制作为运行时决策，并在持久化 MoE 执行中流水化复制通信，在 B200 GPU 上实现前向 1.45×、反向 1.28× 的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE megakernel 融合了专家并行通信与计算，但在固定专家放置下路由偏斜会导致 GPU 负载不均：过载 GPU 决定层延迟，其余 GPU 空闲。复制热点专家可转移负载，但动态副本引入额外开销——副本需接收专家权重，训练时其部分权重梯度还需在专家拥有者处归约。

Method: MegaFlux 将专家复制作为运行时决策，并在持久化 MoE 执行中流水化由复制引起的通信。设备端规划器在每 GPU 副本预算约束下联合选择副本位置并分配 tile 对齐的 token 块，且不改变路由输出。前向与反向 megakernel 实现流水化专家复制：副本在所需权重到达时即开始计算，反向则把副本梯度归约与持续进行的专家计算重叠。方法扩展了 TensorRT-LLM 的 CuTeDSL MegaMoE 前向 kernel，并新增反向 MoE megakernel。

Result: 在八块 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上、每方向 147 种配置下，MegaFlux 相对固定放置的相同 megakernel，前向几何平均加速 1.45×、反向 1.28×，峰值分别达 2.14× 和 2.64×。消融实验中，流水化在前向隐藏了 56–76% 的副本权重传输开销，在反向隐藏 91–100% 的权重传输与副本梯度归约合计开销，相比将这些操作单独执行的相同复制方案，层延迟额外降低最多 13.2% 和 26.7%。集成到 vLLM 用于 DeepSeek-V4-Pro prefill 时，端到端中位加速为 1.13–1.26×。

Conclusion: 将专家复制从固定策略转为运行时决策并流水化其通信，可有效缓解 MoE megakernel 中的路由偏斜导致的 GPU 空转，在保持路由输出不变的前提下显著降低层延迟与端到端延迟。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) megakernels fuse expert-parallel communication with expert computation. However, under fixed expert placement, routing skew creates GPU stragglers: overloaded GPUs determine layer latency while others sit idle. Replicating hot experts can shift work to underloaded GPUs, but dynamic replicas introduce additional work: replicas must receive expert weights to execute and, during training, their partial weight gradients must be reduced at the expert owners. We present MegaFlux, which makes expert replication a runtime decision and pipelines the communication induced by replication within persistent MoE execution. An on-device planner jointly selects replica locations and assigns tile-aligned token blocks under a per-GPU replica budget, leaving router outputs unchanged. The forward and backward megakernels realize pipelined expert replication: replicas begin computation as their required weights arrive, while backward overlaps replica-gradient reduction with ongoing expert computation. MegaFlux extends TensorRT-LLM's CuTeDSL MegaMoE forward kernel and introduces a new backward MoE megakernel. Across 147 configurations per direction on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs, MegaFlux achieves geometric-mean speedups of $1.45\times$ for forward and $1.28\times$ for backward over the same megakernels with fixed placement, peaking at $2.14\times$ and $2.64\times$. In ablations, pipelining hides $56$--$76$% of replica-weight transfer cost in forward and $91$--$100$% of combined weight-transfer and replica-gradient-reduction cost in backward, yielding up to $13.2$% and $26.7$% additional layer-latency reductions over the same replication plans with these operations executed separately. Integrated into vLLM for DeepSeek-V4-Pro prefill, MegaFlux delivers $1.13$--$1.26\times$ median end-to-end speedups over fixed placement.

</details>


### [43] [Leto: Fast In-Place Recovery for LLM Training on Surviving Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00687)
*Geon-Woo Kim,Joon Ha Kim,Daehyeok Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Leto 是一种容错训练系统，利用存活硬件实现原地恢复，避免重载检查点和重算，大幅加速恢复并提升有效训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件可操作性故障会中断大模型训练，但硬件本身无需重置或更换即可恢复。然而现有恢复系统仍需重载检查点、重算丢失进度并重建进程状态，导致本可继续训练的 GPU 处于空闲状态，恢复效率低。

Method: 提出 Leto 系统。核心思想是：恢复训练所需的状态可在活动训练进程之外保留或准备，同时仍处于同一硬件上。Leto 保留工作模型状态和可复用进程状态，并在影子训练器中预初始化其余状态；采用两级纠删保护与块级事务更新，保证所保留模型状态的可恢复性与一致性，并在活动训练需要显存时回收影子状态。

Result: 在 6 卡和 72 卡 NVIDIA A100 集群上，Leto 的恢复速度比最佳检查点基线快 3.6—6.5 倍，有效训练时间最高提升 13.7 个百分点。大规模模拟显示，在 131,072 卡的集群上有效训练时间超过 95%。

Conclusion: Leto 通过原地恢复与状态保留机制，显著减少恢复开销和 GPU 空闲，为大规模 LLM 训练提供了高效的容错方案。

Abstract: Hardware-operable failures (HOFs) interrupt large language model (LLM) training but permit recovery on the same hardware without reset, repair, or replacement. Existing recovery systems nevertheless reload checkpoints, recompute lost progress, and rebuild process state, idling GPUs that could otherwise continue training.
  We present Leto, a fault-tolerant training system that leverages surviving hardware to enable efficient in-place recovery. Our key insight is that the state needed to resume training can be retained or prepared outside the active training process while remaining on the same hardware. Leto retains the working model state and the reusable process state, and preinitializes the remaining state in a shadow trainer. We devise two-tier erasure protection and chunk-level transactional updates to keep the retained model state recoverable and consistent, and reclaim the shadow state when active training needs its GPU memory. Evaluation on 6- and 72-GPU NVIDIA A100 clusters shows that Leto recovers 3.6--6.5$\times$ faster than the best-performing checkpointing baselines and improves productive training time by up to 13.7 percentage points. Large-scale simulation shows over 95% productive training time on a 131,072-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [44] [Ditto: Generalized Reconfigurable Linearizable Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00702)
*Aleksey Panas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架,对现有线性一致读算法进行泛化分类并数学分析其时延,揭示读/写时延与网络方差容忍度的两大权衡,指出算法选择依赖动态条件。作者提出新算法 Pairwise Quorums 弥补现有算法在延迟优化上的空白,并构建可在运行时重配置的 Ditto,统一并优于所有现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性一致性能为分布式数据存储提供顺序执行的假象,但实现代价高昂。由于多数工作负载是读主导的,研究者提出了大量专用读算法以在不牺牲线性一致性的前提下加速读操作。然而没有一个算法在所有部署场景中都是最优的,缺乏统一的理解框架来指导选择。

Method: 作者构建了一个将现有线性一致读算法归入统一泛化空间的分类框架,并对其时延作为该泛化参数的函数进行数学分析;基于分析结果设计新算法 Pairwise Quorums,并构建能在运行时跨空间重配置的 Ditto。

Result: 分析揭示了两个基本权衡:读时延与写时延之间的权衡,以及读与写对网络方差容忍度之间的权衡,二者共同导致最优算法依赖于随时间变化的网络与负载条件。同时发现一个空白:存在一种在读与并发写重叠时最小化延迟且不增加写代价的方法,但现有算法仅能在部分权衡空间中应用。

Conclusion: Pairwise Quorums 弥补了现有算法无法覆盖完整权衡空间的空白,与 Ditto 结合后涵盖了所有现有算法:对每个现有算法,该组合要么严格更优,要么在存在真实权衡处重配置以匹配它。

Abstract: Linearizability gives developers the illusion that operations on a distributed datastore execute sequentially on a single machine. Providing it is expensive, so numerous specialized reads algo- rithms have been proposed to make reads significantly faster without sacrificing linearizability, which matters because most workloads are read-dominant by orders of magnitude. Yet, none of these algorithms is the best choice for every deployment. We show why with a framework that classifies these algorithms into a generalized space together with a mathematical analysis of their latencies as a function of this generalization. The analysis exposes two fundamental tradeoffs, one between read and write latency and one between read and write tolerance to network variance, which together leave the best algorithm dependent on network and workload conditions that vary over time. It also exposes a gap: there is a way to minimize the delay a read incurs when it overlaps a concurrent write, at no cost to writes, yet exist- ing algorithms can apply it only for a subset of the tradeoff space, forcing any deployment that needs the full space to settle for a strictly worse solution. We close this gap with a new algorithm, Pairwise Quorums, and build a second, Ditto, that reconfigures across the space at runtime as conditions change. Together they subsume every existing algorithm under our model: against each, the combination is either strictly better, or reconfigures to match it where a genuine tradeoff applies.

</details>


### [45] [MANTA: Machine Learning Augmented Tiering Advisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00714)
*Johannes Freischuetz,Kiet Pham,Sujay Yadalam,Konstantinos Kanellis,Michael Swift,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文针对内存分层中启发式策略难以适应动态热集的问题，提出可扩展离线优化器ChOMP，并据此设计基于学习的在线策略MANTA，在多类工作负载上取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存分层（DRAM与CXL等慢速层结合）依赖将有用页面保留在快速层，但现有启发式策略在分阶段或突发工作负载中难以跟上热集变化，性能受限。

Method: 提出可扩展离线优化器ChOMP以最小化放置与带宽敏感的迁移成本；构建基于该参考的trace驱动模拟器以识别在线策略的性能机会；据此提出MANTA，从运行时访问特征预测页面未来有用性，并将轻量学习模型集成到ARMS中。

Result: 在模拟CXL的八个工作负载上，4GB快速内存下MANTA相对ARMS在Linux 6.2和6.18上分别取得1.12倍和1.08倍的几何平均加速；在六个Optane工作负载上达1.69倍。单工作负载最大加速分别达1.25倍、1.21倍和5.6倍。

Conclusion: 利用离线优化参考可揭示在线策略的性能空间，基于学习的页面有用性预测能有效提升内存分层性能，优于启发式策略ARMS。

Abstract: Memory tiering has been used to expand memory capacity, particularly in datacenters, by combining fast DRAM with slower tiers, including CXL-attached memory. Its effectiveness depends on keeping useful pages in the fast tier, but existing heuristic policies can lag behind changing hot sets in phased or bursty workloads. To explore these limitations, we introduce ChOMP, a scalable offline optimizer that minimizes placement and bandwidth-sensitive migration costs. We then develop a trace-driven simulator that uses this reference to identify performance opportunities for online policies. Motivated by these results, MANTA predicts future page usefulness from runtime access features and integrates a lightweight learned model into ARMS. Across eight workloads on emulated CXL, MANTA achieves geometric-mean speedups over ARMS of 1.12$\times$ and 1.08$\times$ at 4~GB of fast memory on Linux 6.2 and 6.18, respectively; across six Optane workloads, it achieves 1.69$\times$. On individual workloads, MANTA is up to 1.25$\times$ faster than ARMS with emulated CXL on Linux 6.2, 1.21$\times$ on Linux 6.18, and 5.6$\times$ with Optane.

</details>


### [46] [Seamless Reconfiguration for DAG-BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00896)
*Michael Yiqing Hu,Leander Jehl,Paul Franke-Bergmann,Jialin Li,Christian Berger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在全异步网络下为基于DAG的拜占庭原子广播提供无缝重配置的协议，允许系统在成员变更与法定阈值调整期间持续运行，无需暂停。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DAG的BFT协议普遍假设进程集合静态不变，而实际部署系统需动态增删节点。已有重配置方案要么暂停系统，要么将成员变更延迟到预定的纪元边界，可能造成停机且不实用。无缝重配置难点在于：对何时启用新配置的共识可能产生一个已被并发异步传播超越的逻辑轮次号。

Method: 在异步DAG-Rider式抽象基础上设计重配置协议：进程持续可靠广播区块构建DAG，并本地使用随机完美硬币推导全序；协议允许DAG在成员集合与相应法定阈值变化的同时继续增长，不中断系统。

Result: 实现了在成员变更期间DAG持续增长且系统不暂停的无缝重配置，并声称是首个面向全异步网络下基于DAG拜占庭原子广播的无缝重配置协议。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了基于DAG的异步BFT在重配置方面的空白，为实际系统中动态成员管理提供了无需停机的解决方案。

Abstract: Byzantine Atomic Broadcast in Asynchronous networks has been studied extensively for decades. The FLP impossibility result rules out deterministic consensus in a fully asynchronous setting, motivating randomized protocols that combine reliable broadcast with private coins to achieve termination with probability one.
  More recently, DAG-Rider popularized a new abstraction in which processes continuously reliably broadcast blocks to construct a directed acyclic graph (DAG) and then locally derive a total order using a randomized perfect coin. This separation of dissemination from ordering has renewed interest in asynchronous DAG-based Byzantine fault tolerance and inspired numerous protocols that improve expected latency or throughput.
  Reconfiguration, however, remains largely unexplored. Existing DAG-based BFT protocols generally assume a static set of processes, whereas deployed systems must occasionally add or remove nodes in response to operational demands. Existing solutions are not seamless; they either pause the system or delay membership changes until a predetermined epoch boundary. This may be impractical and can introduce downtime. However, a seamless solution is non-trivial; agreement on when to start utilizing a new configuration may yield a logical round number that was already surpassed by the concurrent asynchronous dissemination.
  In this work, we present a seamless reconfiguration protocol for asynchronous DAG-based Byzantine atomic broadcast. Our protocol allows the DAG to continue growing while the membership and corresponding quorum thresholds change, without halting the system. To the best of our knowledge, ours is the first protocol to provide seamless reconfiguration for DAG-based Byzantine atomic broadcast in a fully asynchronous network.

</details>


### [47] [The Other Half of Workflow Portability: Evidence-Backed HPC Site Profiles with Agentic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00971)
*Md Saiful Islam,Douglas Thain*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出"HPC site profile"（HPC 站点画像）：一种结构化、有证据支撑的文档，描述某 HPC 站点的使用规则（资源形态、存储配置、网络权限、运行策略）。通过三步自动构建（测量登录节点、用有界语言模型智能体从文档抽取字段、提交试点作业解决未决字段），并以规则而非模型决定字段去留。在 Purdue Anvil、TACC Stampede3、Notre Dame CRC 构建画像，并给出真实工作流预检案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流可移植方案（包管理器、容器、workflow specification/backpack）只解决了"工作流需要什么"这一半问题，没有描述"某个 HPC 站点必须如何被使用"，导致即便是可移植的工作流在每个新站点仍需人工调整。站点的资源形态、存储配置、网络权限与运行策略往往散落在批处理系统、文档散文描述甚至路由器配置中，自动化部署工具难以将其转化为可用的部署决策。

Method: 提出 HPC site profile（HPC 站点画像）。自动构建分三步，对应信息所在位置：1）测量登录节点；2）用有界语言模型智能体从文档中抽取带类型的字段；3）对符合条件的未决字段提交试点作业（pilot jobs）。每个字段都需与其证据核验，否则被丢弃，由规则而非模型决定哪些字段进入画像。画像随后用于将工作流预检（preflight）为执行计划，或给出早期且可解释的失败。

Result: 在 Purdue Anvil、TACC Stampede3 与 Notre Dame CRC 三个站点构建了站点画像，并展示了一个真实工作流的预检案例研究。

Conclusion: HPC 站点画像将站点知识转化为可执行、可验证的部署依据，弥补了工作流可移植性中"站点如何被使用"这一缺失的一半，使工作流能够在缺乏人工试错的情况下完成跨站点部署规划，并能在无法部署时给出可解释的早期失败。

Abstract: Moving a workflow developed and tested at one HPC site to another rarely succeeds without some amount of trial and error. Package managers rebuild software environments, containers ship whole filesystems, and workflow specifications such as backpacks package a workflow with its software, data, and resource requirements. These approaches address one half of workflow portability: what a workflow needs. But none describes how a given HPC site must be used, and that missing half is why even a portable workflow requires manual adjustment at each new site. That gap includes the site's resource shape, storage configuration, network permissions, and operating policies. This information may be explicit in the batch system, hidden in the prose of documentation, or buried deep within a router's configuration, making it difficult for an automated deployment tool to turn site knowledge into useful deployment decisions. We propose the HPC site profile, a structured, evidence-backed document that makes this knowledge actionable. We automatically construct it in three steps that mirror where the information lives: measuring the login node, extracting typed fields from documentation with a bounded language-model agent, and submitting pilot jobs for eligible unresolved fields. Every field is verified against its evidence or discarded, so a rule, not the model, decides what enters the profile. The profile then preflights a workflow into an execution plan or an early, explainable failure. We build profiles at Purdue Anvil, TACC Stampede3, and Notre Dame CRC and present a case study of preflighting a real workflow.

</details>


### [48] [Serving a Revisable World: Versioned Execution for Interruptible Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01160)
*Yanxin Zhang,Rahul Sharma,Nitin Vegesna,Zheyu Fu,Chang Liu,Trivikram Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 Retire 服务控制平面，以"版本化执行"统一处理 LLM agent 任务修订，实现快速作废与选择性保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 在用户改指令、工具失败或计划变化时需要修订任务，但当前服务器只能通过"中止旧请求+提交新请求"来表达修订。这种方式需分别处理旧执行的缓冲输出、未完成工作和已完成 KV 状态，容易导致过期效果仍可发布，同时迫使后继任务重建本可复用的有效状态。

Method: Retire 围绕"版本化执行"重新设计服务控制平面：请求拥有调度与内存资源，执行版本拥有"权限"（发布输出或为当前执行安装状态的许可）。流程为：先撤销过期工作，再限定其剩余执行范围，并认证后继可继承的已完成前缀；后继从该状态运行，被隔离的旧资源异步回收，从而在同一版本转换中统一快速失效与选择性保留。已在 vLLM 中实现，覆盖输出发布、GPU 执行、KV 交接、分层恢复以及分布式与多租户服务。

Result: 正确性实验验证了各路径下当前版本输出的正确性和有效状态继承。受控配对实验中，将失效与继承结合使修订到后继的首次令牌时间中位数降低 17.1%。对记录的编码 agent 中断到达进行回放，未产生过期输出，且每个最终版本在多次修订中持续推进。

Conclusion: Retire 将"中止并重启"转变为协调式交接：既能快速停止过期工作，又能为后继保留有用的工作成果。

Abstract: LLM agents revise running tasks when users change instructions, tools fail, or new information changes a plan. Today's servers express a revision as aborting old requests and submitting replacements. Yet the old execution's buffered output and outstanding work must stop affecting the application, while completed KV state may still be useful to its replacement. Handling these obligations separately can leave obsolete effects publishable and force the successor to rebuild valid state.
  We present \retire, a serving control-plane redesign around \emph{versioned execution}. Requests own scheduling and memory resources; execution versions own \emph{authority}, the permission to publish output or install state for the current execution. \retire first revokes obsolete work, then bounds its remaining execution and certifies the completed prefix its successor can inherit. The successor runs from that state while isolated old resources are reclaimed asynchronously. This unifies fast invalidation and selective preservation in one version transition.
  We implement \retire in vLLM across output publication, GPU execution, KV handoff, tiered recovery, and distributed and multi-tenant serving. Correctness experiments verify current-version output and valid state inheritance across these paths. Combining invalidation with inheritance reduces revision-to-successor time-to-first-token by a median 17.1\% in controlled paired experiments. A replay of recorded coding-agent interruption arrivals emits no obsolete output and keeps every final version progressing through repeated revisions. \retire turns abort-and-restart into a coordinated handoff that stops obsolete work quickly and preserves useful work for its successor.

</details>


### [49] [RapidMoE: Exploiting Cross-Asymmetry via Adaptive Residual Offloading for Large-Scale MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01265)
*Wenxun Wang,Likai Ma,Zongle Huang,Chen Tang,Yongpan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RapidMoE，一种利用"交叉不对称性"的残差卸载系统，通过位级卸载框架与统一多级重要性仲裁，实现大规模MoE高效异构推理，解码加速最高3.5倍、预填充2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE广泛应用带来异构平台部署需求，但大规模MoE的算法需求与硬件特性存在根本性错配。现有CPU-GPU混合推理系统要么在将专家加载到GPU时遭遇PCIe带宽瓶颈，要么过度依赖CPU计算，导致资源利用率低，且随参数规模扩大必然违反固定延迟预算。

Method: 提出并利用"交叉不对称性"——MoE路由的算法负载倾斜与异构硬件物理差异之间的结构对齐。设计RapidMoE残差卸载系统，采用残差拆分框架，将卸载范式从专家级转向位级，涵盖三个维度：数据表示（紧凑解耦存储）、路由策略（按硬件能力划分为双路径计算）、执行并行（跨设备均衡存储-计算负载调度）。并引入统一多级重要性仲裁，在运行时自适应调整关键专家集合。

Result: 实验结果显示，RapidMoE相比最先进的卸载系统，解码阶段最高实现3.5倍加速，预填充阶段最高实现2.1倍加速。

Conclusion: RapidMoE通过利用内在的交叉不对称性，从根本上打破了算法与硬件的错配，在保证精度-延迟帕累托前沿的同时显著提升大规模MoE异构推理效率。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has created a growing need for deployment on heterogeneous platforms. However, it exposes a fundamental mismatch between the algorithmic demands of large-scale MoE and the disparate characteristics of hardware.Existing CPU-GPU hybrid inference systems fail to resolve this as they either encounter PCIe bandwidth bottlenecks when loading experts to GPUs, or rely heavily on CPU computation. Consequently, this leads to low resource utilization and inevitable violations of fixed latency budgets as parameters scale. In this paper, we identify and exploit Cross-Asymmetry--a structural alignment between the algorithmic workload skew of MoE routing and the physical disparity of heterogeneous hardware. To this end, we introduce RapidMoE, a residual offloading system for efficient large-scale MoE inference. We propose how RapidMoE leverages a residual-split framework to enable offloading paradigm shift from expert-level to bit-level, which unfolds across three key dimensions: (1) data representation, enabling compact and decoupled storage; (2) routing strategy, partitioning computation into dual paths aligned with hardware capabilities; (3) execution parallelism, scheduling a balanced storage-compute workload across devices. We further employ a novel Unified Multi-Level Importance Arbitration to adaptively adjust the critical expert set at runtime, ensuring the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier. These innovations exploit inherent cross-asymmetry, fundamentally breaking the algorithm-hardware misalignment. Experimental results show that RapidMoE achieves up to 3.5x speedup in decoding and 2.1x speedup in prefill compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) offloading systems.

</details>


### [50] [Blockchain Lifecycle Prediction - Dead Coins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01379)
*Uwe A. Kuehn,Syed Muhammad Adnan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文利用公开市场数据与LSTM深度学习模型预测加密货币资产的"死亡"（停止交易），在82个资产样本上取得ROC AUC 0.98的最优结果，但模型在未见数据上性能降至0.59–0.65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2021年以来超52%的代币在2025年初前停止交易，加密货币失败现象规模巨大，但相关预测建模研究不足，受限于定义模糊、数据稀缺及缺乏细粒度生命周期框架。

Method: 基于Coin Metrics的2020至2026年数据，选取82个加密资产（41活跃、41死亡）的90天每日参考价格与估计市值序列，训练LSTM循环神经网络；采用严格按时间顺序的train-test划分以避免前视偏差；并最终应用于未见数据检验泛化能力。

Result: 模型在留出测试集上最优ROC AUC达0.98；应用于未见数据时ROC AUC降至0.59–0.65；诊断分析显示失败资产在停止活跃前数月表现为价值渐进流失与波动率升高，而非突发性崩塌。

Conclusion: 价格与市值的时间模式本身即包含足够判别信号，可识别趋向经济不活跃的资产；同时本文记录了在当前数据条件下多阶段生命周期模型的实际不可行性，为转向二分类方法提供依据。

Abstract: The cryptocurrency ecosystem has experienced extraordinary growth alongside an equally remarkable rate of failure, with over 52 percent of all tokens launched since 2021 ceasing to trade by early 2025. Despite the scale of this phenomenon, predictive modeling of cryptocurrency death remains an underdeveloped area of research, constrained by definitional ambiguity, data scarcity, and the absence of granular lifecycle frameworks. This work investigates whether the failure of cryptocurrency assets can be predicted using publicly available market data and deep learning methods. A Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) recurrent neural network was trained on 90-day sequences of daily reference price and estimated market capitalization for 82 cryptocurrency assets (41 alive and 41 dead), sourced from Coin Metrics over the period 2020 to 2026. The model was evaluated using a strictly chronological train-test split to prevent look-ahead bias. The LSTM classifier achieved in best cases a Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (ROC AUC) of 0.98 on the held-out test set. Finally, the model was applied to unseen data, and it was observed that the ROC AUC decreased between 0.59 and 0.65. The findings demonstrate that temporal patterns in price and market capitalization alone contain sufficient discriminative signal to identify assets on a trajectory towards economic inactivity. Diagnostic analyzes reveal that failing assets exhibit gradual value erosion and elevated volatility in the months preceding inactivity, rather than sudden catastrophic collapse. This work also documents the practical infeasibility of a multi-stage lifecycle model under current data conditions and justifies the transition to a binary classification approach.

</details>


### [51] [GPU-Initiated Communication: Dissecting Down to the Bone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01380)
*Javid Baydamirli,Ismayil Ismayilov,Kaan Oktay,Didem Unat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在GPU-NIC边界系统剖析GPU发起的通信机制，通过自研mini-gda/mini-proxy最小传输与NVSHMEM、NCCL GIN、DeepEP等库对比，分离硬件机制开销与库开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU发起的通信支撑NVSHMEM、NCCL GIN、DeepEP等库，服务MoE模型的细粒度、延迟敏感通信，但其性能特征与优化在源码之外鲜有记录，且库间对比无法区分硬件机制成本与库自身成本。

Method: 详述GPU侧网络路径（队列放置、工作请求构造、doorbell排序、完成语义）；构建GPU与CPU代理提交路径的最小传输mini-gda和mini-proxy，并在H100、H200、B200、GB200上，与NVSHMEM IBGDA、NCCL GIN、DeepEP、UCCL-EP、MSCCL++及fabric-lib一同测量。

Result: 最小GPU路径发起耗时0.7 μs、完成4.0 μs；库因队列管理、内存排序与完成范围额外增加至多4.6 μs发起时间，且发起时间随SM时钟变化。调优的CPU代理在空闲时可匹敌或优于GPU路径，代价是占用专用核。与批量流量共享队列会使延迟升高1~3个数量级。达到InfiniBand平台260 M msg/s上限需doorbell批处理与队列并行，二者均有资源代价：通信代码即使未使用也会降低GPU块驻留；约3000活跃连接时全对全流量损失59% NIC消息率。

Conclusion: 仅凭提交路径无法预测通信性能；实验代码与结果已开源。

Abstract: GPU-initiated communication lets GPU threads post RDMA operations directly to the NIC. It underpins NVSHMEM, NCCL GIN, and DeepEP, which serve the fine-grained, latency-critical communication of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, yet its performance characteristics and optimizations remain scarcely documented beyond source code, and library comparisons fail to separate the costs of the hardware mechanism from those of the library around it.
  This paper dissects GPU-initiated communication at the GPU-NIC boundary. We first detail the GPU-side network path: queue placement, work-request construction, doorbell ordering, and completion semantics. We then introduce mini-gda and mini-proxy, minimal transports for the GPU and CPU-proxy submission paths, and measure them alongside NVSHMEM IBGDA, NCCL GIN, DeepEP, UCCL-EP, MSCCL++, and fabric-lib on NVIDIA H100, H200, B200, and GB200 platforms. A minimal GPU path issues an operation in 0.7 $μ$s and completes in 4.0 $μ$s; libraries add up to 4.6 $μ$s of issue time through queue management, memory ordering, and completion scope, and issue time scales with the SM clock. A tuned CPU proxy matches or beats the GPU path at idle, at the cost of a dedicated core whose operating state sets its latency and throughput. On either path, sharing a queue with bulk traffic raises latency by one to three orders of magnitude. Reaching the 260 M msg/s ceiling of our InfiniBand platform requires doorbell batching and queue parallelism, and both have resource costs: communication code can reduce GPU block residency even when unused, and all-to-all traffic loses 59% of its NIC message rate at about 3,000 active connections. The submission path alone therefore does not predict communication performance. Our experiment code and results are available at https://github.com/ParCoreLab/Dissecting-GPU-Communication-Experiments.

</details>


### [52] [GridSMR: Causal Compression for Sharded Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01443)
*Shir Cohen,Adam Alon,Raz Omessi,Amir Sarid,Dana Shamir,Ofir Zohar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GridSMR 是一种分片区块链系统，通过将原子性本地化到账户级别并以“先执行后共识”方式异步处理跨分片操作，实现因果压缩，使跨分片延迟不随因果深度增长，显著提升吞吐并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分片系统在依赖的跨分片步骤之间放置协调机制，导致延迟随因果深度增长，难以在保证跨分片依赖操作可推进的同时实现水平扩展执行。

Method: GridSMR 将原子性本地化到单个账户，把跨账户工作异步执行；采用 execute-before-agree 架构，依赖操作在可用时跨分片执行，共识随后验证并提交生成的调度，从而实现 Causal Compression。

Result: 单验证者执行扩展到 1.07M requests/s，四验证者执行达到 193K committed requests/s；相比延迟执行，16 跳因果链延迟降低 8.2 倍。

Conclusion: GridSMR 通过账户级原子性与先执行后共识的异步跨分片执行，使跨分片延迟不随因果深度增长，在提升分片区块链吞吐的同时显著降低因果链延迟。

Abstract: We present GridSMR, a sharded blockchain that scales execution horizontally while allowing dependent cross-shard operations to progress within a single block. Existing sharded systems typically place coordination between dependent cross-shard steps, making latency grow with causal depth. GridSMR localizes atomicity to individual accounts and executes cross-account work asynchronously. Using an execute-before-agree architecture, dependent operations execute across shards as they become available, while consensus later validates and commits the resulting schedule. This enables Causal Compression: cross-shard latency need not grow with the causal depth of a computation. GridSMR scales single-validator execution to 1.07M requests/s and four-validator execution to 193K committed requests/s, while reducing 16-hop causal-chain latency by 8.2x versus deferred execution.

</details>


### [53] [Exploiting the Interplay of Compute- and Memory-Bound kernels in MPI Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01587)
*Ayesha Afzal,Krishna Manda,Georg Hager*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在通信稀疏、计算密集与内存密集交替的应用中，通信停顿可通过自然去同步缓解内存带宽争用，接近带宽饱和点时去同步能带来最优加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统并行应用将通信停顿视为性能危害，追求同步锁步执行。但论文指出在通信稀少的应用中，计算密集与内存密集阶段交替时，MPI通信停顿反而可能无意中缓解内存带宽争用。

Method: 以并行光流求解器为案例，将计算密集的光线追踪核与内存密集的光流求解核结合，进程间通信可忽略；并开发可调双核微基准以在受控条件下研究动态行为，最后用带宽感知的模型化模拟器验证。

Result: 程序通过去同步与计算/内存密集阶段的自动重叠获得显著加速；当在ccNUMA域上并发执行内存密集阶段的进程数接近带宽饱和点时达到最优加速。用MPI异步进展减少通信开销反而因过多rank同时争用带宽而显著降低性能。需要显著的应用或系统噪声才能实现完全去同步。

Conclusion: 自然去同步是一种架构感知的优化手段，通信停顿在特定场景下可成为性能收益来源，且需权衡并发内存密集阶段的进程数量以避免带宽过度争用。

Abstract: Parallel applications are often designed for synchronous, lock-step execution, treating communication stalls as performance hazards. Yet, in a communication-light application without frequent synchronization points that alternates between compute-bound memory-bound execution, an MPI communication stall can act as an unintentional relief on memory-bandwidth contention. We demonstrate this using a Parallel Optical Flow Solver, which combines a compute-bound Ray Tracing kernel with a memory-bound Optical Flow Solver kernel and negligible inter-process communication. This program shows considerable speedup via desynchronization and automatic overlap between compute- and memory-bound phases, showing that natural desynchronization is an architecture-aware optimization. An optimal speedup is achieved when the number of processes concurrently executing the memory-bound phase on a ccNUMA domain is near the bandwidth saturation point. We also show a case where reducing communication overhead using MPI asynchronous progress significantly degrades performance because it allows too many ranks to contend for memory bandwidth simultaneously. In order to study the dynamics under more controlled conditions, we develop a tunable dual-kernel microbenchmark, with which we show that significant application or system noise (natural or injected) is required to achieve full desynchronization. Finally, we also validate these results using a bandwidth-aware, model-based simulator.

</details>


### [54] [Towards a Cloud Fog Edge System for Smart Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01647)
*Christophe Cérin,Mamadou Sow,Frédéric Andrès*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出构建以建筑为数据中心的去中心化边缘学习系统，通过Kubernetes式编排框架在低功耗微控制器上实现AI服务的就地学习，兼顾隐私保护、可持续性与资源受限场景下的自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能建筑依赖外部云基础设施，存在数据主权、能效和基础设施受限等问题，尤其在人道主义与欠发达地区，隐私泄露和能源消耗风险突出，亟需可持续且保护隐私的本地化智能方案。

Method: 一方面评估两种新的在线机器学习算法，用于嵌入式系统实现就地学习；另一方面设计基于KOptim与FIWARE组件的云-雾-边架构，并提出二者耦合的方法论，研发轻量级"类Kubernetes"编排框架以在Arduino等微控制器上部署AI服务。

Result: 在低功耗、低成本的微控制器（如Arduino生态）上成功实现AI算法的就地部署与在线学习，并结合真实世界数据对两种在线算法进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: 该工作将智能服务引入资源受限环境，促进脆弱或服务不足社区的自主性、韧性与可持续发展，验证了建筑即数据中心与边缘计算理念的可行性。

Abstract: In this article, we present our vision and recent advancements toward creating a decentralized system capable of learning from real-time data within buildings to support sustainable and privacy-preserving smart environments. Our approach promotes the concept of the building itself as the data center, aligning with the principles of edge computing to safeguard confidentiality and reduce reliance on external cloud infrastructure. This is particularly valuable in humanitarian contexts, where data sovereignty, energy efficiency, and infrastructure constraints are critical. We detail a lightweight, "Kubernetes-like" orchestration framework for deploying AI services within such environments and demonstrate our progress in implementing AI algorithms on low-power, cost-effective microcontrollers such as those in the Arduino ecosystem. By enabling in-situ learning directly on sensors or microcontrollers, our work aims to bring intelligent services to resource-limited settings, fostering autonomy, resilience, and sustainable development in vulnerable or underserved communities. The contributions in this article are related, firstly, to our project "Online Machine Learning Algorithms for Embedded Systems" and the evaluation of two new online algorithms. Secondly, we envision a cloud-fog-edge architecture based on the KOptim and FIWARE components, and we propose a methodology for coupling them. Experimental results of the online algorithms are also presented, showcasing real-world traces.

</details>


### [55] [ePACT: Energy-Performance-Aware Commitment Tracking for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01784)
*You Peng,Youhe Jiang,Chen Wang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ePACT，一个两级控制器，通过调整服务容量和GPU时钟来追踪小时能源承诺，在保持SLO的同时大幅降低偏离成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低LLM服务的能耗并不必然降低部署成本，因为电力采购中实际用电与预设承诺的偏离会产生（可能非对称的）惩罚成本。因此需要一种在满足请求级服务要求的前提下，最小化偏离成本的能源-性能感知调度方法。

Method: 将问题形式化为energy-Performance-Aware Commitment Tracking（ePACT），即最小化偏离成本并受请求级服务需求约束。实现上采用两级控制器：全局规划器根据实测消耗与剩余小时承诺更新区间能源目标；局部决策器预测候选配置的能耗与完成时间，检查截止期错失，按非对称目标偏离成本选择被接纳的配置，并带有服务优先的回退机制。粗到细的动作搜索与服务异步运行。

Result: 通过单小时对比、控制器消融以及整日trace仿真（H20与H200 GPU池）进行评估。24小时仿真中，ePACT相比vLLM将非对称偏离成本降低73.8%和75.7%，同时保持接近vLLM的SLO达成率；平均每小时绝对偏离分别为2.16%和2.31%。

Conclusion: ePACT能够在维持服务性能的同时有效追踪小时能源承诺，显著降低非对称偏离成本，为LLM服务在电力承诺约束下的成本优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reducing LLM serving energy does not by itself guarantee lower deployment cost when electricity procurement exposes operators to unfavorable deviations from preset commitments. We study hourly commitments with positive, potentially asymmetric costs for overuse and underuse, and formulate energy-Performance-Aware Commitment Tracking: minimize deviation costs subject to request-level service requirements. We implement ePACT, a two-level controller that adjusts serving capacity and GPU clocks as requests arrive. A global planner updates interval energy targets from measured consumption and the remaining hourly commitment. A local decision maker predicts candidate configurations' energy and completion times, checks predicted deadline misses, and selects among admitted configurations by asymmetric target-deviation cost, with a service-first fallback. Coarse-to-fine action search runs asynchronously with serving. We evaluate ePACT through single-hour comparisons, controller ablations, and full-day trace simulations for H20 and H200 GPU pools. In the 24-hour simulations, ePACT reduces the asymmetric deviation cost by $73.8\%$ and $75.7\%$ relative to vLLM while retaining near-vLLM SLO attainment. Mean absolute hourly deviations are $2.16\%$ and $2.31\%$, respectively.

</details>


### [56] [MoE-CORE: Coordinated Expert Offloading and Residency for Memory-Constrained MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01950)
*Ke Yang,Yongji Gao,Xushi Li,Kui Luo,Sicheng Zhang,Tianming Zhou,Keyi Liu,Shufang Lu,Aoxuan Chen,Jie Meng,Jingchun Gao,Dan Li,Xinkai You,Dan Li,Zhixiang Xia,Yan Shi,Yang Liu,Yanjia Zeng,Liangjun Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MoE-CORE 通过协同专家卸载与驻留调度，在受限设备内存下显著降低 MoE 推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏专家激活虽可降低 MoE 计算量，但专家权重总量可能超出设备内存。卸载（offloading）使紧凑 AI 设备上的推理可行，却将主机到设备的传输暴露在推理路径中，带来延迟瓶颈。

Method: 系统在 prefill 阶段用交替缓冲区暂存完整专家层；在 decode 阶段结合非均匀分层缓存容量、领域信息初始化、路由历史感知替换和跨层预取。主配置严格执行路由器选中的专家，另设可选的基于分数的替换路径处理合格的低分未命中。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash-W4A8 上，MoE-CORE 平均每输出 token 时间（TPOT）为 38.0–44.8 ms，对比 vLLM Prefetch 的 1268.9–1269.1 ms；在 GLM-5.2-W4A8C8 上分别为 206.6–220.5 ms 与 5941.5–5941.8 ms。在 84-GB NPU 内存上限下，最佳 DeepSeek GSM8K 配置达到 21.5 ms TPOT（近似专家替换 + 深度 2 的 MTP）。

Conclusion: 结果支持在设备内存约束下进行协同的专家驻留与传输调度，可大幅提升内存受限 MoE 推理效率。

Abstract: Sparse expert activation reduces MoE models' computation, yet expert weights can exceed limited device memory. Offloading makes inference feasible on a compact AI appliance but exposes host-to-device transfers to the inference path. We present MoE-CORE, a system that coordinates expert offloading and residency for memory-constrained MoE inference. It stages complete expert layers in alternating buffers during prefill. During decode, it combines nonuniform layer-wise cache capacity, domain-informed initialization, routing-history-aware replacement, and cross-layer prefetching. The main configuration executes router-selected experts exactly; an optional score-based substitution path handles eligible low-score misses. The main comparison uses 1K- and 128-token output caps for MoE-CORE and vLLM Prefetch, respectively. Across five workloads per model, MoE-CORE records a mean time per output token (TPOT) of 38.0-44.8 ms versus 1268.9-1269.1 ms for the evaluated vLLM Prefetch configuration on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-W4A8; the corresponding values on GLM-5.2-W4A8C8 are 206.6-220.5 and 5941.5-5941.8 ms. Under an 84-GB NPU-memory cap, the best measured DeepSeek GSM8K configuration achieves a TPOT of 21.5 ms with approximate expert substitution and multi-token prediction (MTP) at depth 2. These results support coordinated expert residency and transfer scheduling under a device-memory constraint. The code is here.

</details>

### [57] [Reverse Item Response Theory for Sparsity-Robust Ranking in Fragmented Cancer Drug-Response Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00002)
*Jung Min Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"反向IRT"方法，将癌症类型视为具有耐药能力的潜在"被试"、药物视为具有逃逸难度的"题目"，用于药物基因组学药物反应分析，在GDSC2数据上验证了其在缺失数据下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物基因组学药物反应数据存在大量缺失，简单的平均法在碎片化评估下难以稳健地恢复潜在排序，需要一种能同时估计癌症类型耐药性与药物广谱活性的方法。

Method: 引入反向项目反应理论（IRT），将癌症类型建模为具有"耐药能力"的潜在被试、药物建模为具有"逃逸难度"的题目，在共享潜在尺度上估计癌症类型层面的体外耐药性与药物层面的广谱活性。数据为GDSC2数据库中242,036条药物敏感性测量。

Result: 在四种缺失情形下，反向IRT比简单平均更好地恢复全数据潜在排序，在60%缺失下MCAR、癌症偏置和药物偏置稀疏情形中Delta-rho达+0.089至+0.095；留出预测中在五种方法里Brier分数最优；Bootstrap置信区间显示28种癌症类型中有19种耐药/敏感分类稳定；PRISM跨平台重复实验方向一致率为82%，但排序相关性较弱（rho=0.25）。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于碎片化评估下的方法学稳健性，而非提供普适的临床耐药排行榜。

Abstract: We introduce reverse Item Response Theory (IRT) to pharmacogenomic drug-response analysis by treating cancer types as latent "subjects" with resistance ability and drugs as "items" with evasion difficulty. Applied to 242,036 drug sensitivity measurements from the Genomics of Drug Sensitivity in Cancer (GDSC2) database, the model estimates cancer-type-level in-vitro resistance and drug-level broad activity on a shared latent scale. Validation across four missingness regimes demonstrates that reverse IRT better recovers the full-data latent ranking than simple averaging, with advantages of Delta-rho = +0.089 to +0.095 at 60% missingness under MCAR, cancer-biased, and drug-biased sparsity. Held-out prediction confirms IRT achieves the best Brier score among five evaluated methods. Bootstrap confidence intervals show 19 of 28 cancer types have stable resistant/sensitive classifications. Cross-platform PRISM replication shows 82% directional agreement but weak rank-order correlation (rho = 0.25), indicating the contribution is methodological robustness under fragmented evaluation, not a universal clinical resistance leaderboard.

</details>


### [58] [How Far is Adam from Natural Gradient Descent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00004)
*Vihaan Paka-Hegde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Adam 的完整更新规则（含动量）解释为对角经验 Fisher 近似，并用尺度不变的 γ(Δθ) 度量其在四种损失地形上与真实自然梯度下降的几何偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam 是深度学习的标准优化器，但其与自然梯度下降（NGD）之间的几何关系仍存在未解决的问题。

Method: 将 Adam（含动量）建模为受对角截断、经验标签替换和时间滞后约束的对角经验 Fisher 近似；采用尺度不变的 γ(Δθ) 度量，在良态线性回归、病态线性回归、逻辑回归和一个小型非凸神经网络四种损失地形上测量其与真实 NGD 的几何偏差，并比较改进版经验 Fisher（iEF）与标准经验 Fisher（EF）的路径稳定性。

Result: Adam 的几何轨迹依赖具体情境：良态设置下偏差较低，病态条件下显著升高，在神经网络中错位可达约 10^3；更高的几何漂移对应更慢的初期优化，但不损害最终目标最小化，Adam 始终能达到较低损失。此外，iEF 比标准 EF 轨迹更稳定，后者常出现振荡或发散。

Conclusion: Adam 的实际优化能力可能来自结构近似误差与动量平滑之间的平衡，而非对自然梯度路径的紧密跟踪。

Abstract: Adam is the standard optimizer in deep learning, yet its geometric relationship to natural gradient descent (NGD) contains unresolved questions. We study Adam's full update rule, including momentum, as a diagonal empirical Fisher approximation subject to diagonal truncation, empirical label substitution, and temporal lag. Using the scale-invariant $γ(Δθ)$ metric, we measure Adam's geometric deviation from true NGD across four loss landscapes: well-conditioned linear regression, ill-conditioned linear regression, logistic regression, and a non-convex small neural network. Adam's geometric trajectory is context-dependent. Deviation remains low in well-conditioned settings but rises significantly under ill-conditioning, reaching misalignments of $\approx 10^3$ in the neural network. Higher geometric drift correlates with slower initial optimization but does not degrade final objective minimization; Adam consistently reaches low loss. Furthermore, the improved empirical Fisher (iEF) tracks more stable paths than the standard empirical Fisher (EF), which frequently oscillates or diverges. Our results suggest Adam's practical optimization power may stem from a balance of structural approximation errors and momentum smoothing rather than close tracking of the natural gradient path.

</details>


### [59] [FourierQK: Filter Shape, Admissibility and the Leakage-Coverage Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00009)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过受控消融实验研究频域注意力中滤波器的性质，发现直流/奈奎斯特分量有害、最优单尺度带宽σ≈2 bins（段落尺度），可容许零均值滤波器优于高斯，窄带滤波器无泄漏，因果时域方案无法超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频域坍缩注意力（Frequency-collapse attention）用带通滤波内积替代标准Q/K点积获得了显著提升，但"哪种滤波器形状最好、为什么"这一问题尚未解答。本文旨在系统性检验关于滤波器属性的五个假设。

Method: 在字符级语言建模任务（TinyShakespeare，6层GPT）上开展受控消融，检验五类滤波器属性假设：直流抑制、奈奎斯特抑制、带宽、中心频率、多尺度覆盖。对比可容许滤波器（零均值、墨西哥帽DOG m=2）与非可容许高斯滤波器，并分析双边FFT泄漏与频谱覆盖的关系。

Result: (1) 直流与奈奎斯特分量主动有害（val≈2.0，等价于相位随机化），证实振荡带通结构必不可少；(2) 最优单尺度带宽σ≈2 bins，中心在段落尺度（~70 tokens），较BASE-DOT干净增益Δ=+1.15 nats；(3) 可容许滤波器（DOG m=2）在同尺度下优于高斯，并对双边FFT泄漏有部分保护；(4) 双边FFT泄漏随频谱覆盖单调增加，窄带（gap>+4）干净，宽带（gap<+2）泄漏；(5) 字符尺度因果时域Morlet无法超越BASE-DOT（K=128 taps仅覆盖T=256上下文的50%）。

Conclusion: FourierQK在双向注意力场景（编码器式，如BERT）中有效，训练和推理时均可获得完整序列上下文；自回归生成需要因果频谱变体如MorletQK（解码器式，如GPT）。

Abstract: Frequency-collapse attention [Zeris, 2026e] achieves large gains over standard dot-product attention by replacing the Q/K dot product with a bandpass-filtered inner product at a learned frequency. A natural follow-up question is: which filter shape works best, and why? We test five hypotheses about filter properties -- DC suppression, Nyquist suppression, bandwidth, centre frequency, and multi-scale coverage -- using a controlled ablation on character-level language modelling (TinyShakespeare, 6-layer GPT). Our main findings are: (1) DC and Nyquist components are actively harmful (val ~= 2.0, equivalent to phase randomisation), confirming that oscillatory bandpass structure is essential, not just any low-dimensional spectral summary; (2) the optimal single-scale bandwidth is sigma ~= 2 bins centred at paragraph scale (~70 tokens), giving a clean gain of Delta = +1.15 nats over BASE-DOT; (3) admissible filters (zero-mean, Mexican Hat DOG m = 2) outperform non-admissible Gaussians at the same scale and provide partial protection against bilateral FFT leakage; (4) bilateral FFT leakage scales monotonically with spectral coverage -- narrowband filters (gap > +4) are clean, wideband filters (gap < +2) are leaky; and (5) causal time-domain Morlet at character scale cannot beat BASE-DOT (K=128 taps covers 50% of T=256 context), motivating word-level experiments in the companion MorletQK paper [Zeris, 2026f]. Together, findings (1)-(5) characterise FourierQK as effective in bidirectional attention settings (encoder-style, e.g. BERT), where full-sequence context is available at both training and inference time; autoregressive generation requires a causal spectral variant such as MorletQK [Zeris, 2026f] (decoder-style, e.g. GPT). Code available at: https://github.com/AthanasiosZeris/energy-gated-attention

</details>


### [60] [Integrating Fairness and Explainability in a Multiple Instance Reinforcement Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00035)
*Bente Hinkenhuis,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个结合强化学习多示例学习（RL-MIL）、对抗去偏与偏好条件超网络的多目标框架，用于学生风险预测，发现超网络出现模式崩溃，无法通过偏好权重连续控制公平性与性能的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育交互数据预测学生表现需要既准确又具有足够透明度的模型，以支持有意义的干预；同时人口统计信息会带来不公平预测的额外风险，因此需要兼顾公平性与可解释性的多目标方法。

Method: 提出多目标框架：以多示例学习将每位学生表示为弱标注交互的包，用强化学习智能体选择信息量大的实例供下游分类；引入对抗去偏处理人口统计偏差；并评估两种超网络变体，检验用户定义的偏好标量能否连续控制预测性能与均等赔率（Equalized Odds）之间的权衡。

Result: 基础RL-MIL基线取得较强分类性能，但两种超网络扩展均出现模式崩溃：改变偏好权重几乎不能使模型沿预期的公平性-性能前沿系统性移动。失败原因与目标支配、条件机制中梯度传播微弱以及动态生成参数间的相互作用有关。

Conclusion: 公平目标可以嵌入可解释的RL-MIL流程，但仅靠偏好条件化并不能保证可控的多目标行为；稳健的公平RL-MIL需要显式的梯度平衡、目标分离与稳定性分析机制。

Abstract: Predicting student performance from educational interaction data requires models that are both accurate and sufficiently transparent to support meaningful intervention, while demographic information introduces an additional risk of unfair predictions. This study investigates a multi-objective framework that combines reinforcement learning-based multiple instance learning (RL-MIL), adversarial debiasing, and preference-conditioned hypernetworks for student-at-risk prediction. MIL represents each student as a bag of weakly labeled interactions, while an RL agent selects informative instances for downstream classification. Two hypernetwork variants are evaluated to determine whether a user-defined preference scalar can continuously control the trade-off between predictive performance and Equalized Odds. The underlying RL-MIL baseline achieves strong classification performance, but both hypernetwork extensions exhibit mode collapse: changing the preference weight produces little systematic movement along the intended fairness-performance frontier. The failure is associated with objective dominance, weak gradient propagation through the conditioning mechanism, and interactions between dynamically generated parameters. The results show that fairness objectives can be incorporated into an interpretable RL-MIL pipeline, but preference conditioning alone does not guarantee controllable multi-objective behavior. Robust fair RL-MIL therefore requires explicit mechanisms for gradient balancing, objective separation, and stability analysis.

</details>


### [61] [Fast Polynomial Transcendentals for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00049)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文测试短多项式程序能否在LLM中加速其他SFU运算。将sigmoid、tanh、SiLU替换为3/4次BF16多项式程序，在GB200四项任务中隔离路径提速1.19–2.19x（L2），训练步吞吐提升最高8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU代际对矩阵、特殊函数、内存管线的扩展速率不同，导致内核瓶颈随硬件演化而转移。FlashAttention-4在Blackwell上暴露了注意力内部的这种不平衡，作者由此想探究短多项式程序是否能加速LLM中其他SFU运算。

Method: 先在隔离的FP16扫描中对比原生PyTorch与打包融合FMA程序，涵盖L2驻留和HBM驻留工作集；再用3/4次BF16多项式程序替换原生sigmoid、tanh、SiLU，应用于四项GB200集成任务：密集SiLU、tanh-softcapped注意力、sigmoid注意力、路由专家SwiGLU。程序结合解析对称性、目标格式舍入与消费内核内的打包算术。

Result: 隔离路径在L2提升1.19–2.19x，在HBM提升1.00–1.70x。密集SiLU、tanh-softcapped注意力、路由专家替换分别使完整训练步吞吐提升2.7%、2.9%、8.0%；sigmoid注意力替换使完整注意力前向提升7.4%、完整GPU步提升0.3%。同检查点开放权重消融与每任务一次配对预训练对比将评估扩展到模型行为。在约1000亿token的常见训练视界下，四项任务的最终平滑训练损失差异（多项式减原生）为-0.107至+0.079。

Conclusion: 短多项式SFU程序能有效加速LLM中的多种特殊函数运算，带来可观的吞吐提升，同时在约1000亿token视界下训练损失差异保持在较小范围（-0.107至+0.079），表明该替换在性能与模型质量之间具有可行权衡。

Abstract: Graphics processing unit (GPU) generations scale matrix, special-function, and memory pipelines at different rates, so kernel bottlenecks move as hardware evolves. FlashAttention-4 exposed this imbalance inside attention on NVIDIA Blackwell. We test whether short polynomial programs can accelerate other special-function-unit (SFU) operations in large language models (LLMs). We first compare native PyTorch evaluation with packed fused multiply--add (FMA) programs in an isolated IEEE binary16 (FP16) sweep spanning L2-resident and high-bandwidth-memory (HBM)-resident working sets. We then replace native sigmoid, tanh, and sigmoid linear unit (SiLU) with degree-3 or degree-4 bfloat16 (BF16) programs in four GB200 integration tasks: dense SiLU, tanh-softcapped attention, sigmoid attention, and routed-expert Swish-gated linear unit (SwiGLU). The programs combine analytical symmetry, target-format rounding, and packed arithmetic inside consuming kernels. The isolated paths improve by 1.19--2.19x in L2 and 1.00--1.70x in HBM. The dense-SiLU, tanh-softcapped-attention, and routed-expert substitutions improve complete training-step throughput by 2.7\%, 2.9\%, and 8.0\%, respectively. The sigmoid-attention substitution improves complete-attention forward by 7.4\% and the complete GPU step by 0.3\%. Same-checkpoint open-weight ablations and one paired pre-training comparison per task extend the evaluation to model behavior. At common horizons near 100 billion tokens, the final smoothed training-loss differences (polynomial minus native) range from $-0.107$ to $+0.079$ across the four tasks.

</details>


### [62] [SW-KAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Stieltjes-Wigert q-Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00050)
*Amirhosein Azarpour,Seyyed Moein Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SW-KAN，用定义在半无限域上的Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式替代B样条，通过指数-tanh映射桥接域间隙，实现高效准确的多项式KAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式KAN变体虽降低了B样条的计算开销，但存在无界实数输入与正交多项式基的有界/半无限支撑之间的域不匹配问题，该挑战尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出SW-KAN：在(0,∞)半无限域上使用Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式；引入平滑的exponential-of-tanh映射稳定桥接域间隙并保持良态梯度；采用数值稳定的三项递推，在O(N)内完成展开且无需特殊函数调用。

Result: 在图像分类和连续函数逼近任务上取得更优的精度-效率权衡；对数和权重结构及可学习q参数提供独特归纳偏置，在降维特征和有限数据等资源受限条件下表现稳健；优于现有多项式KAN基线，并能以极少参数逼近复杂多元函数。

Conclusion: SW-KAN是资源受限场景下高效函数逼近与分类的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) represent a paradigmatic shift in deep learning by replacing fixed node activations with learnable univariate functions on edges, offering enhanced interpretability and parameter efficiency. While recent polynomial-based KAN variants have addressed the computational overhead of original B-spline implementations, they introduce a fundamental yet underexplored challenge: the domain mismatch between unbounded real-valued inputs and the bounded or semi-infinite support of orthogonal polynomial bases. To address this limitation, we propose the Stieltjes-Wigert Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (SW-KAN), a novel architecture that employs Stieltjes-Wigert q-orthogonal polynomials defined on the semi-infinite domain (0, infinity). We introduce a smooth exponential-of-tanh mapping that stably bridges the domain gap while preserving well-conditioned gradients, and leverage a numerically stable three-term recurrence that evaluates polynomial expansions in O(N) operations without special-function calls.
