Together, controlled simulation, NLP, and vision support the dynamic structural account across settings, with real-model evidence testing its observable predictions under the specified monitors.

</details>


### [234] [Least-time Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01426)
*Alessandro Betti,Marco Gori,Stefano Melacci,Jinwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究梯度流速度调控下风险最小化时间问题，通过正则化变分问题证明了最优速度函数为线性缩放摆线，并揭示其零点附近以2/3指数幂律增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过设定 $\dot w=-u(E(w))\nabla E(w)/|\nabla E(w)|^2$，可让风险 $e(t)=E(w(t))$ 精确满足 $\dot e=-u(e)$，与景观 $E$ 无关，从 $e_0$ 到零风险所需时间为 $\int_0^{e_0} de/u(e)$。仅最小化该时间是不适定的，因此需要引入正则化来研究最优速度控制问题。

Method: 研究正则化问题 $\inf\{\int_0^{e_0}(\frac\lambda2|u'|^2+1/u)de: u\in H^1(0,e_0), u\ge0, u(0)=0\}$，λ>0。证明路线为经典方法：用直接方法证明存在性，用严格凸性证明唯一性，证明极小值在远离原点处为正，并显式积分 Euler–Lagrange 方程。

Result: 证明极小值存在且唯一，为线性缩放的摆线；最优速率在零风险附近表现为 $u^*(e)\sim(9/(2\lambda))^{1/3}e^{2/3}$。指数 2/3 与文献中幂律拟设得到的结果一致，且落在 Hölder 窗口 (1/2,1) 内，此时到达时间为有限且权重速度趋于零。

Conclusion: 该工作为正则化时间最小化问题提供了严格变分分析，明确了最优速度的摆线形式与零点附近的 2/3 幂律行为，并验证了其处于有限时间到达且速度权重消失的 Hölder 区间。

Abstract: Prescribing the speed of gradient flow on the risk itself, by the dynamics $\dot w=-u(E(w))\nabla E(w)/\abs{\nabla E(w)}^{2}$, makes the risk $e(t)=E(w(t))$ obey $\dot e=-u(e)$ exactly, whatever the landscape~$E$; the time needed to reach zero risk from $e_0$ is $\int_0^{e_0}\dd e/u(e)$. Minimizing this time alone is ill posed, and we study the regularized problem $\inf\{\int_0^{e_0}(\tfrac\lambda2\abs{u'}^{2}+1/u)\,\dd e:\ u\in H^{1}(0,e_0),\ u\ge0,\ u(0)=0\}$, $λ>0$. We prove that the minimizer exists, is unique, and is a linearly scaled cycloid, and we show that the optimal rate behaves like $u^{*}(e)\sim(9/(2λ))^{1/3}e^{2/3}$ near zero risk: the exponent $2/3$ is the one found in \cite{betti2026holder} by a power-law ansatz, and it lies in the Hölder window $(\tfrac12,1)$ where the arrival is in finite time with vanishing weight speed. The proof follows the classical route: existence by the direct method, uniqueness by strict convexity, positivity of the minimizer away from the origin, and the explicit integration of the Euler-Lagrange equation.

</details>


### [235] [Distillation of Tabular Foundation Models into Efficient Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01435)
*Minho Jeong,Dooho Lee,Jinmo Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究表格基础模型(TFM)的知识蒸馏，提出有效蒸馏配方：用全部标注训练集作为教师上下文，仅用教师对观测和合成查询的预测来训练学生，在TabArena和TALENT上均取得显著提升，并实现3–21.6倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFM通过上下文学习获得强预测性能，但反复依赖标注数据做条件推理导致推理成本高昂。知识蒸馏可将预测能力迁移到轻量、数据集专用的学生模型以降低成本，然而TFM预测同时依赖标注上下文和查询，由此引出两个设计问题：如何构造教师监督，以及扩大查询覆盖是否有益于蒸馏，现有工作缺乏系统考察。

Method: 在两个TFM、以及神经网络和树模型两类学生上系统考察上述两个设计问题，并据此推导蒸馏配方：以完整标注训练集作为教师上下文，仅在教师对观测查询和合成查询的预测上训练学生。

Result: 在TabArena上，蒸馏学生比其有监督训练并调参集成的对应模型高57–98 Elo分；配方不变地应用于TALENT，在300个数据集中有236–258个超过匹配的默认学生，主指标中位误差降低4.0–6.4%；蒸馏学生相对教师取得3.0–21.6倍的中位推理加速。

Conclusion: 该蒸馏配方在多种TFM和学生架构上通用有效，兼顾预测性能与重复推理成本，提供实用的性能–效率权衡；代码已开源。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong predictive performance through in-context learning, yet repeatedly conditioning on labeled data makes inference expensive. Knowledge distillation can reduce this cost by transferring their predictive ability to lightweight, dataset-specific students. However, the dependence of TFM predictions on both a labeled context and a query introduces two design questions: how to construct teacher supervision and whether expanding query coverage improves distillation. We examine these questions across two TFMs and both neural and tree-based students, and derive an effective distillation recipe. The recipe uses the full labeled training set as teacher context and trains students solely on teacher predictions for observed and synthetic queries. On TabArena, the resulting students outperform their supervised trained tuned-and-ensembled counterparts by 57-98 Elo points. Applied unchanged to TALENT, the same recipe improves matched default students on 236-258 of 300 datasets and reduces median primary error by 4.0-6.4%. The distilled students also achieve median inference speedups of 3.0-21.6 times over their teachers, offering a practical trade-off between predictive performance and repeated inference cost. Code is available at https://github.com/nums-ai/TFM_Distillation .

</details>


### [236] [ibUMAP: Coherent and Scalable Field Evaluation for UMAP Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01445)
*Bin Chen,Yumeng Xue,Patrick Paetzold,Yunhai Wang,Oliver Deussen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ibUMAP，一种基于相干场的UMAP替代方案，用共享嵌入快照同步计算吸引与排斥，避免随机负采样带来的不稳定，并在CPU/GPU上实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP依赖随机负采样进行可扩展布局优化，但采样顺序的随机性会导致多次运行及下游复用时嵌入结果不稳定，因为估计的排斥力依赖采样事件的顺序。

Method: ibUMAP从共享嵌入快照同步评估吸引力和排斥力。其度加权排斥场由固定嵌入下负采样的条件期望推导，用三个标量矩表示，并通过基于插值的FFT方案在CPU和GPU上高效计算，从而避免显式全对计算。该形式产生与标准在线UMAP不同的优化动力学。

Result: 控制实验显示，同步性和核截断会改变局部-全局保真度权衡，而FFT评估对最终质量的平均影响较小。端到端基准测试在CPU上相对umap-learn的中位加速为3.29倍（无种子）和5.79倍（有种子），在百万级数据集无种子GPU执行下相对cuML加速1.44倍。这些收益伴随更好的运行间稳定性和可测量的保真度权衡。

Conclusion: ibUMAP通过相干场与同步更新提升了UMAP的运行间稳定性并实现明显加速，但会带来一定的保真度权衡。

Abstract: UMAP achieves scalable layout optimization through stochastic negative sampling. However, this stochasticity can lead to unstable embeddings across reruns and downstream reuse, as the estimated repulsive forces depend on the ordering of sampling events. We present ibUMAP, a coherent field-based alternative that evaluates attraction and repulsion from a shared embedding snapshot and applies them synchronously. Its degree-weighted repulsive field is motivated by the conditional expectation of negative sampling for a fixed embedding and represented by three scalar moments, which are evaluated efficiently on CPUs and GPUs using an interpolation-based FFT scheme. This formulation avoids explicit all-pairs computations while inducing optimization dynamics that differ from those of standard online UMAP. Controlled experiments show that synchrony and kernel capping alter the local-global fidelity trade-off, whereas FFT evaluation produces small average changes in final quality. End-to-end benchmarks show median speedups of 3.29x unseeded and 5.79x seeded over umap-learn on CPU, and 1.44x over cuML on million-scale datasets under unseeded GPU execution. These gains accompany greater run-to-run stability and measurable fidelity trade-offs.

</details>


### [237] [Repurposing Obsolete Representations for Post-Deployment Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01453)
*Daniel Bethell,Charmaine Barker,Simos Gerasimou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Deep Repurposing (DR)事后框架，在任务淘汰场景下无需梯度更新即可移除失效行为并保留有效任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在长期部署中任务需求可能变化，部分原始输出空间（类别、预测区域或行为）会变得失效。现有方法要么保留失效行为，要么需要昂贵的微调。

Method: DR估计失效区域与保留区域的潜在几何结构，移除支持失效输出的成分，并通过解析修复映射重新分配与保留任务兼容的证据，全程无需梯度更新，从而得到修复后的预测和表示。

Result: 在多种任务设置下，DR移除失效行为同时保留有效效用；在分类基准上，其保留准确率匹配或超过竞争的去学习与编辑基线，消除失效预测，且适应速度比竞争去学习方法快至多60倍。

Conclusion: DR是一种高效的事后适配框架，可在任务淘汰下既消除失效输出又保留有用结构，性能与效率优于现有基线。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are increasingly deployed in long-lived systems, where task requirements may change after training. In such settings, part of the original output space may become obsolete: a class, prediction region, or learned behaviour may no longer be valid. Existing approaches either leave the obsolete behaviour intact or require fine-tuning, which can be expensive. We propose Deep Repurposing (DR), a post-hoc framework for adapting models under task obsolescence. DR estimates the latent geometry of obsolete and retained regions, removes obsolete-supporting components, and reallocates retained-compatible evidence through an analytic repair map without gradient updates. This yields repaired predictions and representations in which obsolete regions no longer act as valid outputs, while useful obsolete structure can support the retained task. Across multiple task settings, DR removes obsolete behaviour while preserving retained utility. More importantly, across classification benchmarks, DR matches or exceeds competing unlearning and editing baselines in retained accuracy, eliminates obsolete predictions, and adapts up to $60\times$ faster than competing unlearning methods.

</details>


### [238] [Streaming algorithms for robust max-min diversification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01456)
*Andrea Pietracaprina,Geppino Pucci,Stefano Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出[Amagata, AAAI23]鲁棒最大最小多样化流算法的三个缺陷，并提出确定性coreset算法，返回恰好k个内点且为(2+ε)-近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: max-min diversification目标对噪声点高度敏感，[Amagata, AAAI23]提出排除z个离群点的鲁棒表述及coreset流算法，但该算法存在coreset构建需离线线性内存、单遍提取可能返回不足k个点（不可行解）、离群点排除保证仅概率性且随coreset缩小而变弱三个缺陷。

Method: 提出确定性coreset算法，在自然的inlier-outlier分离假设下运行；采用单遍流式实现，能自适应数据集的doubling dimension D，对k、z、ε、D的广泛范围使用与n无关的内存；对足够长流，摊还更新时间正比于coreset大小。

Result: 返回恰好k个inliers，构成(2+ε)-近似解（任意ε>0），仅比最佳多项式时间顺序近似高ε，即使无离群点亦然；内存和摊还更新时间对广泛参数范围独立于n。

Conclusion: 该确定性coreset流算法克服了已有工作的可扩展性、可行性和保证强度缺陷，实现内存与更新时间均不依赖n，并提供强确定性近似保证。

Abstract: Given a set of $n$ points $X$ in a metric space and an integer $k$, max-min diversification aims to select $k$ points of $X$ maximizing their minimum pairwise distance. This objective function is however highly vulnerable to noisy points. In[Amagata, AAAI23], a robust formulation is proposed which addresses this vulnerability by excluding solutions containing any of $z$ outliers, defined as the $z$ points in $X$ with the largest nearest-neighbor distances. That paper also presents a coreset-based streaming algorithm for the new formulation, based on a suitable inlier-outlier separation assumption. However, we identify three shortcomings in the algorithm by [Amagata, AAAI23]: its coreset construction requires an offline computation over $X$, which needs memory linear in $n$, in stark contrast with the typical goals of stream processing; the one-pass procedure used to extract the solution from the coreset may return fewer than $k$ points (hence, an unfeasible solution) because it permanently discards points too far from the current solution; and its outlier-exclusion guarantee is only probabilistic and weakens as the coreset size shrinks. In contrast, we present a deterministic coreset-based algorithm that, under a natural inlier-outlier separation assumption (similar to the one used in [Amagata, AAAI23]), returns exactly $k$ inliers which are a $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximate solution, for any $\varepsilon>0$, thus only $\varepsilon$ above the best polynomial-time sequential approximation, even without outliers. Its one-pass streaming implementation adapts obliviously to the dataset's doubling dimension $D$ and, for wide ranges of $k$, $z$, $\varepsilon$, and $D$, it uses memory independent of $n$. For sufficiently long streams, its amortized update time is proportional to the coreset size, thus also independent of $n$.

</details>


### [239] [Tight Transition Time Bounds for Separable Logistic Regression at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01459)
*Haodong Wen,Kaiyue Wen,Jiaye Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大常数步长梯度下降在线性可分数据上的逻辑回归，否证了"任意维度下过渡时间与步长无关"的猜想，证明最坏情况过渡时间为 Θ((logη)^{min{n-2,d-2}})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在二维情形下给出了紧的 Θ(1) 界（当 η→∞ 时），并猜想任意维度 d≥2 下该界与 η 无关。本文旨在检验并否证这一猜想，刻画高维情形下过渡时间对步长的真实依赖关系。

Method: 针对样本对梯度贡献最大的样本可能在迭代中反复变化这一核心难点，作者通过按维度和样本量进行归纳来控 制此类变化，并构造与之匹配的困难实例以得到紧界。

Result: 对任意固定样本量 n≥2 和足够小的间隔 γ，最坏情况过渡时间为 Θ((logη)^{min{n-2,d-2}})，且在 d≥2 上一致成立。

Conclusion: 该结果推翻了此前关于过渡时间与 η 无关的猜想，表明在大常数步长下过渡时间随 η 呈对数幂次增长，其指数由样本量与维度的较小者决定。

Abstract: We study logistic regression on linearly separable data under gradient descent with a large constant stepsize $η$. Such dynamics may exhibit a characteristic Edge of Stability phenomenon, in which the loss initially oscillates before transitioning to a stable phase of monotone decrease. Existing work provides a tight $Θ(1)$ bound in dimension $d=2$ as $η\to \infty$ and conjectures a bound independent of $η$ in arbitrary dimensions $d\geq 2$. In this paper, we disprove this conjecture by showing that, for every fixed sample size $n\geq 2$ and sufficiently small margin $γ$, the worst-case transition time is $$Θ\!\left((\logη)^{\min\{n-2,d-2\}}\right)$$ uniformly over $d\geq2$. The key challenge in establishing a tight bound is that the sample contributing most strongly to the gradient can change repeatedly across iterations. To address this issue, we control such changes by induction on dimension and sample size, and construct matching hard instances.

</details>


### [240] [Let the Heads Talk: Beyond Diagonal Graph Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01494)
*Riccardo Ali,Alessio Borgi,Mario Severino,Alessio Gravina,Davide Bacciu,Pietro Liò,Christopher Irwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过quiver表示分离出Sheaf Neural Networks中的矩阵值传输算子，揭示标准多头注意力本质上是实现对角边映射，并提出Topological Attention (Top-A)，允许边依赖的非对角跨头路由，在多种推理任务上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sheaf Neural Networks用线性传输映射替代标量边权重，但矩阵值传输的作用与整个sheaf-diffusion构造纠缠在一起，难以单独分析其计算作用。作者希望隔离"传输"这一原语，理解它在注意力机制中的真正角色，并探究跨头通信是否能带来标准多头注意力无法实现的计算能力。

Method: 作者通过quiver表示分离传输原语，建立其与多头注意力的直接联系，将注意力头视为局部传输空间的坐标。分析表明标准多头注意力只实现对角边映射（源头只连接到对应接收头）。作者据此提出Top-A：一种多头注意力，学习边依赖的非对角路由，同时保留原有同头路径，并在额外路由消失时精确退化为标准注意力。在关系推理、异构图学习、算法推理（含分布外泛化）及异性节点分类对比设置上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，跨头传输在任务受益于交互依赖变换时最有用，而单纯的异性（heterophily）本身并不带来系统性优势。此外，作者证明该跨头操作一般不能被吸收为聚合后的单一共享线性映射。

Conclusion: 边条件化的跨头通信是矩阵值传输的一个独特计算原语，区别于共享线性变换和纯异性处理，为设计更具表达力的注意力机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Sheaf Neural Networks generalize scalar-weighted message passing by replacing scalar edge weights with linear transport maps between local feature spaces. Yet the role of this matrix-valued transport is entangled with the broader sheaf-diffusion construction. We isolate the transport primitive through quiver representations and establish a direct connection with multi-head attention. Treating attention heads as coordinates of a local transport space reveals that standard multi-head attention implements diagonal edge maps: along each directed interaction, a source head can contribute only to the corresponding receiver head. Allowing off-diagonal entries instead enables edge-conditioned communication across heads before neighborhood aggregation. We show that this operation cannot, in general, be absorbed into a single shared linear map applied after aggregation. Building on this characterization, we introduce Topological Attention (Top-A), a multi-head attention that learns edge-dependent off-diagonal routes while preserving the original same-head paths and exactly recovering vanilla attention when the additional routing vanishes. We evaluate Top-A on relational reasoning, heterogeneous graph learning, and algorithmic reasoning, including out-of-distribution generalization, with heterophilic node classification as a contrast setting. The results show that cross-head transport is most useful when the task benefits from interaction-dependent transformations, while heterophily alone provides no systematic advantage. These findings identify edge-conditioned cross-head communication as a distinct computational primitive of matrix-valued transport.

</details>


### [241] [Learned End-to-End Guidance Schedules for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01502)
*Aneesh Barthakur,Mathias Niepert,Luiz F. O. Chamon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEEGS，通过学习端到端引导调度，在更少采样步数下同时保证生成质量与约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导扩散通过在推理时加入引导函数梯度作为漂移项来满足生成要求，但引导尺度决定数据质量与要求满足之间的权衡。要兼顾两者通常需使用小引导尺度并配合长采样，计算成本高。

Method: LEEGS训练一个时间相关的引导调度：在一小部分样本上以随机梯度下降最小化引导函数；由于穿越引导采样的反向传播代价高昂，采用一种梯度近似，将训练时间缩短约4倍。

Result: 在图像修复、含噪图像逆问题、Face-ID引导生成，以及正问题与逆问题的PDE任务上评估，在相同预算（50或100 NFEs）下优于基线，或以仅10%的步数匹配恒定引导效果。

Conclusion: LEEGS通过可学习的端到端引导调度，在显著减少采样步数与计算成本的同时，实现生成质量与约束满足的更好平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion models are a powerful generative paradigm used across multimedia and scientific applications. Guided diffusion methods impose requirements on the generation by adding the gradient of a differentiable loss (the guidance function) as a drift term during inference. The weight of this drift (the guidance scale) is critical for the trade-off between data quality and requirement satisfaction. To achieve both of these goals, guided diffusion must resort to small guidance scales and lengthy sampling, incurring high computational costs. This work proposes learned end-to-end guidance schedules (LEEGS) to achieve these objectives with fewer sampling steps. LEEGS trains a time-dependent schedule by minimizing the guidance function over a small set of examples using stochastic gradient descent. Backpropagating through guided sampling is computationally expensive, so LEEGS uses an approximation of the gradient that cuts training time by a factor of 4. We evaluate LEEGS on diverse guidance tasks, including (a) image inpainting, (b) noisy image inverse problems, (c) face-ID-guided generation, and (d) forward and inverse PDE problems, outperforming baselines at equal budget (50 or 100 NFEs), or matching constant guidance with only 10% of the steps.

</details>


### [242] [FedMIX-P: Mixing Local and Global Preconditioners for Federated Vision and Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01515)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FedMIX-P，在联邦学习的每个本地训练步骤中混合共享与本地预条件器，将均方算子失配降低 λ² 倍，并在光滑非凸、随机梯度和部分参与下建立 O(R^{-1/2}) 的平稳性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应预条件器能加速模型训练，但客户端几何异构会导致即使在同一模型处评估梯度，联邦更新仍出现偏差；仅在轮次开始时同步无法阻止该失配在本地训练过程中再次出现。

Method: 提出 FedMIX-P，在每个本地步骤混合共享预条件器与本地预条件器，在保留本地自适应的同时降低均方算子失配。采用合适步长和随训练时域变化的混合权重，对光滑非凸目标、随机梯度和部分参与进行分析；理论覆盖有界线性对称正定预条件器。

Result: 建立 O(R^{-1/2}) 的平稳性界，且不要求本地预条件器相互收敛；两客户端反例表明固定正混合可能保留非平稳不动点；在视觉和语言任务上，SOAP、Sophia、Muon 变体相较对应本地优化器均有提升，准确率最高提升 19.47 个百分点，60M–350M 语言模型验证损失更低。

Conclusion: FedMIX-P 通过逐步混合共享与本地预条件器，缓解异构客户端几何带来的联邦更新偏差，并给出理论保证与实证改进；但完整非线性与基于动量的更新仍需单独分析。

Abstract: Adaptive preconditioners accelerate model training, but heterogeneous client geometries can bias federated updates even when gradients are evaluated at the same model. Round-start synchronization alone cannot prevent this mismatch from reappearing during local training. We propose \texttt{FedMIX-P}, which mixes shared and local preconditioners at every local step, retaining local adaptation while reducing mean-squared operator mismatch by a factor of $λ^2$. For smooth nonconvex objectives with stochastic gradients and partial participation, we establish an $O(R^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound using suitable stepsizes and a horizon-dependent mixing weight, without requiring local preconditioners to converge to one another. A two-client counterexample shows that fixed positive mixing can preserve a nonstationary fixed point. The theory covers bounded linear symmetric positive-definite preconditioners. Experiments with SOAP, Sophia, and Muon variants across vision and language tasks show improvements over corresponding local optimizers, including accuracy gains of up to $19.47$ percentage points and lower validation loss for 60M--350M language models. Full nonlinear and momentum-based updates require separate analysis.

</details>


### [243] [Auto-Formalizing Neuro-Symbolic Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01519)
*Samuele Bortolotti,Weixin Chen,Han Zhao,Andrea Passerini,Stefano Teso,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 auto-nesy-bench 基准，研究利用LLM自动形式化文本知识为符号约束，并评估其对神经符号预测器下游精度的影响，发现LLM生成的约束常与专家相似且能带来高质量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号预测器需要将领域知识编码为逻辑公式以满足约束，但这一过程依赖人工与专家，成为该范式的关键瓶颈。作者希望探究LLM自动形式化能否系统地将文本知识转化为可插入神经符号预测器的符号知识。

Method: 引入 auto-nesy-bench 新基准，用于评估约束形式化及其对下游预测精度的影响，并在多个领域对LLM自动形式化进行广泛评测。

Result: LLM能在有意义的程度上形式化约束，生成的公式往往与人类专家提供的相似；当生成的公式语法有效时，可获得高质量的下游预测。

Conclusion: LLM自动形式化可缓解神经符号预测器中符号约束获取的瓶颈，语法有效的公式能支撑高质量预测，代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) predictors incorporate prior knowledge into the prediction process of neural networks, ensuring that outputs satisfy specified constraints, making them particularly suitable for high-stakes applications where compliance with domain knowledge is essential. A key bottleneck in this paradigm is the acquisition of symbolic constraints: encoding domain knowledge into logical formulas remains a manual and expert-intensive process. In this work, we investigate the extent to which auto-formalization via LLMs can systematically translate textual knowledge into symbolic knowledge that can be plugged into NeSy predictors. To this end, we introduce auto-nesy-bench, a new benchmark for evaluating constraint formalization and its impact on downstream accuracy of NeSy predictors. Through an extensive evaluation across several domains, we find that LLMs can formalize constraints to a meaningful extent, generating formulas that are often similar to those provided by human experts. Moreover, when the generated formulas are syntactically valid, they can lead to high-quality downstream predictions. The code and benchmark are available at https://unitn-sml.github.io/auto-nesy-bench/.

</details>


### [244] [Langevin-Informed Transfer Learning: Replacing Target Samples by Black-Box Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01522)
*Vladimir R. Kostic,Karim Lounici,Hélène Halconruy,Timothée Devergne,Michele Parrinello,Massimiliano Pontil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Langevin-Informed Transfer Learning (LITL)，利用带偏源样本和黑盒反馈恢复目标Langevin动力学，通过Dirichlet表示学习刻画生成元谱结构与投影漂移，并给出有限样本理论保证，在分子模拟、生成模型与神经网络潜空间操控等任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与机器学习系统受低维结构、慢时间尺度的随机动力学支配，但用于识别和解释这些动力学的目标轨迹往往不可获得，只能得到带偏或静态的样本，因此需要在分布偏移下恢复目标随机动力学。

Method: 提出LITL框架，仅依赖黑盒反馈从带偏源样本中恢复目标Langevin动力学；通过学习目标无穷小生成元的主导谱结构和投影漂移（基于Dirichlet表示学习），实现谱形式的动力学重构与慢流形梯度场估计；并给出适用于归一化潜表示的球面变体。

Result: 理论上建立了特征值、特征函数与投影漂移在Sobolev范数下的有限样本保证，确保这些量及其一阶导数的泛化。实验上，LITL从带偏分子模拟中恢复物理跃迁时间尺度，从生成模型静态样本中构建动力学结构，重建物理系统的球面对称性，并在黑盒反馈下实现已训练神经网络的潜空间后处理操控。

Conclusion: 该工作将谱算子学习定位为在分布偏移下恢复随机动力学的实用框架，为机器学习与物理科学开辟了广泛应用空间。

Abstract: Many scientific and machine learning systems, from molecular dynamics to diffusion models and beyond, are governed by stochastic dynamics with low-dimensional structure, evolving on slow timescales. However, target trajectories, used to identify and interpret such dynamics, are often inaccessible: only biased or static samples that explore the underlying manifold are available. We introduce Langevin-Informed Transfer Learning (LITL), a framework for recovering target Langevin dynamics from biased source samples using only black-box feedback. LITL learns the leading spectral structure of the target infinitesimal generator and the projected drift through Dirichlet representation learning, enabling kinetic reconstruction in spectral form and slow-manifold gradient field estimation. We further introduce a spherical variant well suited to steering normalized latent representations commonly used in learning systems toward desired objectives. We establish finite-sample guarantees for eigenvalue, eigenfunction, and projected drift estimation in Sobolev norms, thereby ensuring generalization of these quantities and their first-order derivatives. Empirically, LITL recovers physical transition timescales from biased molecular simulations, builds kinetic structure from static samples of generative models, reconstructs spherical symmetries of physical systems, and enables post-hoc latent steering of trained neural networks under black-box feedback. Together, these results position spectral operator learning as a practical framework for recovering stochastic dynamics under distribution shift and unlock applications across machine learning and the physical sciences.

</details>


### [245] [Exact Distinguishability in Non-Markovian Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01527)
*Kabir Murjani,Nisarg Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究固定行为策略下离线数据能否区分两个候选RDP，证明观测等价候选的后验几率恒等于先验几率，精确刻画该等价关系，并提出线性时间判定算法PEC，实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫环境常建模为RDP，其动力学通过有限自动机依赖交互历史。现有RDP的离线保证依赖行为策略的可区分性假设，但该假设无法验证；一旦违反，不同模型可同样好地解释数据。因此需要研究数据在何种条件下能区分两个候选RDP。

Method: 在固定行为策略下分析两个候选RDP的可区分性，证明观测等价候选的后验几率在任何样本量下都等于先验几率（即使策略访问每个自动机状态），并用Lean 4对两个结果进行形式化验证；随后精确刻画该等价关系，推导出PEC算法，在乘积自动机规模上以线性时间判定等价。

Result: 证明了观测等价候选的后验几率恒等于先验几率，并在Lean 4中形式化验证；精确刻画了等价关系并给出线性时间PEC算法；在四个测试环境中，先前工作的可区分性假设在三个上失效，而PEC识别出的实验在每个情形下都恢复了该假设。

Conclusion: 固定策略下RDP的可区分性由观测等价性决定，PEC可高效判定并设计出恢复可区分性的实验，弥补了现有假设不可验证的缺陷。

Abstract: Non-Markovian environments are often modeled as Regular Decision Processes (RDPs), where dynamics depend on the interaction history through a finite automaton. Existing offline guarantees for RDPs rely on a distinguishability assumption on the behaviour policy but provide no means of verifying it. When the assumption is violated, distinct models may explain the data equally well. We study when data collected under a fixed behaviour policy can distinguish two candidate RDPs. We prove that the posterior odds between observationally equivalent candidates remain equal to the prior odds at every sample size, even when the policy visits every automaton state, and verify both results formally in Lean 4. We then characterize this equivalence exactly and derive PEC, an algorithm that decides it in time linear in the size of the product automaton. The distinguishability assumption of prior work fails on three of our four test environments, and the experiment identified by PEC restores it in each case.

</details>


### [246] [Calibrating Prediction Timeliness Through Multi-Objective Hyperparameter Optimization for Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01530)
*Tugrul Cabir Hakyemez,Ener Uras Gokhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将RUL预测的优化目标本身视为设计变量，在5种架构、3种优化策略、2个基准共75种组合上比较，发现多目标优化在精度相当的前提下可改善早晚预测的方向性失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性维护中，RUL预测过早与过晚的代价不对称，但超参数优化通常只优化单一精度指标，对两个方向一视同仁，导致预测行为偏离实际需求。

Method: 以优化目标为设计变量，在MLP、LSTM、XGBoost、TCN、Transformer五种架构上设置三种策略：最大化R²、最小化NASA评分函数、以及同时优化两者的多目标形式；多目标搜索采用NSGA-II并结合Entropy-CRITIC加权进行Pareto选择。

Result: 在NASA C-MAPSS上各策略精度相近（R²≈0.89），但多目标优化使方向性失衡降低约33%，改善了早晚预测的校准；模型排名依赖配置，简单架构常优于深层时序模型。在BackBlaze上两目标由互补转为冲突，Entropy-CRITIC权重分化，泛化差距明显（最佳R²≈0.34）。

Conclusion: 优化目标会实质性地塑造预测行为，多目标搜索为在RUL建模中校准预测时效性提供了可行机制。

Abstract: In predictive maintenance, early and late RUL prediction errors carry asymmetric consequences, yet hyperparameter optimization typically targets a single accuracy metric that treats both directions equally. This study treats the optimization objective itself as a design variable. Five architectures (MLP, LSTM, XGBoost, TCN, and Transformer) are evaluated under three regimes: single-objective maximization of $R^2$, single-objective minimization of the NASA scoring function, and a multi-objective formulation that jointly optimizes both criteria. The multi-objective search employs NSGA-II with Entropy-CRITIC weighting for Pareto selection. Seventy-five model-dataset-strategy combinations are assessed on the NASA C-MAPSS turbofan and BackBlaze hard-disk drive benchmarks. On C-MAPSS, all strategies achieve comparable accuracy ($R^2 \approx 0.89$), yet multi-objective optimization reduces directional imbalance by approximately 33%, improving calibration of early versus late predictions. Model rankings prove configuration-dependent, with simpler architectures frequently outperforming deeper temporal models. On BackBlaze, the objectives shift from complementary to conflicting, producing divergent Entropy-CRITIC weights and a substantial generalization gap (best $R^2 \approx 0.34$). These results demonstrate that the optimization objective materially shapes prognostic behavior and that multi-objective search provides a practical mechanism for calibrating prediction timeliness in RUL modeling.

</details>


### [247] [FedFit: Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs via Vector-Bank Parameterization and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01537)
*Hang Zou,Chao Zhang,Yuzhi Yang,Yu Tian,Samson Lasaulce,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedFit：通过分离式共享向量库参数化与交替优化，解决联邦LoRA中SoP与PoS的聚合困境，在保持性能的同时实现最高100倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可支持大语言模型的隐私保护微调，但通信开销是严重瓶颈。在联邦场景中使用LoRA时，存在"聚合困境"：精确的"积之和"（SoP）与通信高效的"和之积"（PoS）实现之间难以兼顾。

Method: 1）提出分离式共享向量库参数化，用两个紧凑且互不相交的全局向量库重建高维适配器矩阵以降低通信开销；2）设计交替优化调度，在解耦的单库更新（可精确聚合）与由残差谱聚合机制校正的联合更新之间循环，化解SoP与PoS冲突；3）结合分块量化与客户端误差反馈进一步压缩传输向量；4）给出理论收敛保证。

Result: 在Qwen2.5模型上的大量实验表明，FedFit的困惑度性能与标准联邦LoRA方法相当，同时压缩率最高提升100倍。

Conclusion: FedFit有效缓解了联邦LoRA的通信瓶颈与聚合困境，在几乎不损失性能的前提下大幅提升压缩效率，并具备理论收敛保障。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet the massive communication overhead remains a critical bottleneck. Furthermore, applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) in FL faces a fundamental "aggregation dilemma" between the accurate Sum-of-Products (SoP) and the communication-efficient Product-of-Sums (PoS) implementations. To tackle these challenges, we propose FedFit. First, to significantly reduce communication overhead, we introduce a disjoint shared vector-bank parameterization that reconstructs high-dimensional adapter matrices from two compact and disjoint global vector banks. Second, to address the aggregation dilemma, we devise an alternating optimization schedule. By cycling between decoupled single-bank updates (which allow for accurate aggregation) and joint updates corrected by a Residual Spectral Aggregation mechanism, we resolve the conflict between SoP and PoS. Additionally, we integrate blockwise quantization with client-side error feedback to further compress the transmitted vectors. Furthermore, we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5 models demonstrate that FedFit achieves perplexity performance comparable to standard federated LoRA methods, while providing compression ratios up to 100x higher.

</details>


### [248] [Range-GRPO: Policy Optimization via Pairwise Relations among Reward Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01548)
*Ryunyi Lee,Kangjun Noh,Somin Kim,Heedong Kim,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Range-GRPO，将LLM-as-a-Judge伪奖励表示为共形校准的奖励区间，在GRPO中通过区间两两比较构建学习信号，实现半监督后训练，性能优于对比方法且训练资源需求更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练依赖可靠监督，但无参考答案或可执行验证器的领域监督成本高。LLM-as-a-Judge虽可提供可扩展伪奖励，但单点分数无法显式表达奖励不确定性。

Method: 提出Range-GRPO半监督后训练框架，结合少量标注数据与无标注提示。将伪奖励建模为共形校准的奖励区间，修改GRPO目标函数，让奖励区间以成对比较方式参与，而非归约为点奖励，使区间不确定性影响相对优势信号的幅度与方向。

Result: 理论分析刻画了该目标的特性，并证明当所有奖励区间退化为点时，所提目标可恢复Dr.GRPO优势。实验中，Range-GRPO在所评估的半监督方法中取得最高的分布内与分布外平均性能，且所需训练资源更少。

Conclusion: 将伪奖励表示为校准区间并在GRPO中进行区间成对比较，是提升半监督LLM后训练效果与效率的有效途径。

Abstract: As the use of large language models (LLMs) expands, post-training has become increasingly important for adapting them to downstream tasks. However, obtaining reliable supervision remains costly, especially in domains without reference answers or executable verifiers. LLM-as-a-Judge provides scalable pseudo-rewards for unlabeled responses, but a single point score does not explicitly represent reward uncertainty. This motivates representing pseudo-rewards as conformally calibrated reward ranges. We propose Range-GRPO, a semi-supervised post-training framework that combines limited labeled data with unlabeled prompts. In Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), learning signals depend on relative reward comparisons within each rollout group. The proposed objective compares reward ranges pairwise rather than reducing them to point rewards, allowing interval uncertainty to affect both the magnitude and direction of these signals. Our theoretical analysis characterizes this distinction and shows that the proposed objective recovers the Dr.GRPO advantage when all reward ranges collapse to points. Empirically, Range-GRPO achieves the highest in-distribution and out-of-distribution average performance among the evaluated semi-supervised methods while requiring fewer training resources.

</details>


### [249] [QK-Wanda: Coupling Queries and Keys for Unstructured Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01554)
*Ivan Ilin,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QK-Wanda，将查询和键权重联合评分剪枝，降低重构误差并在部分大模型上改善困惑度与零样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wanda独立对每个线性投影内的权重打分剪枝，但查询与键通过点积相互作用，忽略这种耦合可能不是最优的。

Method: QK-Wanda在未掩码的pre-RoPE重构目标下，按单个删除代价对查询和键权重评分，并用对侧投影信息增强Wanda分数，使两者共享剪枝预算；分数为闭式解，无需梯度或权重更新。

Result: 剪枝耗时仅比Wanda多1.3%（A100）和3.1%（H200）。在15个模型（0.5B-72B）上，50%稀疏度QK重构误差平均降低60%，80%降低45%。Llama 2 70B在80%稀疏度下WikiText-2与C4困惑度分别下降20.3%和13.5%，零样本准确率提升5.94个百分点；Qwen2.5-72B亦改善，但Llama-3.1-70B困惑度显著升高。

Conclusion: 结果表明耦合剪枝准则具有潜力，同时揭示局部重构误差作为模型质量预测指标存在局限。

Abstract: Wanda (Sun et al., 2024) prunes large language models by scoring weights independently within each linear projection, although queries and keys interact through dot products. We introduce QK-Wanda, which scores query and key weights by their individual deletion costs under an unmasked pre-RoPE reconstruction objective. It augments Wanda scores with information from the opposite projection (keys for query weights, and queries for key weights), allowing both projections to share a pruning budget. Its closed-form scores require no gradients or weight updates; full pruning takes 1.3% longer than Wanda on A100 and 3.1% longer on H200 with the calibration used in our main experiments. We evaluate QK-only pruning across 15 models from TinyLlama, Llama 2, Llama 3, and Qwen2.5, spanning 0.5B-72B parameters. Relative to Wanda, QK-Wanda reduces QK reconstruction error by an average of 60% at 50% sparsity and 45% at 80%. Downstream gains depend on the model. At 80% sparsity on Llama 2 70B, WikiText-2 and C4 perplexity decrease by 20.3% and 13.5%, while mean zero-shot accuracy rises by 5.94 percentage points. Qwen2.5-72B also improves, but Llama-3.1-70B has substantially higher perplexity despite lower reconstruction error. These results show both the promise of coupled pruning criteria and the limits of local reconstruction as a predictor of model quality.

</details>


### [250] [Towards Optimal Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01566)
*Yaniv Oren,Viliam Vadocz,Wiktor Zabka,Thomas Evers,Jan Robine,Wendelin Böhmer,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Martha White,Hendrik Baier,Fenghui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理研究策略改进，将"最优策略改进"定义为在给定约束下单次更新所能达到的最优策略；证明限制在状态子集上的最优改进等价于求解一个诱导MDP，并把贪心化建模为不确定性下的概率决策问题，推导出新的最优算子，在多种RL算法和设定中取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际强化学习算法依赖在近似评估（approximate evaluation）存在的情况下进行迭代策略改进，但"最优策略改进"缺乏从第一性原理出发的清晰定义，且实际中常用的贪心化（greedification）步骤在近似评估约束下是否最优尚不清楚，因此作者希望从理论层面刻画最优改进及其可实现的途径。

Method: 1）将最优策略改进定义为在指定约束下单次更新可得到的最优策略；2）证明限制在状态子集上的最优改进等价于求解一个诱导MDP，从而把"带显式或隐式模型的规划"刻画为通往最优策略改进的路径；3）在近似评估这一核心实际约束下，将贪心化形式化为不确定性下的概率决策问题，并推导出对该目标最优的新算子，同时给出基于梯度的实用近似。

Result: 新算子及其梯度近似在GumbelAlphaZero、SAC、ReBRAC和广义策略迭代（Generalized Policy Iteration）上均提升了整体性能，实验覆盖离散与连续动作、基于模型与无模型、在线与离线强化学习等多种设定。

Conclusion: 该工作为最优策略改进提供了统一的理论视角，说明规划可视为实现最优改进的途径，并给出在近似评估约束下理论上最优的贪心化算子和实用的梯度近似版本，具有较强的通用性和跨算法、跨设定的实证效果。

Abstract: Practical Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms learn to solve Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) through iterative policy improvement in the presence of approximate evaluation. We study policy improvement from first principles, defining optimal policy improvement as producing the best policy attainable in a single update under specified constraints. We show that optimal improvement restricted to a set of states is equivalent to solving an induced MDP, characterizing planning with an explicit or implicit model as a path towards optimal policy improvement. Because practical methods commonly solve such induced problems through iterative improvement in the form of greedification, we take steps towards optimal greedification under the central practical constraint of approximate evaluation. We formulate greedification under this constraint as probabilistic decision-making under uncertainty and derive a novel operator that is optimal with respect to the resulting objective. Empirically, the operator and its practical gradient-based approximations improve aggregate performance across GumbelAlphaZero, SAC, ReBRAC and Generalized Policy Iteration, in experiments spanning discrete and continuous actions, model-based and model-free, online and offline RL.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond Pointwise Error: A Multi-Metric Evaluation of Spatial Climate Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01579)
*Loys Masquelier,Etienne Le Naour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多指标基准，用于评估五种气候空间降尺度方法在ERA5温度、风速和降水场上的表现，揭示空间保真度与精细尺度变率之间存在系统性权衡，说明不存在单一最优降尺度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候降尺度旨在从粗分辨率输入重建精细尺度空间场，但其质量评估存在困难：低逐点误差可能牺牲精细尺度变率，而真实的空间变率又可能伴随不准确的局部结构。评估指标的选择会改变哪种方法看起来最优，因此需要多指标评估。

Method: 本文构建了一个多指标基准，在ERA5温度、风场和降水场数据上比较五种空间降尺度方法。评估采用五个标准，分别考察互补性质：逐点误差、结构相似性、分布误差、谱误差和梯度误差。

Result: 结果显示空间保真度与精细尺度变率之间存在系统性权衡。一些方法在逐点误差和空间对齐指标上表现最佳，但丢失了高频内容；另一些方法在牺牲局部结构定位准确性的情况下保留了显著更多的谱变率。因此，方法排名随指标和变量的不同而变化。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明不存在单一最优的降尺度方法。多指标评估对于判断气候场的哪些性质被保留至关重要。

Abstract: Climate downscaling aims to reconstruct fine scale spatial fields from coarse resolution inputs. Evaluating the quality of these reconstructions is challenging: low pointwise error can come at the cost of fine scale variability, while realistic spatial variability can be achieved with inaccurate local structures. The evaluation metric can therefore change which method appears to perform best. This work presents a multi metric benchmark comparing five spatial downscaling methods on ERA5 temperature, wind, and precipitation fields. Five criteria assess complementary properties: pointwise error, structural similarity, distribution error, spectral error, and gradient error. The results reveal a systematic trade off between spatial fidelity and fine scale variability. Some methods perform best on pointwise and spatially aligned metrics, but lose high frequency content, while others preserve substantially more spectral variability at the cost of less accurately positioned local structures. Consequently, method rankings change across metrics and variables. These results show that there is no single best downscaling method. Multi metric evaluation is therefore essential for assessing which properties of a climate field are preserved.

</details>


### [252] [Two Routes to the Middle: Placement Search and Brain Readouts Converge on Where Continual Learners Should Specialize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01590)
*Yuan Huang,Zihan Chen,Runbin Zhang,Hongwei Ding,Changzeng Fu,Shiqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在预训练ViT中为持续学习放置任务特定适配器（adapter）的位置问题，提出无需搜索的LS-B方法：利用冻结的人脑视觉区fMRI编码模型观察首批任务，将任务特定容量一次性分配给跨任务读数变化最大而结构稳定的块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在每个块中保留任务特定适配器会随任务数线性增长存储；仅在少数块中保留适配器可抑制增长，但"放在哪些块"成为关键问题，现有方法缺乏明确指导。

Method: 从两个角度研究：(1) 算法上训练所有连续四块组合，观察准确率随深度的变化及基于权重谱/激活统计的低成本判据；(2) 借鉴视觉皮层层次组织与中期可塑性，提出LS-B：用冻结的12个人脑视觉区fMRI编码模型观察首批任务，将任务特定容量一次性提交给跨任务读数变化相对其稳定结构最大的块，无需标签或反向传播。

Result: 连续四块放置呈倒U形，准确率峰值在中间深度，波动达3.5个百分点，而低成本判据偏好最深层块。LS-B在三个ViT-B/16骨架上给出稳定且骨架特定的分配；在AugReg和iBOT上选中的块与搜索得到的中间深度区域重叠，在相同存储与观察预算下优于最浅和最深的四块配置。在Split ImageNet-R上，LS-B用60%的full-BiLoRA适配器存储，最终准确率差距在1.5个百分点内，运行时间增加不到0.6%，并呈现骨架特定的皮层特征。

Conclusion: 适配器放置存在中间深度最优的倒U形规律，低成本判据并不可靠；基于人脑视觉皮层fMRI读数的方法LS-B可在无标签、无反向传播、极低开销下找到接近搜索最优的放置方案，显著节省存储并保持准确率。

Abstract: Continual learners that keep a task-specific adapter in every block of a pre-trained vision transformer accumulate storage linearly with the number of tasks; keeping task-specific adapters in only a few blocks curbs this growth but raises the question of where to place them. We investigate this question from two perspectives. Algorithmically, training all contiguous four-block placements yields an inverted U: final accuracy peaks at intermediate depth and varies by up to 3.5 percentage points (pp), while inexpensive criteria based on weight spectra or activation statistics favor the deepest blocks. From neuroscience, the hierarchical organization and intermediate-stage plasticity of the visual cortex motivate us to ask whether a measurement taken outside the learner can guide layer specialization without placement search. LS-B observes the first tasks through a frozen fMRI encoding model of twelve human visual areas and commits task-specific capacity once to the blocks whose readouts vary most across tasks relative to their stable structure. Across three ViT-B/16 backbones, LS-B yields stable, backbone-specific allocations. On the two backbones with placement search, AugReg and iBOT, the selected blocks overlap the intermediate-depth region identified by search. Under matched storage and observation budgets, the selected blocks outperform the shallowest and deepest four-block configurations. On Split ImageNet-R, LS-B uses 60% of full-BiLoRA adapter storage while remaining within 1.5 pp of its final accuracy. The allocation requires no labels or backpropagation, adds under 0.6% runtime, and exhibits backbone-specific cortical signatures.

</details>


### [253] [Permutation-Robust Decision Modeling with Candidate-Independent Block-Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01601)
*Guy Amit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出候选无关的块因果注意力（candidate-independent block-causal attention），通过阻断候选间的信息流并重置候选位置，降低决策模型对候选序列化顺序的敏感性，同时保持有竞争力的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型常将可变数量的候选动作编码在单一序列中打分，这在生成式系统的System 1组件中很常见，候选的提出或排序在不同运行中可能不同。标准因果交叉编码虽有表达力，但会使候选得分依赖于序列化顺序而非决策问题本身，因此需要一种对候选排列不敏感的方法。

Method: 提出候选无关的块因果注意力：在共享上下文和每个候选内部保留因果计算，同时阻断跨候选信息流并重置候选位置。在Gemma 3 1B、Qwen3 1.7B和Qwen3 4B骨干网络上，与标准因果注意力和互补的不变基线进行对比。此外还在Qwen3-4B上使用更多训练数据进一步研究。

Result: 候选无关注意力持续降低排列敏感性，同时保持有竞争力的决策质量。消融实验表明候选隔离是该效果的主要来源，位置重置补全了预期的对称性。更大规模的Qwen3-4B研究进一步考察了该架构在更多训练数据下的表现。

Conclusion: 候选无关的块因果注意力能有效减少决策模型对候选排列顺序的依赖，保持决策质量，且候选隔离是核心机制。代码和模型已公开。

Abstract: Decision models often score a variable-sized set of candidate actions encoded in a single sequence. This setting is increasingly relevant for System 1 components inside generative systems, where candidates may be proposed or ordered differently across runs. Standard causal cross-encoding is expressive, but it can make a candidate's score depend on serialization order rather than on the underlying decision problem. We introduce candidate-independent block-causal attention, which preserves causal computation within the shared context and each candidate while blocking cross-candidate information flow and resetting candidate positions. We compare this architecture with standard causal attention and complementary invariant baselines across Gemma 3 1B, Qwen3 1.7B, and Qwen3 4B backbones. Candidate-independent attention consistently reduces permutation sensitivity while retaining competitive decision quality; ablations indicate that candidate isolation is the primary source of the effect, with position resetting completing the intended symmetry. A larger Qwen3-4B study further examines the behavior of the proposed architecture with substantially more training data. Code is available at the \href{https://github.com/guyAmit/ci-decision-models}{\textcolor{blue}{project repository}}, and the \href{https://huggingface.co/Guy-Amit/qwen3-4b-ci-decision-4096-poc}{\textcolor{blue}{Qwen3-4B model artifact}} is available on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [254] [Exposing the Cost of Deep Learning Audio Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01619)
*Constance Douwes,Paul Magron,Romain Serizel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于计算平台活动日志的方法，估算深度学习音频项目在开发阶段的能耗，发现开发阶段能耗远超模型训练能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注模型训练和推理的能耗与碳排放，而忽略了整个开发阶段（架构原型设计与大量实验），这一阶段能耗需求很高却缺乏系统评估。

Method: 提出一种估算开发阶段能耗的方法，基于LORIA实验室使用的Grid5000共享计算平台的活动日志，并以Multispeech研究团队的音频项目为案例，评估四个项目的总体能耗，并与报告模型的训练能耗进行对比。

Result: 开发阶段所需能耗是单独训练最佳性能模型的3至256倍。

Conclusion: 呼吁对基于深度学习的音频项目全生命周期的能耗进行更系统的报告。

Abstract: The environmental impact of deep learning has attracted increasing attention over the past decade. Existing studies mainly focus on the energy and carbon emissions of model training and inference, while the whole development phase is often overlooked. Yet, architecture prototyping and intensive experiments are conducted during this stage, which is highly energy-demanding. In this article, we propose a methodology to estimate these costs, based on activity logs from the Grid5000 shared computing platform used by the LORIA laboratory. As a case-study, we focus on audio projects developed in the Multispeech research team. We evaluate the overall energy cost of four projects, and we compare them to those of training the reported models. Our results show that the energy required for the development phase is 3 to 256 times greater than that required to train the best-performing model alone. These results advocate for a more systematic reporting of energy consumption across the entire life cycle of deep learning-based audio projects.

</details>


### [255] [Generalization in Neural Networks Through the Lens of Magnitude Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01633)
*Sahel Torkamani,Henry Gouk,Rik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"幅度势"(magnitude potential)这一基于度量幅度理论的量,用以刻画点被给定集合表示的程度,并将其应用于分析神经网络的泛化、记忆与grokking现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络泛化与训练动力学的解释仍是难题,现有多种方法从不同侧面研究这些现象,但缺乏一个能统一刻画几何表示结构的量,因此作者希望引入一个基础量来考察神经元泛化的多种特征。

Method: 引入 magnitude potential,即基于度量幅度理论衡量任意点被给定集合表示得好坏的量。在 logit 层计算"相对于某一类别的幅度势"与"相对于整个数据集的幅度势"之比,用该比值刻画点的表示;并将单点的比值与 Feldman memorization 分数对比,同时对跨点聚合的比值进行统计分析。

Result: 单点幅度势比值与 Feldman 记忆分数相关;跨点聚合的幅度势比值能检测决策边界的结构性变化,并为模算术中的 grokking 提供几何指标;即使在显式抑制 neural collapse 的情况下,幅度势比值仍保持信息量。

Conclusion: 幅度势是一个简洁而有效的量,可从几何角度统一刻画记忆、决策边界变化与 grokking 等泛化特征,且其信息不依赖于 neural collapse。

Abstract: Explaining generalization and training dynamics in neural networks remains a challenge, and various approaches have been developed to study different aspects of these phenomena. In this paper, we introduce the idea of {\em magnitude potential} -- a quantity based on the theory of metric magnitude -- that reflects how well an arbitrary point is represented by a given set. We find that this basic quantity can be applied to examine various features in neural generalization. The ratio between the magnitude potential with respect to a class and with respect to the entire data, computed at the logit layer, is informative of the representation of the point. In experiments, these ratios for individual training points are found to be correlated with the Feldman memorization scores. Magnitude potential ratios aggregated across points detect structural changes in the decision boundaries and provide a geometric indicator of grokking in modular arithmetic. Although the magnitude potential ratio and neural collapse are both closely associated with intra-class and inter-class geometric structure, the magnitude potential ratio remains informative even when neural collapse is explicitly suppressed.

</details>


### [256] [FedSAP: Federated Learning with Structured Adaptive Partitioning for Multi-Domain Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01638)
*Wentao Yue,Tianyou Lai,Hongji Li,Qingyu Mao,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSAP：将结构化剪枝转化为预算约束下的三态通道分配，实现域感知异构联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构边缘设备的资源预算不均且本地数据存在域偏移。现有资源自适应方法只决定客户端训练模型多少，却不决定保留容量应放在哪里、如何共享；联邦域泛化方法通常假设共享完整架构。统一压缩会丢弃高价值通道，单一聚合路径会混合可迁移特征与域敏感更新。

Method: FedSAP 将结构化剪枝建模为预算约束的三态通道分配：把每个保留率转换为非均匀层预算，将稳定通道分入 Global 池、有用的域敏感通道分入伪域特定 Private 池、低效用通道置为 Dropped。域引导分配（Domain-Guided Assignment）通过浅层梯度相似度推断伪域；类型匹配聚合（Type-Matched Aggregation）限制每个通道仅在其预期共享范围内。

Result: 在三个随机种子上，FedSAP 在 Digits 和 Office-Caltech 上达到 76.00% 和 72.67% 的平均全局准确率，分别超过最强基线 1.70 和 4.92 个百分点，同时支持异构客户端最高 80% 的客户端剪枝率。

Conclusion: FedSAP 通过三态通道分配与类型匹配聚合，在资源受限和域偏移并存的异构联邦学习场景下有效提升全局准确率并支持高剪枝率。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) on heterogeneous edge devices must jointly accommodate unequal resource budgets and domain-shifted local data. Existing resource-adaptive methods decide how much of a model each client trains but not where retained capacity should reside or how it should be shared, whereas federated domain-generalization methods usually assume a shared full architecture. Uniform compression can therefore discard high-utility channels, and a single aggregation path can mix transferable features with domain-sensitive updates. We propose FedSAP, a domain-aware heterogeneous FL framework that casts structured pruning as budget-constrained tri-state channel allocation. FedSAP converts each keep ratio into non-uniform layer budgets, assigns stable channels to a Global pool, useful domain-sensitive channels to pseudo-domain-specific Private pools, and low-utility channels to a Dropped state. This partition lets broadly useful features benefit from cross-client pooling while isolating domain-sensitive updates from incompatible clients. Domain-Guided Assignment infers pseudo-domains from shallow-gradient similarity, while Type-Matched Aggregation restricts each channel to its intended sharing scope. Across three random seeds, FedSAP reaches 76.00% and 72.67% mean global accuracy on Digits and Office-Caltech, exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.70 and 4.92 percentage points while supporting client pruning ratios of up to 80% across heterogeneous clients.

</details>


### [257] [MCIR: A Feature Dependence-Aware Explainability Method with Reliability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01641)
*Poushali Sengupta,Sabita Maharjan,Frank Eliassen,Shashi Raj Pandey,Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCIR-M，一种依赖感知的全局特征重要性方法，通过MCIR指标衡量特征在强依赖邻居之外贡献的独特预测信息，在多重共线性和近重复特征下表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型常含强依赖或冗余特征，共享的预测信息分散在相关预测变量中，导致SHAP、LIME、HSIC、MI/CMI、SAGE等现有归因方法在多重共线性或近重复特征下产生不稳定的重要性排序。

Method: 提出MCIR-M：先为每个特征选定强依赖邻居构成依赖邻域，将特征条件化于邻居后，计算条件信息与块级信息的归一化比值，即互相关影响比（MCIR）；总体得分落在[0,1]区间，在完全条件冗余时为零。并设计轻量估计流程，仅用部分数据计算MCIR，并通过排序、头部集合与忠实性诊断评估其与全数据解释的一致性。

Result: 在受控合成冗余实验和UCI HAR基准上，MCIR呈现依赖感知的排序行为，在注入近重复预测变量时优势最明显；与独立/条件SHAP、SAGE、HSIC、MI类得分及CIR系列基线的比较在真实数据准则上结果不一。减少解释样本可降低计算负担，同时另行评估其与全数据解释的一致性。

Conclusion: MCIR-M为强特征依赖下的全局解释提供了一种实用的依赖感知诊断工具。

Abstract: Modern machine-learning models often contain strongly dependent or redundant features, making feature attribution difficult because shared predictive information can be distributed across correlated predictors. Existing methods such as SHAP, LIME, HSIC, MI/CMI, and SAGE may therefore produce unstable rankings under multicollinearity or near-duplicate predictors. We propose the Mutual Correlation Impact Ratio Method (MCIR-M), a dependence-aware global feature-importance approach that quantifies the unique predictive information contributed by each feature beyond a selected dependence neighbourhood. MCIR-M introduces the Mutual Correlation Impact Ratio (MCIR), which conditions each feature on strongly dependent neighbours and computes a normalized ratio of conditional to block-level information. The population score lies in [0,1] and equals zero under exact conditional redundancy. We also introduce a lightweight estimation procedure that computes MCIR using a fraction of the available data and evaluates agreement with full-data explanations. Across controlled synthetic redundancy experiments and the UCI HAR benchmark, MCIR shows dependence-aware ranking behaviour, with its clearest advantage under injected near-duplicate predictors. Comparisons with independent and conditional SHAP, SAGE, HSIC, MI-based scores, and CIR-family baselines are mixed across real-data criteria. Reduced explanation samples lower computational burden in the evaluated configurations, while agreement with full-data explanations is assessed separately through ranking, head-set, and faithfulness diagnostics. Overall, MCIR-M provides a practical dependence-aware diagnostic for global explanation under strong feature dependence.

</details>


### [258] [Beyond Demographic Balance: Multi-Metric and Intersectional Evaluation of Fairness in MIMIC-IV Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01645)
*Abdullah Al Noman,Fahmid Al Rifat,Tahrima Hashem,Syed Muhammad Ibne Zulfiker,Rishov Paul,Tanzima HAshem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在MIMIC-IV的ICU死亡预测中，公平性干预的评估结论高度依赖所报指标与人口分组粒度；作者提出一种不考虑结局的轻量表征均衡策略，并在边际与三重交叉子群层面进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性结论对评估指标（准确率/AUROC、敏感度、假阳性率）和人口分辨率高度敏感，边际人口汇总可能掩盖交叉子群内部的异质性误差，因此有必要重新审视这些评估选择。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV上做ICU死亡预测，比较多种公平性干预下的预测效用指标与子群误差指标；并引入一种轻量适应策略，在不以死亡结局为条件的前提下联合平衡种族—性别—保险的代表性，使人口表征均衡可与结局条件化或直接误差率干预区分开；在边际及对应的三重交叉子群层面评估，并考虑更细粒度估计的统计支撑。

Result: 干预措施在准确率/AUROC、敏感度和假阳性率上可得到显著不同的评价；边际人口汇总可能掩盖其构成交叉子群中的异质性误差轮廓，甚至包括较大子群内部。

Conclusion: 评估公平性干预时需同时在互补的指标与子群分辨率两个维度进行，并考虑干预目标与子群估计的可靠性。

Abstract: Fairness conclusions in clinical prediction can depend strongly on both the metrics reported and the demographic resolution at which performance is evaluated. We revisit these evaluation choices for ICU mortality prediction on MIMIC-IV, comparing predictive-utility and subgroup-error metrics across several fairness interventions. As a complementary case study, we introduce a lightweight adaptation strategy that jointly balances ethnicity--gender--insurance representation without conditioning on mortality outcomes, allowing demographic representation balancing to be examined separately from outcome-conditioned or direct error-rate interventions. We evaluate its behavior at both marginal and corresponding three-way intersectional subgroup levels, while accounting for the statistical support of finer-grained estimates. The results show that interventions can receive substantially different assessments across accuracy/AUROC, sensitivity, and false-positive rate, and that marginal demographic summaries can conceal heterogeneous error profiles within their constituent intersections, including among larger subgroups. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating fairness interventions at both complementary metric and subgroup resolutions, while accounting for the intervention target and the reliability of subgroup estimates.

</details>


### [259] [CrossGMN: Graph Metanetworks for Cross-Architecture Weight-Space Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01649)
*Adir Dayan,Yam Eitan,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨架构的权重空间算子新范式，通过引入目标网络初始化作为第二输入，定义了对源置换不变、对目标置换等变的算子，并提出 CrossGMN，在图元网络上实现跨网络消息传递，用于模型压缩加速知识蒸馏，最高加速 8.89 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变权重空间架构主要针对保持网络架构不变的变换（如神经元置换），但模型压缩、升尺度等实际任务会将源网络映射到不同架构的目标网络。此时源与目标的置换对称性作用于不同参数空间，等变性难以直接定义，因此需要新的形式化框架来处理跨架构算子。

Method: 作者将跨架构算子重新表述为双输入算子：一个已训练的源网络和一个目标网络的初始化。基于此定义等变跨架构算子——对源网络置换不变、对目标网络置换等变，用源网络信息来精炼目标网络的初始化。据此提出 CrossGMN，一种图元网络，通过保持对称性的跨网络消息传递联合处理两个网络，并证明其在一般位置假设下、在紧集上对连续跨架构算子具有通用性。

Result: 在模型压缩任务中，CrossGMN 预测更小网络的参数以加速后续知识蒸馏。在 2-D/3-D INR 以及 MLP、CNN、ViT 的图像分类任务上，蒸馏最高加速 8.89 倍；跨数据集迁移无需重训练即可加速 3.78 倍；单个模型即可支持从异构源架构向同一目标架构的压缩加速。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨架构权重空间任务建立了等变算子与通用图元网络框架，证明了通用性，并在多种架构与任务上验证了其在加速知识蒸馏与模型压缩方面的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Weight-space networks operate directly on parameters of other neural networks, enabling tasks such as predicting model properties, editing trained models, and generating weights. Weight-space symmetries such as neuron permutations make equivariance a key design principle. However, existing equivariant weight-space architectures have primarily been studied for transformations that preserve the network architecture. In contrast, many practical transformations, including model compression and upscaling, map a trained source network into a target network with a different architecture. In this setting, the source and target permutation symmetries act on different parameter spaces, making equivariance less straightforward to formulate. Our key idea for addressing this mismatch is to reformulate cross-architecture operators with two inputs: a trained source network and an initialization of the target network. This lets us define equivariant cross-architecture operators that refine the initialization of the target network using information from the source network, while being invariant to source-network permutations and equivariant to target-network permutations. Based on this formulation, we introduce CrossGMN, a graph metanetwork that jointly processes both networks through symmetry-preserving cross-network message passing. We prove CrossGMN is universal for continuous cross-architecture operators on compact sets under a general-position assumption. We evaluate CrossGMN for model compression, predicting a smaller network's parameters to accelerate subsequent knowledge distillation. Across 2-D and 3-D INRs and image classification with MLPs, CNNs, and Vision Transformers, CrossGMN speeds up distillation by up to 8.89x, transfers across datasets without retraining (3.78x), and a single model can accelerate compression from heterogeneous source architectures into a common target architecture.

</details>


### [260] [Iterative Policy Refinement through Semantic Rollout Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01652)
*Feiyu Gavin Zhu,Qi Xu,Zhifei Deng,Zhigang Hua,Luke Simon,Jean Oh,Reid Simmons*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出闭环框架，用LLM对策略rollout进行表格化诊断分析，迭代优化结构化策略，无需人工指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化策略通过任务特定的归纳偏置提升模仿学习的效率、鲁棒性和可解释性，但现有结构生成方法要么依赖大量人工输入，要么依赖LLM中编码的静态领域知识，可能与专家演示不一致。

Method: 提出闭环框架，将策略rollout记录为语义上有意义的表格数据，提示LLM生成诊断分析代码，从而识别策略结构中的次优之处并迭代修正，无需人工指令。

Result: 在赛车和开门任务上，相比零样本LLM生成结构，模仿学习性能提升高达15%；达到相同强化学习性能所需计算量减少75%。

Conclusion: 表格化rollout分析为对齐LLM生成的策略结构与专家演示提供了有效反馈信号，可自动生成优质策略结构。

Abstract: Structured policies improve efficiency, robustness, and interpretability in imitation learning by introducing task-specific inductive bias, but existing structure generation methods rely either on extensive human input or on static domain knowledge encoded in LLMs, which may be inconsistent with the expert demonstrations. We propose a closed-loop framework that iteratively refines structured policies using LLM-guided analysis of policy rollouts. By logging rollouts as semantically meaningful tabular data and prompting the LLM to generate diagnostic analysis code, our method identifies suboptimalities in the policy structure and iteratively corrects them without requiring human instruction. Experiments on car racing and door opening tasks show that our approach improves imitation learning performance by up to 15% over zero-shot LLM-generated structures and requires 75% less compute to achieve the same reinforcement learning performance. These results demonstrate that tabular rollout analysis provides an effective feedback signal to align LLM-generated policy structures with expert demonstrations, and we can utilize it to generate good policy structures automatically.

</details>


### [261] [pCoMole: Pareto-Constrained Molecule Editing with Discrete Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01663)
*Tong Chen,Maximilian Holsman,Lin Zhao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pCoMole是一个基于离散流匹配的框架，可在满足硬约束的同时对生物分子序列进行多目标帕累托对齐编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子治疗药物通常从已知序列出发，需在满足生物化学与可制造性硬约束的前提下进行靶向编辑以改善多种性质。然而现有生成方法无法在离散、变长生物空间中同时支持多目标优化、硬可行性约束和序列编辑。

Method: 该框架构建在离散流匹配之上，引导预训练的Edit Flow朝向用户偏好并强制终端可行性：通过增强Tchebycheff效用定义可行性门控的终端分布，并利用底层编辑过程的Doob h-变换实现偏好倾斜；用短程蒙特卡洛rollout近似所需的调和函数，从而得到高效且具有可证偏好一致性的引导编辑器。

Result: pCoMole在缩短GFP同时保留荧光相关性质、缩短多种Cas9直系同源物同时保持PAM特异性、以及将肽结合物压缩为在硬约束下优化七种药物相关性质的短拟肽等任务上得到验证。湿实验中，两个229残基的pCoMole设计eGFP变体在BL21细胞中经10处缺失、1或2处替换后仍保持明显绿色荧光。

Conclusion: pCoMole实现了离散、变长空间中生物分子序列的约束感知、帕累托对齐编辑。

Abstract: Biomolecular therapeutics often start from known sequences and require targeted editing to improve multiple properties while satisfying hard biochemical and manufacturability constraints. However, existing generative methods do not jointly support multi-objective optimization, hard feasibility, and sequence editing in discrete, variable-length biological spaces. In this work, we introduce Pareto-Constrained Molecule Editing (pCoMole), a framework built on discrete flow matching that steers a pre-trained Edit Flow toward user-specified preferences while enforcing terminal feasibility. pCoMole defines a feasibility-gated terminal distribution using an augmented Tchebycheff utility and realizes the resulting preference tilt through a Doob-h transform of the underlying edit process. To make this construction practical, we approximate the required harmonic function using short Monte Carlo rollouts over candidate edits, yielding an efficient guided editor with provable preference consistency. We validate pCoMole by shrinking GFP while retaining fluorescence-related properties, shortening diverse Cas9 orthologs while preserving PAM specificity, and compressing peptide binders into short peptidomimetics that optimize seven drug-related properties under hard constraints. In wet lab testing, two 229-residue pCoMole-designed eGFP variants retained clear green fluorescence in BL21 cells after 10 deletions, with either one or two substitutions. Together, pCoMole enables constraint-aware, Pareto-aligned editing of biomolecular sequences in discrete, variable-length spaces.

</details>


### [262] [Invent a Dataset: Measuring dataset generation abilities with zero seed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01674)
*Shivalika Singh,Andrija Djurisic,Gbemileke Onilude,Sudip Roy,Sara Hooker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Invent-A-Dataset，一个基于提示的系统，在零数据情境下从数据集描述生成大规模、真实的后训练数据集，在多任务、多规模下显著优于五种前沿模型 API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集构建是 AI 开发中最依赖人工且脆弱的环节之一。论文聚焦于从业者面临的最极端也最普遍的"零数据"场景：对想学习的能力完全没有可用数据，因此需要一个能从描述直接生成数据集的自动化方案。

Method: 提出 Invent-A-Dataset，一个基于提示（prompt based）的系统，可将数据集描述转换为真实且大规模的后训练数据集。将其与 Anthropic、Google、OpenAI、DeepSeek、Zai 五种前沿模型 API 进行对比，覆盖八种任务类型、数据集规模最高达 20K 样本。

Result: Invent-A-Dataset 显著胜出：质量相对提升 17%，多样性相对提升 19%。多样性优势随训练数据集规模扩大而增强，从 200 样本时的持平，到 20K 样本时达到 37% 的相对提升。下游训练收益显著，后训练模型性能更强，其微调模型在不同后训练模型架构上排名始终高于其他生成器微调结果。

Conclusion: 在零数据情境下，Invent-A-Dataset 能同时提升生成数据的质量与多样性，且多样性优势随规模放大，最终带来可观的下游后训练性能提升，验证了提示式数据集生成在零数据场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Building datasets remains one of the most manual and brittle parts of AI development. In this technical report, we focus on the most extreme but also most prevalent setting real world practitioners face: a zero data regime. Here, practitioners don't have any data for the capability they want to learn. We introduce Invent-A-Dataset which is a prompt based system to go from dataset description to realistic and large scale post-training datasets. We evaluate Invent-A-Dataset against five frontier model APIs including Anthropic, Google, Open AI, DeepSeek, Zai. Across eight task types and dataset sizes up to 20K samples, Invent-A-Dataset significantly outperforms with both the highest quality (17% relative gains) while simultaneously producing the most diverse samples (19% relative gains). Its diversity advantage widens with scale of training dataset size (from parity at 200 samples to 37% relative gains at 20K samples). This translates into considerable downstream training gains, resulting in far more performant post-trained models. Invent-A-Dataset fine-tune consistently ranks higher compared to other generator fine-tunes across different post-trained model architectures.

</details>


### [263] [MiLoop: Selective Memory Propagation for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01685)
*Changliang Zhou,Yuanyao Chen,Rongsheng Chen,Zhiyun Lin,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MiLoop：一种纯强化学习的构造式神经组合优化框架，通过复用一个rollout中已有的多步计算结果进行选择性记忆传播，使浅层策略也能学到有效的动态嵌入，无需外部解标签或训练期搜索空间剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构造式NCO方法虽有动态嵌入且泛化性好，但每步都用深层注意力栈从零重建子问题表示，计算开销大；同时许多高性能方法依赖解标签/伪标签训练，或在强化学习中采用激进的搜索空间剪枝，限制了通用性与效率。

Method: 提出Memory-in-the-Loop (MiLoop)：完全基于RL的构造式框架，利用rollout本身所需的多步计算进行选择性记忆传播。具体地，在注意力层之前将当前嵌入与历史记忆融合，之后施加自适应门控更新，更新后的表示既支持当前决策也支持逐步复用。

Result: 在四类组合优化问题上的大量实验表明，MiLoop在100到1000万节点规模的实例上均能持续生成高质量解，展现出很强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MiLoop通过将记忆机制嵌入循环式的构造过程，使浅层策略无需外部标签或搜索空间剪枝即可获得有效动态嵌入，实现了跨规模的良好泛化。

Abstract: Constructive neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) has emerged as a promising paradigm that learns to construct solutions to combinatorial optimization problems (COPs) step by step, which reduces reliance on handcrafted rules and enables fast inference. While many methods with dynamic embeddings generalize well, they typically rebuild subproblem representations from scratch at each step using deep attention stacks. Many high-performing methods in this category rely on solution labels or pseudo-labels for efficient training, or on aggressive search space pruning during reinforcement learning (RL). To address these limitations, we propose Memory-in-the-Loop (MiLoop), a purely RL-based constructive framework that leverages the multi-step computation already required by a rollout for selective memory propagation. Each rollout provides solution-quality feedback for learning while propagating historical representations, thereby enabling a shallow policy to learn effective dynamic embeddings without external solution labels or training-time search-space pruning. Specifically, MiLoop fuses current embeddings with historical memory before the attention layers and applies adaptive gated updates afterward. The updated representations support both current decisions and stepwise reuse. Extensive experiments across four COPs demonstrate that MiLoop consistently produces high-quality solutions on instances ranging from 100 to 10 million nodes, highlighting its strong generalization ability.

</details>


### [264] [Artifact Annotations Partially Substitute for Per-User Calibration: SAFE-EDA and a Normalization-Controlled Evaluation of Wrist-EDA Affect Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01692)
*Haochen Chai,Xinbi Luo,Zining Liu,Fangfang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 腕部皮肤电活动的归一化统计来源选择会显著改变预训练收益的测量结果；多数已发表研究未报告该选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部EDA幅度因人而异，模型通常需归一化后再分类，但少有研究报告归一化统计量来自何处。若统计量来自被留出被试自己的整段记录，模型便获得了设备首次佩戴时并不具备的信息，从而可能高估预训练收益。

Method: 以紧凑卷积网络SAFE-EDA为对象，先基于43名被试的专家伪迹标注进行预训练，再在WESAD数据集（15名被试，留一被试交叉验证）上从头训练作对比；交叉两种归一化来源与四种窗口步长。另在第二个数据集（26名被试）上共测试13种配置，并统计50篇已发表WESAD研究中报告归一化数据来源的比例。

Result: 当统计量仅来自训练被试时，预训练使宏F1提升0.078至0.227；当统计量来自被留出用户的完整记录时，增益降至0.020至0.050且不再显著。伪迹监督预训练远优于同数据的自监督预训练（0.078对0.008）。13种配置中预训练网络在12种下更优，但在第二数据集上，按用户归一化反而增大了增益。50篇WESAD研究中仅5篇说明了归一化所用数据。

Conclusion: 归一化统计量的来源会实质性影响预训练收益的评估，二者交互作用依赖具体数据。为区分首次使用性能与校准后性能，论文应明确报告归一化所依据的数据。基于伪迹标注的监督预训练是更有效的方向。

Abstract: Wrist electrodermal activity (EDA) differs in amplitude from one person to the next, so affect-recognition models normalize their input before classification. Studies that test such models on held-out subjects seldom report where the normalization statistics come from, yet statistics computed from the held-out subject's own recording give the model information that a device does not have when it is first worn. We asked how this choice alters the measured benefit of pretraining. A compact convolutional network, SAFE-EDA, was pretrained on expert artifact annotations from 43 subjects and compared with the same network trained from scratch on the Wearable Stress and Affect Detection (WESAD) dataset (15 subjects, leave-one-subject-out), with two normalization sources crossed with four window hops. When the statistics came only from training subjects, pretraining raised macro-F1 by 0.078 to 0.227; when they came from the held-out user's full recording, the gain fell to between 0.020 and 0.050 and was no longer significant. Artifact supervision was far more useful than self-supervised pretraining on the same recordings (0.078 versus 0.008). Across 13 configurations in two datasets, the pretrained network was better in 12, but on the second dataset (26 subjects) per-user normalization increased the gain instead of reducing it, so the interaction depends on the data. Only five of 50 published WESAD studies state which data were used for normalization. Reporting this choice is necessary to separate first-use performance from performance after calibration.

</details>


### [265] [Learning PDE Dynamics between Submanifolds Using Green's Observation Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01697)
*Jan Tauberschmidt,Jephte Abijuru,Samuel Okon,Naukshatro Bose,Sophie Fellenz,Marius Kloft,Jonas Latz,Sebastian Josef Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Green's Observation Operator (GObO)，将固定环境介质一次性映射为线性PDE在源与观测子流形上的Green核，使新源只需一次低维积分且无需网络推理，在热传导与对流扩散任务上实现零样本移动源预测，误差比黑盒代理低4–8倍、单次查询1.4ms，并可跨分辨率迁移及无重训修正弱非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理系统的驱动与观测仅发生在较大空间域的低维子流形上，而动力学由占据该域的环境介质决定（如红外相机成像的激光加热区域、传感器平面测得的地面排放）。全域求解器对每个新源都要计算整个体积，即使只需观测子流形；黑盒代理则未利用环境介质固定这一结构先验。因此需要一种只针对源与观测子流形、且能复用固定介质的算子方法。

Method: 提出Green's Observation Operator (GObO)：先将环境介质一次性映射为线性PDE的Green核，并限制到源与观测子流形上。之后新源求解退化为一次低维积分，无需网络评估。核中的指数率给出具有与时间视界无关内存的精确有限流式状态，并证明其稳定性及受限热核的近似率。

Result: 在三维热传导与对流扩散问题（源与观测几何同址及异址）上，GObO基于静态源训练即可零样本预测移动源响应，误差比黑盒代理低4–8倍；一次条件化传递后每次查询耗时1.4ms。同一核可跨分辨率迁移，并支持对轻度非线性（辐射损失、温度依赖导热率）的修正而无需重训，代价是分布内精度有所下降。

Conclusion: GObO通过分离固定环境介质与变化的源，将线性PDE响应预测简化为受限Green核上的低维积分，兼具高效率、零样本泛化、跨分辨率迁移及弱非线性修正能力，为低维观测子流形上的物理系统建模提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Many physical systems are driven and observed only on lower-dimensional submanifolds of a larger spatial domain, while their dynamics are governed by the ambient medium occupying that domain. Examples include laser-heated parts imaged by an infrared camera, and ground-level emissions measured on a sensor plane. Full-domain solvers, however, compute the entire volume for every new source although only the observation submanifold is needed, and black-box surrogates do not exploit that the ambient medium remains fixed. We introduce the \emph{Green's Observation Operator (GObO)}, which maps the ambient medium once to the Green's kernel of a linear PDE restricted to the source and observation submanifolds. New sources then cost one lower-dimensional integral and no network evaluation. Exponential rates in the kernel yield an exact finite streaming state with horizon-independent memory; we prove its stability and an approximation rate for the restricted heat kernel. On three-dimensional heat conduction and advection--diffusion with collocated and distinct source and observation geometries, GObO trained on static sources predicts responses to moving sources zero-shot with 4--8$\times$ lower error than black-box surrogates, at 1.4\,ms per query after a single conditioning pass. The same kernel transfers across resolutions and admits corrections for mild nonlinearities, including radiative losses and temperature-dependent conductivity, without retraining, at the cost of lower in-distribution accuracy.

</details>


### [266] [In-context Learning of Single-index Targets: Comparing Kernel and Feature Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01712)
*Haotian Gu,Yizhou Xu,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较两种单层注意力架构（核学习器 vs 特征学习器）在同一单指标任务族上的非线性上下文学习能力，用复制方法推导记忆与泛化误差，并给出相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL理论多聚焦线性目标函数，非线性情形下的架构选择与数据规模、任务多样性如何交互尚不清楚，因此需要研究非线性ICL中不同注意力架构的优劣条件。

Method: 在同一类单指标任务上对比两种一层注意力架构：核学习器先用固定非线性特征映射再做线性注意力，特征学习器直接对原始输入做注意力并接可学习非线性读出。采用复制方法推导二者的记忆误差与泛化误差，保留预训练规模、任务池多样性和训练/推理上下文长度的影响。

Result: 理论预测在广泛机制下与数值实验吻合良好；得到相图，刻画在预训练数据量、任务多样性和上下文长度变化时各架构何时占优；并识别出两种学习器在上下文长度标度上的定性差异。

Conclusion: 架构选择会与数据集特性相互作用并共同决定非线性上下文学习的效果，为理解非线性ICL提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables a pretrained model to infer a task from demonstrations without updating its parameters. While much of the existing theory focuses on linear target functions, in this paper we study nonlinear cases by comparing two one-layer attention architectures on the same family of single-index tasks. A kernel learner first maps inputs through a fixed nonlinear feature map and then applies linear attention, whereas a feature learner applies attention to the original input, followed by a learned nonlinear readout. We derive predictions for their memorization and generalization errors using the replica method, retaining the effects of pretraining size, task-pool diversity, and training and inference context lengths. The resulting predictions closely match numerical experiments across a broad range of regimes. Our analysis yields phase diagrams that characterize when each architecture is advantageous as the amount of pretraining data, task diversity, and context lengths vary. We further identify qualitatively different context-length scalings for the two learners. Together, these results clarify how architectural choices interact with the dataset and govern nonlinear in-context learning.

</details>


### [267] [Anomaly Detection and Localization for the Pantograph-Catenary System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01721)
*Francesco Vitale,Hangli Ge,Francesco Flammini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合GPS路线对齐的受电弓-接触网系统（PCS）定位与集体异常检测框架，用于评估PCS健康状态，并在真实工业数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频监控和卷积神经网络的PCS接触线高度/之字值测量方案未考虑列车运行路线的地理位置，缺乏空间定位信息，难以准确评估PCS的健康状况。

Method: 提出一个新框架：1）通过与参考路线标称GPS坐标对齐来对PCS高度/之字值进行定位；2）采用集体异常检测方法评估PCS健康状态。

Result: 在基于真实工业数据集（由某铁路运输公司提供，涵盖多条意大利铁路路线、多个列车行程的PCS高度/之字值）的案例研究中，应用并评估了所提方法的定位与检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够利用GPS对齐实现PCS高度/之字值的空间定位，并通过集体异常检测评估PCS健康状况，在真实工业场景中具备可行性与应用价值。

Abstract: Monitoring the Pantograph-Catenary System (PCS) provides insight into the health conditions of the pantograph and the railway infrastructure. Recent industrial solutions trace the pantograph's contact wire height and stagger (PCS height/stagger) using video monitoring through convolutional neural networks. However, these solutions do not account for the train route's geographic location. Therefore, in this paper we propose a novel framework for 1) localization of the PCS height/stagger by alignment with the nominal GPS coordinates of the reference route, and 2) collective anomaly detection to evaluate the health conditions of the PCS. We apply and assess the localization and detection performance of the methodology to a case-study based on a real-world industrial dataset provided by a railway transportation company, which includes the PCS height/stagger of several train journeys across Italian railway routes.

</details>


### [268] [RelICL: Training-free Relational Learning with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01725)
*Simon Forbat,Rainer Gemulla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出深度特征合成（DFS）用于关系学习时存在特征爆炸与交互盲区两个问题，并提出 RelICL 方法通过在图模式上逐步传播与融合信息来缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在单表任务上表现优异且无需训练，近期研究借助 DFS 将其扩展到关系学习。但 DFS 存在两个关键缺陷：一是随着模式变复杂，特征数量迅速增长，影响可扩展性与性能（特征爆炸）；二是按列的聚合无法捕捉特征间的交互作用，限制性能（交互盲区）。

Method: 提出 RelICL 方法，保留 DFS 的优势同时缓解上述问题。其核心是沿着模式图逐步传播并融合信息，且使用最终用于预测的同一个表格基础模型来完成这一传播融合过程。

Result: 在 RelBench 任务上的实验研究表明，RelICL 与基于深度特征合成的最强方法表现相当。

Conclusion: RelICL 提供了一种替代 DFS 的关系学习思路，在保持 DFS 优势的同时缓解特征爆炸与交互盲区问题，实验上达到了与最强 DFS 方法相当的性能。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve state-of-the-art performance on single-table tasks without any training. Recent work suggests that they are also well-suited for relational learning via deep feature synthesis (DFS), which flattens a relational schema into a single table by adding aggregates of the other tables' columns as features. This approach is appealing because it directly benefits from improvements to or customization of the underlying tabular foundation model. In this paper, we identify two key problems with DFS: feature explosion and interaction blindness. The first problem arises because the number of DFS features grows quickly as the schema becomes more complex, limiting scalability and performance. The second problem arises because column-wise aggregates do not account for feature interactions, limiting performance. We propose and explore an alternative method termed RelICL, which keeps the benefits of DFS but alleviates these two problems. At its heart, RelICL propagates and fuses information step by step through the schema graph, using the same tabular foundation model that is eventually used for prediction to do so. In our experimental study using RelBench tasks, RelICL was on par with the strongest approach based on deep feature synthesis.

</details>


### [269] [Removing spurious minima for planar features by skip connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01728)
*Jakob Paul Zimmermann,Moritz Grillo,Andrei Balakin,Georg Loho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究bias-free浅层ReLU网络在teacher-student设定下的高斯总体损失景观，发现加入可学习的线性skip连接可消除所有非负输出权重的伪局部极小值，而该伪极小值在无skip时可随任意过参数化持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解损失景观对解释神经网络训练至关重要，即使在简单模型中其结构也仅被部分理解。作者希望通过teacher-student设定下的浅层无偏置ReLU网络，为特征学习和过参数化等核心问题提供一个简单研究模型。

Method: 研究浅层无偏置ReLU网络在teacher-student设定下的高斯总体损失；对具有正输出权重与平面特征的teacher网络，分析加入可学习线性skip连接后，学生网络宽度至少不小于教师网络时伪局部极小值的消失情况；并构造反例说明无skip时伪极小值的持续存在。

Result: 加入线性skip后，当学生宽度不小于教师宽度时，所有非负学生输出权重下的伪局部极小值被消除；无skip时，构造出一个二维输入、仅三个隐藏神经元的固定teacher网络，其伪局部极小值在任意学生宽度下持续存在。此外，非负输出权重学生网络的局部极小点特征位于teacher特征张成的子空间中；二维ReLU网络中，正输出权重临界点的不同学生特征方向数至多为教师神经元数的两倍。

Conclusion: 可学习的线性skip能够消除任意过参数化下仍持续存在的伪极小值；学生网络的有效宽度受教师宽度控制，且良性结果可推广到任意给定半径参数球上的经验极小值，所需采样精度取决于该半径。

Abstract: Understanding loss landscapes is central to explaining neural-network training, yet their structure remains only partially understood even in simple models. We study the Gaussian population loss of shallow, bias-free ReLU networks in the teacher--student setting. This provides a simple model for studying essential aspects such as feature learning and overparameterization. For teacher networks with positive output weights and planar features, we show that including a learned linear skip removes all spurious local minima with non-negative student output weights once the student network is at least as wide as the teacher network. In contrast, without the skip, we construct a fixed teacher network with positive output weights and only three hidden neurons in input dimension two whose spurious local minima persist at every student width at least three. Thus, a learned linear skip can remove spurious minima that persist under arbitrary overparameterization. Furthermore, we show that a positive output weight student network always learns the subspace spanned by the teacher features: student features at local minima with non-negative student output weights lie in the span of the teacher features. For ReLU networks in two dimensions, even heavily overparameterized student networks have effective width controlled by the teacher width: every critical point with positive student output weights has at most twice as many distinct student feature directions as teacher neurons. Finally, we transfer the benignity result to empirical minima over parameter balls of any prescribed radius, with the required sampling accuracy depending on that radius.

</details>


### [270] [Function-Structured Reinforcement Learning with Executable Verifiers for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01729)
*Zihan Liu,Xurong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出函数结构化图强化学习（FSG-RL），将子问题图与Python实现结合，利用多验证器反馈优化数学推理。相比SFT，GRPO将最终答案准确率从43.25%提升至67.50%，完整解题成功率从32.25%提升至52.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法数学推理需要可靠的分解、计算与聚合。仅依靠最终答案奖励对中间错误指导不足，且成功执行代码并不保证数学正确性，因此需要更细粒度的监督与验证信号。

Method: 提出FSG-RL框架，连接子问题图与Python实现并引入多验证器反馈。策略先通过监督微调（SFT）学习从函数图生成代码，再用组相对策略优化（GRPO）结合答案门控奖励与跨度级信用分配进行优化，同时支持教师监督与结构化记忆。基准由GSM8K、MathQA、MATH和Omni-MATH构建，将公开函数图与私有验证规范配对。

Result: 在统一评估协议下，GRPO相比SFT将最终答案准确率从43.25%提升至67.50%，完整解题成功率从32.25%提升至52.25%；引入教师监督的持续强化学习可带来额外增益，且增益不仅限于生成格式正确的代码。

Conclusion: 结果表明验证器引导的强化学习对数学推理有效，能够超越单纯代码格式正确的层面，提升数学正确性。

Abstract: Algorithmic mathematical reasoning requires reliable decomposition, computation, and aggregation. Final-answer rewards provide limited guidance on intermediate errors, while successful execution does not guarantee mathematical correctness. This work proposes Function-Structured Graph Reinforcement Learning (FSG-RL), connecting subproblem graphs and Python implementations with multi-verifier feedback. The policy first learns to generate code from function graphs through supervised fine-tuning (SFT).
