We derive an exact variational formulation of this objective and connect its fixed-terminal-cost subproblem to stochastic optimal control and distributionally robust optimization. This formulation motivates an alternating algorithm that updates the adversarial initial distribution, estimates the terminal log-density ratio, and trains the controller. We develop Wasserstein and Sinkhorn variants using stochastic control optimality conditions to approximate the gradients required for adversarial updates. Experiments on two-dimensional transport tasks and image-to-image translation show improved robustness to input perturbations relative to standard SB, with a tradeoff in nominal performance. On Gaussian mixture transport, Sinkhorn DRSB also achieves lower mean sliced Wasserstein distance than fixed-level noise augmentation at both tested unseen noise levels.

</details>


### [306] [Foundations without Fundamentals: Zero-Shot Blind Spots in Time Series FMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02058)
*Nafiseh Ghoroghchian,Haipeng Zhang,Shuyi Han,Alex Labach,George Stein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SimpleTimeBench诊断基准，发现主流多元时间序列基础模型（Chronos-2、Moirai、Toto）在单调趋势、周期信号、领先指标等本应轻松预测的基本时序逻辑上仍频繁失败，且在真实传感器预测中持续低估协变量信息，揭示预训练规模与基础时序推理能力之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）虽在广泛基准上表现优异，但其内化基本时序逻辑（尤其是在外生协变量支持的场景下）的能力尚未被充分检验。作者希望探究这些模型是否真正具备捕捉简单可预测函数的归纳偏置。

Method: 作者提出SimpleTimeBench，一个包含单变量与多变量的"单元测试"式诊断套件，覆盖单调趋势、周期信号和领先指标协变量等基础原语场景，并在此基准上对Chronos-2、Moirai、Toto等主流多元TSFM进行零样本与微调评估，同时在真实世界传感器预测任务中验证。

Result: 主流多元TSFM在这些本应近乎完美预测的输入上常常给出次优的零样本预测；微调Chronos-2虽能改善特定任务表现，却会削弱其在其他基本模式上的能力，而非增强可泛化的基础能力。这些失败不仅存在于合成任务中，在真实传感器预测中也持续存在，模型始终未能充分利用观测协变量中的领先指标。

Conclusion: 当前TSFM可能缺乏捕捉简单可预测函数所需的归纳偏置，预训练规模与基础时序推理之间存在明显鸿沟；这种无法捕捉简单关系的能力缺陷限制了现有多元模型的实用性与可靠性。

Abstract: Despite the success of Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) on broad benchmarks, their ability to internalize basic temporal logic, especially in settings supported by exogenous covariates, remains under-examined. We introduce SimpleTimeBench, a diagnostic univariate and multivariate "unit test" suite for primitives such as monotonic trends, periodic signals and leading indicator covariates, scenarios where near-perfect forecasts should be trivial. Surprisingly, prominent multivariate TSFMs (Chronos-2, Moirai and Toto) frequently produce suboptimal zero-shot forecasts for these inputs. While fine-tuning Chronos-2 improves its behaviour on specific tasks, we show that this adaptation degrades performance on other fundamental patterns rather than enhancing its generalizable foundational capabilities. This reveals a gap between pre-training scale and basic temporal reasoning, suggesting that current TSFMs could potentially lack the inductive biases needed to capture simple predictable functions. We further demonstrate that these failures are not merely synthetic curiosities: they persist in real-world sensor forecasting, where TSFMs consistently underutilize leading indicators available in observed covariates. This inability to capture simple relationships limits the practical utility and reliability of current multivariate models.

</details>


### [307] [Learn the Directions, Normalize the Gains: Post-Training Normalization for LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02067)
*Zailong Tian,Yanzhe Chen,Zhuoheng Han,Houfeng Wang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现LoRA更新存在"适应失衡"问题，提出无需数据与训练的LoRA-Norm后处理方法，通过谱重平衡与核范数恢复来均衡奇异值增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然能高效实现任务特化，但其学习到的更新可能损害目标任务之外的能力。作者识别出"适应失衡"现象：少数奇异方向主导了训练更新，使性能对增益分配方式十分敏感。作者认为，学会"在哪里适应"并不能保证"适应增益被良好均衡"。

Method: 提出LoRA-Norm，一种后训练归一化方法，在保留已学习方向的同时重新平衡其增益。方法包含两个部分：谱重平衡（对奇异值施加固定的非线性变换）与核范数恢复（保持原始总谱质量）。该方法无需校准数据、无需额外训练，也不引入推理开销。

Result: 在两个骨干网络和三个适应任务上，LoRA-Norm提升了平均特化性能与能力保留度，在这两项指标上均优于所评估的后处理谱剪枝和梯度引导编辑配置。更强的函数均衡化并未带来一致的额外收益，说明"均衡适配器增益"与"均衡其响应"是两个不同的目标。

Conclusion: LoRA-Norm通过重新平衡奇异值增益，在不增加数据与推理成本的前提下同时改善特化性能与能力保留，表明适应增益均衡是LoRA后处理中的关键且独立于响应均衡的目标。

Abstract: While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient task specialization, its learned updates can compromise capabilities beyond the target task. We identify \textbf{adaptation imbalance}: a few singular directions dominate the trained update, leaving its performance sensitive to how gains are allocated. We argue that \textbf{learning where to adapt does not ensure that adaptation gains are well balanced}. This motivates \textbf{LoRA-Norm}, a post-training normalization method that retains learned directions while rebalancing their gains. LoRA-Norm combines spectral rebalancing, a fixed nonlinear transformation of singular values, with nuclear-norm restoration, which preserves the original total spectral mass. It requires no calibration data or additional training and introduces no inference overhead. Across two backbones and three adaptation tasks, LoRA-Norm improves average specialization and capability retention, outperforming the evaluated post-hoc spectral pruning and gradient-guided editing configurations on both measures. Stronger functional equalization brings no consistent additional gains, revealing that balancing adapter gains and equalizing their responses are distinct objectives.

</details>


### [308] [Are We Recovering Mechanisms? Objective-Level Recovery Gaps in Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02098)
*Chuqin Geng,Li Zhang,Haolin Ye,Mark Zhang,Luke Zhang,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 干预定义的忠实度可能偏好行为复现更差的等大电路，形成"目标层面恢复鸿沟"，仅靠更好的发现算法无法解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动电路发现常被框定为搜索问题，假设评估目标能找到更好的电路。但作者质疑这一假设：评估目标本身可能无法识别更优电路。

Method: 在四个人类参考任务和InterpBench上，对比验证忠实度与固定重采样下留出提示的行为表现；使用对照参考编辑揭示误排序，并测试EAP、EAP-IG、ACDC、Edge-SP四种方法；通过从接收方完整模型执行恢复被排除信号来验证"上下文扭曲"假说。

Result: KL在人类参考任务上误排9.4%-41.2%的候选电路对，四种方法均出现该失败；恢复选定信号后，修复了发现池中100个持续KL误排序中的96个（验证和留出提示上均如此），且电路及其原行为分数不变。

Conclusion: 更好的发现算法本身不足以解决问题，当评估目标奖励错误候选时，需要修正目标本身；上下文扭曲是这一恢复鸿沟的重要机制。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to recover the internal computations responsible for model behavior. Progress in automated circuit discovery is often framed as a search problem: better attribution or optimization should identify better mechanisms. This assumes that the evaluation objective can recognize a better circuit once it is found. We show that intervention-defined faithfulness can instead prefer an equally sized circuit that reproduces the model's behavior less well, creating an objective-level recovery gap. Across four human-reference tasks and InterpBench, we compare validation faithfulness with behavior on held-out prompts under fixed ordinary resampling. The behavioral criterion is agreement with the intact model, including its mistakes, except on Greater-Than, where we use semantic accuracy. Controlled reference edits reveal misranking without any discovery algorithm, and outputs of EAP, EAP-IG, ACDC, and Edge-SP exhibit the same failure. Under resampling, KL misranks 9.4%-41.2% of candidate pairs across these methods on the human-reference tasks. We investigate context distortion as an explanation: replacing excluded signals changes the inputs on which retained components operate. Restoring selected signals from the recipient's intact-model execution repairs 96 of 100 persistent KL misrankings from the discovery pool on both validation and held-out prompts. The circuits and their original behavioral scores remain unchanged. These findings show why better discovery alone is insufficient when its objective rewards the wrong candidate.

</details>


### [309] [Local Support Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02126)
*Assaf Ben-Kish,Akarsh Kumar,James Glass,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部支撑学习（LSL）框架，通过为权重适配器加装GMM门控函数，使更新仅作用于当前训练分布，从而在无历史数据条件下缓解大模型灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型在新任务上微调时会出现灾难性遗忘。作者从几何视角将遗忘视为每个权重矩阵输入空间中的几何问题，发现在这种自然的保持目标下，基于梯度的优化器产生的更新是次优的，因此需要新的训练机制来兼顾新知识学习与旧能力保持，且不依赖历史数据。

Method: 提出Local Support Learning（LSL）：在训练新阶段时组合两个组件，一是按常规方式最小化损失进行训练的权重适配器，二是门控函数，仅在来自其自身训练分布的输入激活上启用适配器，使更新局限在该分布上。为解决门控需覆盖所有学习阶段数据而只能接触当前阶段数据的难题，采用高斯混合模型（GMM）构建门控，其似然在远离训练数据时快速衰减，从而自然倾向于对旧阶段数据关闭。该方法为训练后处理方式。

Result: 在参数量最高70亿的大语言模型上，LSL能够解决遗忘问题，在多个训练阶段中同时保持预训练能力与微调能力，并且在内存与计算上高效、对超参数选择稳健，并展现出扩展潜力。

Conclusion: LSL是一种通用、无需历史数据的框架，通过GMM门控实现局部化更新，可有效缓解大模型多阶段训练中的灾难性遗忘，兼具效率、稳健性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We explore catastrophic forgetting in the context of large pre-trained models. By considering forgetting as a geometric problem in the input space of each weight matrix, we uncover a natural retention objective under which updates produced by gradient-based optimizers are suboptimal. Following this observation, we propose Local Support Learning (LSL), a general-purpose framework that augments gradient-based training for retention of prior capabilities without access to prior data. During a new learning phase, LSL pairs two components with distinct roles: a standard weight adapter, trained as usual to minimize the loss, and a gating function that enables the adapter only on input activations from its own training distribution, making the update local to that distribution. The key challenge is that this gate must route data from all learning phases while training only on data from the current one. We address this with a gate based on a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), whose likelihood decays rapidly away from its training data, giving it a natural tendency to stay closed on data from prior phases. We show that this post-training approach can resolve forgetting in LLMs of up to 7 billion parameters, retaining both pretrained and finetuned capabilities across multiple training phases, while being efficient in memory and compute, robust to hyperparameter choice, and showing scaling potential.

</details>


### [310] [Linear Programming Representations and Strongly Polynomial Algorithms for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02131)
*Han Zhong,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为具有有理多面体状态-动作矩形不确定性的鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDPs）构建了单一线性规划表示，并给出强多项式算法及复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究RMDPs的LP表示与强多项式算法，针对奖励和转移中具有有理多面体状态-动作矩形不确定性的情形，改进已有复杂度界并拓展到更广泛的不确定性集合。

Method: 通过编码有限序列的鲁棒策略迭代步骤，构造单一LP，其最优解可恢复鲁棒最优值和所有最优平稳随机策略；并发展鲁棒策略迭代的一般复杂度分析，结合不确定集最小化代价与策略评估所需迭代次数。

Result: 在固定折扣下，LP具有多项式维度和编码长度，可在强多项式时间内构造；改进ℓ1和ℓ∞ RMDPs的已知复杂度界，并为一般区间、加权ℓ1和Wasserstein RMDPs以及相应不确定性集的轮次随机博弈建立新的强多项式界。

Conclusion: 该工作为广泛类型的RMDPs提供了统一的LP表示与强多项式算法框架，显著拓展了可高效求解的鲁棒决策问题范围。

Abstract: We study linear programming (LP) representations and strongly polynomial algorithms for robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) with rational polyhedral state-action rectangular uncertainty in rewards and transitions. By encoding a finite sequence of robust policy-iteration steps, we construct a single LP whose optimal solutions recover the robust optimal value and all optimal stationary randomized policies. At fixed discount, the LP has polynomial dimension and encoding length and can be constructed in strongly polynomial time. We also develop a general complexity analysis of robust policy iteration that combines the cost of minimizing over uncertainty sets with the number of iterations needed to evaluate a policy. For a fixed discount factor, we use this analysis to improve the known complexity bounds for $\ell_1$ and $\ell_\infty$ RMDPs and establish new strongly polynomial bounds for general interval, weighted $\ell_1$, and Wasserstein RMDPs, as well as turn-based stochastic games with these uncertainty sets.

</details>


### [311] [Finetuning with Sampling: SFT Learns Better Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02140)
*Aayush Karan,Sitan Chen,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种MCMC采样算法，将离策略（off-policy）数据逐步转化为更接近在策略（on-policy）的数据分布，使SFT能够兼具在策略学习的泛化优势与离策略数据的信息优势，在多项任务上媲美甚至超越主流后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型引入新能力主要依赖后训练中的SFT与RL：传统观点认为RL泛化强、遗忘少，但只能靠模型自身反复采样成功轨迹；SFT可从离策略专家数据中学习，却泛化弱且易灾难性遗忘。作者希望同时利用在策略学习的优势与离策略数据中的特权信息，但不修改学习目标，而是调整数据分布以适配学习器。

Method: 提出一种马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样算法。在给定参考模型用于微调的条件下，该算法逐步将离策略轨迹转化为更接近在策略的分布，从而在不改变学习目标的前提下，使SFT能够从经过分布塑造的数据中学习。

Result: 在科学技能习得、数学推理和开放式专业知识等任务上，该采样算法使SFT能够媲美主流后训练技术，且相比强在策略基线往往泛化更好、遗忘更少。微调后的模型表现出良好的分布性能，并能在锐化基础模型分布之外进行学习。

Conclusion: 该工作将采样提升为一种模型原生的算子，用于塑造数据的可学习性，可作为通用原语贯穿整个后训练流程，具有更广泛的适用价值。

Abstract: Introducing new capabilities to frontier models has long been the goal of posttraining, which predominantly employs supervised finetuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) to this end. Conventional wisdom dictates that RL enables strong generalization on new tasks without losing existing capabilities, while SFT is prone to weak generalization and catastrophic forgetting. At the same time, SFT can learn from off-policy expert data, whereas RL must rely on a model's ability to find successful trajectories with repeated sampling. In our work, we seek to leverage the strength of on-policy learning while utilizing the privileged information contained in off-policy data. However, rather than modifying the learning objective to accommodate this data, we instead tailor the data distribution to better suit the learner. We introduce a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling algorithm that progressively transforms off-policy traces to be more on-policy given a reference model for finetuning. Across tasks like scientific skill acquisition, mathematical reasoning, and open-ended expertise, our sampling algorithm enables SFT to rival prevailing posttraining techniques, often generalizing better and forgetting less than strong on-policy baselines. In addition, the resulting finetuned models exhibit strong distributional performance and are capable of learning beyond sharpening the base model distribution. At a higher level, our approach presents sampling as a model-native operator that shapes data for learnability, offering broader utility as a general-purpose primitive throughout the posttraining stack.

</details>


### [312] [Faynt: Scaling and Optimizing Policies for Competitive Melee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02144)
*Ali Janati,Nikita Kuzmin,Rohit Swamy,Charles Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Faynt系列Transformer策略（10M和75M参数），单一检查点控制《任天堂明星大乱斗》全部26个角色，经强化学习后10M在同角色对局中胜率达98.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在用统一策略网络覆盖全角色，探索Transformer架构、优化、缩放及超参数迁移，并结合人类回放预训练与强化学习提升对战表现。

Method: 在约84万人类回放上预训练；后训练结合排名与结果课程、75M到10M蒸馏，及限定Fox镜像对局的强化学习；对手含21-24帧延迟专家及零延迟Slippi-AI。

Result: 10M对14个释放版本取得240/244（98.4%）胜率；对零延迟模型68局全胜。监督10M在152局基准胜率69.7%，高于45.4%的预训练75M；加权验证损失与胜率排序一致；T4上推理5.2ms/8.7ms。

Conclusion: 单一检查点多角色控制可行且性能优异，缩放与蒸馏有效，加权验证损失可指导检查点选择；开源权重、基准与自动化锦标赛平台。

Abstract: We introduce Faynt, a family of 10M- and 75M-parameter Transformer policies for Super Smash Bros. Melee, each controlling all 26 characters with a single checkpoint. After reinforcement learning (RL), the 10M wins 240 of 244 same-character games (98.4%) against fourteen specialist and multi-character releases on their supported rosters, with a winning record against every release. These opponents retain 21- or 24-frame action delays; Faynt uses no added delay, and we have not isolated the effect of this difference. In a separate evaluation against a privately supplied zero-delay Slippi-AI model, the 10M wins all 68 games across two conditioning settings. We study architecture, optimization, scaling, and hyperparameter transfer to guide pretraining on approximately 840,000 human replays. Post-training combines rank- and outcome-based curricula, 75M-to-10M distillation, and RL restricted to Fox mirror matches. On the initial 152-game benchmark, the supervised 10M wins 69.7% of games, compared with 45.4% for the pretrained 75M, despite higher overall held-out controller-prediction loss. The weighted validation loss used for supervised checkpoint selection agrees with the win-rate ordering of all four pretrained and supervised policies. After supervised post-training, both models take less damage per minute, build larger early leads, and win more often after losing the first life. Optimized inference on recorded game states averages 5.2 ms per decision for the 10M and 8.7 ms for the 75M on an NVIDIA T4, excluding emulator execution and communication. We open-source the weights, both benchmark suites, and a platform for automated model tournaments.

</details>


### [313] [Muon meets Tamed Langevin: Momentum Preconditioning beyond Convex and gradient-Lipschitz Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02158)
*Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸、非全局梯度Lipschitz势能下矩阵空间Gibbs分布的采样问题，提出一类非二次动能构造的新型欠阻尼Langevin系统，证明其保持目标测度不变、以加权全变差距离指数收敛，并给出时间一致矩界的Euler-Maruyama离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从矩阵空间上的Gibbs分布采样在统计与机器学习中很重要，但现有方法多依赖势能的凸性或全局梯度Lipschitz假设，这些假设在实际中常不成立，因此需要在更弱的势能假设下设计可证明收敛且数值稳定的采样动力学。

Method: 引入一族非二次动能函数，构造带有动量预条件（preconditioning）的新型欠阻尼Langevin系统，其中动能梯度充当动量的光滑谱驯化（spectral taming）项；在松弛的势能假设下分析动力学的测度不变性与收敛性，并研究其Euler-Maruyama离散化。

Result: 证明所得动力学保持目标Gibbs测度不变，并在加权全变差距离下达到指数收敛到平衡；同时证明对应的Euler-Maruyama离散化在不对势能梯度作任何修改的情况下具有时间一致的矩界，保证了采样算法的稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过非二次动能与动量预条件机制，可在非凸、非全局梯度Lipschitz势能下实现理论可证且数值稳定的Gibbs采样，为处理矩阵空间上更一般的分布采样提供了新思路。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sampling from Gibbs distributions on matrix spaces whose potential energies are neither convex nor globally gradient-Lipschitz. We introduce a family of non-quadratic kinetic energies that lead to a new underdamped Langevin system with momentum preconditioning, in which the gradient of the kinetic energy acts as a smooth spectral taming of the momentum. We prove that, under these relaxed assumptions on the potential, the resulting dynamics leaves the target Gibbs measure invariant, and we establish exponential convergence to equilibrium in a weighted total variation distance. Finally, we show that the corresponding Euler-Maruyama discretization admits moment bounds that are uniform in time, without any modification of the potential gradient, which ensures the stability of the resulting sampling algorithm.

</details>


### [314] [When Do Intrinsic Rewards Lead to Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02159)
*Scott W. Viteri,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以"反事实信息"为准则衡量探索，指出常见内在奖励（计数、预测误差、赋能、信息增益）在特定环境下可能帕累托次优，并给出成功条件及一个新目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内在奖励（如预测误差、学习进度）旨在引导强化学习中的探索，但最大化这些奖励并不一定产生最具信息量的经验。作者希望建立一个更严格的探索评价标准，以判断探索策略是否真正获取了最有价值的信息。

Method: 提出一个正式准则：通过"反事实信息"比较策略，即考察一个策略所获得的历史能在多大程度上替代其他替代策略下的经验。构建一个单一而简单的环境，在该环境中让计数型、预测误差型、赋能型与信息增益型目标的最优策略在反事实信息获取上呈现帕累托次优。分析这些失败的原因，并推导现有内在奖励成功鼓励最优探索的条件。此外，构建一个新目标函数，当探索在本准则下严格改进时赋予更高价值。

Result: 在构造的简单环境中，指定类型的计数、预测误差、赋能与信息增益目标均存在最大化策略，在获取反事实信息方面是帕累托次优的。作者解释了这些失败机制，并给出内在奖励能够成功促进最优探索的条件；同时提出了一个与反事实信息准则一致的新目标函数。

Conclusion: 以反事实信息作为探索的评价标准可揭示现有内在奖励的局限；现有方法仅在特定条件下才能实现最优探索，而基于该准则设计的新目标函数能更好地激励真正有信息量的探索。

Abstract: Intrinsic rewards are designed to guide exploration in reinforcement learning by assigning value to an agent's experience, for example through prediction error or learning progress. However, maximizing these rewards need not produce the most informative experience available. We propose a formal criterion for exploration that compares policies by the counterfactual information they acquire: how well their histories can substitute for experience under alternative policies. We construct a single, simple environment in which specified count-based, prediction-error, empowerment, and information-gain objectives have maximizing policies that are Pareto-suboptimal at acquiring counterfactual information. We explain these failures and establish conditions under which existing intrinsic rewards successfully encourage optimal exploration. We also construct an objective that assigns a higher value whenever exploration strictly improves under our criterion.

</details>


### [315] [Every Ablation Is a Dose: Counterweights and the Semblance of Self-Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02173)
*Areeb Ahmad,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“自修复”现象可由消融前已存在的增益（counterweight）解释，符合仿射定律 E_r(λ)=own_r+γ_rλ，并在多模型任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型某组件被消融后，其他组件常调整补偿，即“自修复”，但机制不明。此前最系统的研究认为自修复是噪声性的、难有单一解释。本文主张其有统一解释：消融前已存在的增益。

Method: 将任何对因果重要组件的干预视为反事实对比强度坐标轴 λ 上的点，传统消融是未校准的点。对细粒度单元 r，检验因果修复响应是否服从仿射定律 E_r(λ)=own_r+γ_rλ，其中斜率 γ_r 为固定系数，其符号决定单元抵消还是强化被移除信号。并在事实判定任务上跨 Gemma、Qwen、LLaMA、Mistral 四模型识别 MLP 神经元、OV 神经元及奇异方向；在 GPT-2 Small 的 IOI 电路上检验十个头中的干预可达部分。

Result: 事实判定任务中，81 个下游方向有 68 个符合仿射定律；可从固定权重预测 γ_r 的大小。IOI 电路中十个头里有七个干预可及者符合该定律，且全部七个都是反权重（counterweights）。

Conclusion: 所谓自修复，可能只是反权重在对比信号出现于核心时执行其常规操作；该现象可由消融前已存在的增益统一解释，而非噪声性多因素现象。

Abstract: Ablate a component of a language model, and other components often appear to adjust and compensate. This phenomenon, termed self-repair, has been observed repeatedly, but its mechanism remains unclear. The most systematic study to date concluded that self-repair is noisy and unlikely to have a single explanation. We argue that it has one: a gain already present before any ablation. Any intervention on a causally important component can be viewed as a point on a coordinate axis $λ$, the signed strength of a counterfactual contrast. Hence, conventional ablation methods are uncalibrated points on this axis. We show that the causal repair response for a fine-grained unit $r$ is governed by an affine law, $E_r(λ)=\mathrm{own}_r+γ_rλ$. The slope $γ_r$ is a fixed coefficient that consistently influences the model, with or without ablation, and its sign determines whether the unit counteracts or reinforces the removed signal. On a factual-verdict task across four models from distinct families (Gemma, Qwen, LLaMA, and Mistral), we identify components including MLP neurons, OV neurons, and singular directions that follow this affine law, 68 of 81 downstream directions in all. Moreover, we can anticipate the magnitude of $γ_r$ from the fixed weights. On the IOI circuit of GPT-2 Small, seven of the ten heads the intervention can reach follow the law, and all seven are counterweights. From this perspective, what may appear as self-repair is a counterweight performing its usual operation when the contrastive signal emerges at the core.

</details>


### [316] [Effective Resistance and Graph Neural Network Reliability in Tissue-Specific Interactomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02175)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究组织特异性互作网络中的有效电阻（effective resistance）残差是否能作为判定蛋白质功能注释预测可信度的信号。结果显示该残差确实携带超出常规控制变量的额外预测误差信息，且随深度增强，但受度退化限制，对选择性预测的改进有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质功能注释不仅需要模型给出预测，还需要知道哪些预测不应被信任。作者希望找到一种能指示预测可信度的结构信号，并检验组织特异性互作结构是否携带该信息。

Method: 以有效电阻为候选信号，在24个组织特异性互作组中分析其性质；发现其被逆度主导，随共表达过滤网络增长而退化（Spearman相关-0.955）。将残差与度保持零模型比较，并在控制预测熵、度、注释基数、局部结构和仅特征难度后，检验残差对留出网络中每节点损失的额外解释力。

Result: 24个网络中残差对度保持零图的偏离均超阈值；在扣除置换下限后，19/24留出网络中残差可额外解释每节点损失，且在所有深度上成立并随深度单调增强。增量达到控制变量未解释方差的0.37%，为置换下限的5.6倍（而度保持零世界重训模型仅1.5倍）。

Conclusion: 组织特异性互作结构中的有效电阻残差是可复现的可信度信号，但其作用受度退化限制；对选择性预测的改进可忽略不计。

Abstract: Protein function annotation needs to know which predictions to distrust, not only what a model predicts. We ask whether tissue-specific interaction structure carries that information. Our candidate signal is effective resistance, used previously to relieve over-squashing by rewiring. Across 24 tissue-specific interactomes it is dominated by inverse degree, and the degeneration deepens as the co-expression filtered network grows, with a Spearman correlation of -0.955. The residual departure from that limit exceeds degree-preserving null graphs in all 24 networks. Controlling for predictive entropy, degree, annotation cardinality, local structure and feature-only difficulty, the residual explains additional per-node loss in 19 of 24 held-out networks once a permutation floor is subtracted, at every depth, and the effect strengthens monotonically with depth. The increment reaches 0.37% of the variance the controls leave unexplained, 5.6 times a permutation floor, against 1.5 times when the model is retrained in a degree-preserving null world. Selective prediction improves negligibly. The signal is reproducible; degree degeneration bounds it.

</details>


### [317] [From Gradients to Capabilities: Understanding Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02179)
*Siqi Zhu,Suozhi Huang,Kaixuan Zhang,Yuheng Yang,Zhanyang Jin,Yihang Sun,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多教师在线策略蒸馏(MOPD)中教师信号如何影响参数变化，基于Qwen3-1.7B与四个RL训练的领域教师进行梯度、优化器更新与任务学习曲线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOPD旨在将多个RL训练教师的优势整合到单一学生模型中，但教师信号如何影响参数变化尚缺乏深入探究。

Method: 以Qwen3-1.7B为对象，使用四个从同一初始化用RL训练的领域教师，比较梯度、优化器更新与任务学习曲线，并辅以SmolLM3-3B诊断。

Result: 发现四类因素影响教师信号：(1)损失平均隐式加权响应，token平均偏好更长响应；(2)Adam一阶矩缩小参数更新差异，教师间余弦相似度0.83，平均规则间0.96；(3)BF16舍入掩盖微小变化，FP32主权重约97%偏离初始化而BF16仅7-11%；(4)top-64交集KL梯度接近全词表梯度，但任务表现依赖平均方式，数学准确率在响应平均下比采样token PG高2.6分，在全局token平均下低2.1分。

Conclusion: 教师信号受损失平均、Adam一阶矩、BF16舍入及词表裁剪等多因素影响，其效果依赖于平均规则的选择。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) aims to combine the strengths of RL-trained teachers in a single student, but how teacher signals affect parameter changes remains underexplored. We study Qwen3-1.7B with four domain teachers trained with RL from the same initialization as the student, comparing gradients, optimizer updates, and task learning curves, with additional SmolLM3-3B diagnostics. We find that several factors influence teacher signals. First, loss averaging implicitly weights responses: token averaging favors longer responses, and equalizing domain contributions retains this weighting within domains. Second, Adam's first moment reduces differences in parameter updates: the cosine similarity is 0.83 between teachers and 0.96 between averaging rules, despite differences in raw gradients. Third, BF16 rounding hides small changes: about 97\% of FP32 master weights differ from initialization, but only 7--11\% of BF16 weights do. Finally, the top-64 intersection KL gradient closely matches Qwen's full-vocabulary gradient, but the effect on task performance depends on averaging: mathematics accuracy is 2.6 points higher than with sampled-token policy-gradient (PG) under response averaging and 2.1 points lower under global token averaging.

</details>


### [318] [SoftServe: A Scalable Quasi-Newton Method for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02182)
*Joohwan Ko,Tetiana Parshakova,Diana Cai,Robert M. Gower*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftServe系列拟牛顿方法，通过变分目标导出正定曲率估计，并用Newton-Schulz迭代替代矩阵分解，使其无需线搜索即可扩展至超大规模神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟牛顿方法在大型无约束凸优化中高效，但用于深度学习受限于非凸性和巨大参数量两大障碍。现有方法依赖线搜索或临时曲率修正，难以直接适用。作者希望设计无需线搜索与启发式修正、又能保留正定性的拟牛顿方案。

Method: SoftServe从Berglund等人（2025）的变分目标中推导正定曲率估计，即使存在负曲率也能获得正定估计。作者开发了对角与Kronecker分解两种变体，通过构造本身保证正定性，并可扩展至大型神经网络。矩阵运算采用稳定的耦合Newton-Schulz迭代，以GPU友好的矩阵乘法替代昂贵的矩阵分解。

Result: 在严重病态问题上表现优异，包括循环网络、深度自编码器、物理信息神经网络以及一个1.36亿参数的物理信息扩散模型，其损失往往低于Adam、Muon和SOAP等已有基线。

Conclusion: SoftServe证明无需线搜索或临时曲率修正，也能在大规模非凸优化中实现有效拟牛顿方法，尤其适合严重病态场景，为深度学习优化提供了新的可扩展选择。

Abstract: Quasi-Newton (QN) methods have long been among the most effective methods for large-scale unconstrained convex optimization. Two obstacles have limited their use in deep learning: non-convexity and enormous parameter sizes. We introduce SoftServe, a family of QN methods designed to overcome these obstacles without line searches or ad hoc curvature corrections. SoftServe derives positivedefinite curvature estimates from the variational objective of Berglund et al. (2025), even in the presence of negative curvature. We develop diagonal and Kroneckerfactored variants that preserve positive definiteness by construction and scale to massive neural networks. Finally, SoftServe relies on the stable coupled Newton-Schulz iteration for the required matrix operations, replacing costly matrix decompositions with GPU-friendly matrix multiplications. SoftServe excels on problems that are severely ill-conditioned, including tasks such as recurrent networks, deep autoencoders, physics-informed neural networks, and a 136M-parameter physics-informed diffusion model, often achieving lower losses than established baselines including Adam, Muon, and SOAP.

</details>


### [319] [Decoding Looped Transformers Better for (Almost) Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02185)
*Weihao Liu,Huangjie Zheng,Tianrong Chen,Rohit Dilip,Richard He Bai,Yizhu Jiao,Yuyang Wang,Ruixiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoopCD，一种无需训练的对比解码框架，利用循环 Transformer 中早期循环状态作为弱预测，与最终强预测对比来引导 token 选择，在多个模型上显著提升性能并减少推理计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer 通过重复执行共享块实现参数效率，每次循环都会产生可解码同一 next token 的中间表示。但标准解码会丢弃早期状态。由于早期循环计算量更少，循环结构天然提供了对齐的"弱-强"预测对，无需辅助模型或额外训练，因此作者希望利用这一特性改进解码。

Method: 提出 LoopCD，一种无需训练的对比解码框架：将最终预测与较早的循环传递进行对比来引导 token 选择。提供两种实现方式：(1) LoopCD-Logits，在 logit 空间操作，需额外一次输出传递；(2) LoopCD-Hidden，在隐藏状态空间操作，零输出开销。

Result: 在四种循环 Transformer 家族上，LoopCD 在完整循环深度下带来稳定且显著的增益：LoopCD-Logits 将 Ouro-2.6B-Thinking 在 AIME 2024 上的 pass@1 从 61.88% 提升至 73.33%；LoopCD-Hidden 将 Huginn 在 HumanEval 上的 pass@1 从 22.56% 提升至 31.71%。这些增益使循环次数减半仍能匹配或超越完整深度未引导的基线，前向 FLOPs 减少 22.5%–48.2%。

Conclusion: 通过将中间循环状态转化为有效的引导信号，LoopCD 在显著降低推理计算的同时实现了更优的解码质量。

Abstract: Looped Transformers achieve parameter efficiency by repeatedly executing a shared block across recurrent loops. Each loop yields an intermediate representation decodable for the same next token, yet standard decoding discards earlier states. Because earlier loops embody less computation, recurrence inherently supplies aligned weak-and-strong prediction pairs without auxiliary models or external training. We introduce LoopCD, a training-free contrastive decoding framework that guides token selection by contrasting the final prediction with an earlier recurrent pass, operating either in logit space with one extra output pass (LoopCD-Logits) or in hidden-state space with zero output overhead (LoopCD-Hidden). Across four looped Transformer families, LoopCD delivers substantial, consistent gains at full recurrent depth: LoopCD-Logits raises Ouro-2.6B-Thinking's AIME 2024 pass@1 from 61.88% to 73.33%, while LoopCD-Hidden lifts Huginn's HumanEval pass@1 from 22.56% to 31.71%. Crucially, these performance gains enable halving the number of recurrent loops while still matching or exceeding full-depth unguided baselines, reducing forward FLOPs by 22.5% to 48.2%. By transforming intermediate recurrent states into effective guidance signals, LoopCD achieves superior decoding quality while substantially reducing inference compute.

</details>


### [320] [Higher-Order Molecular Grammars for Generative and Foundation Models in Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02186)
*Yiming Huang,Yujie Zeng,Vijay Prakash Dwivedi,Simone Foti,Jianmin Wang,Jure Leskovec,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出高阶文法表示（HGR），将分子提升为组合复形并用上下文无关高阶文法解析为产生式规则序列，实现高效的高阶拓扑建模，同时构建RingDiv基准和RDI指标，在生成与表示学习任务上均取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准序列和图表示难以显式编码高阶拓扑（如环系统与重复基元）；现有多阶表示虽能直接刻画这些结构，但计算代价高且难以解码为有效分子。同时基准存在偏向简单环系统的偏差。

Method: 1) HGR框架：将分子提升为组合复形（combinatorial complexes），在上下文无关高阶文法下将每个复形解析为紧凑的产生式规则序列，使高阶拓扑可被标准序列模型直接处理，避免显式高阶编码开销并保持拓扑表达能力；2) 构建RingDiv基准（118万分子，含RingDiv300k子集），并提出环多样性指数（RDI）量化环系统覆盖。

Result: 生成任务：基于HGR的模型天然保证100%有效性，同时分布对齐领先，在全部五个生成基准上FCD排名第一。表示学习：HGR-FM在七项MoleculeNet基准上，在probing与全微调两种迁移协议下均取得最高平均AUC，分别较最强基线提升8.3和3.3个AUC点。

Conclusion: HGR是一种高效的高阶表示方法，可同时服务于分子生成与可迁移表示学习，并通过RingDiv与RDI缓解基准对简单环系统的偏置。

Abstract: Molecular learning models are strongly shaped by their underlying representations. Yet standard sequential and graph formalisms struggle to explicitly encode higher-order topology, such as ring systems and recurring motifs. Existing higher-order representations can capture these structures directly, but they are often computationally demanding and difficult to decode into valid molecules. Here, we introduce Higher-order Grammar Representation (HGR), a principled, topology-aware framework that lifts molecules to combinatorial complexes and parses each complex into a compact sequence of production rules under a context-free higher-order grammar. By serialising higher-order topology into rule sequences, HGR makes these structures directly compatible with standard sequence models, avoiding the computational overhead of explicit higher-order encodings while preserving topological expressiveness. To reduce benchmark bias towards simple ring systems, we construct RingDiv, a ring-enriched benchmark containing 1.18 million molecules, including the curated RingDiv300k subset, and introduce the ring diversity index (RDI) to quantify ring-system coverage. In molecular generation, HGR-based models uniquely combine 100% validity by construction with leading distributional alignment, ranking first in FCD on all five generation benchmarks. In representation learning, HGR-FM achieves the highest mean AUC across seven MoleculeNet benchmarks under both transfer protocols, improving on the strongest baseline by 8.3 and 3.3 AUC points under probing and full fine-tuning, respectively. Collectively, these results establish HGR as an efficient higher-order representation for molecular generation and transferable representation learning.

</details>


### [321] [Generative modeling of intrinsically disordered protein regions by reinforcing sparse autoencoder features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02189)
*Jason X. Liu,Sebastian Ibarraran,Frank Hu,Soojung Yang,Xinyu A. Feng,Abigail Park,Anagha Aneesh,Lacramioara Bintu,Alexander R. Dunn,Grant M. Rotskoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IDiom是一个在5400万条预测IDR上训练的自回归蛋白质语言模型，结合RL-SAE后训练方法，可通过稀疏自编码器特征实现可解释、可组合的IDR功能设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在无序蛋白区域（IDRs）在转录调控、信号转导和亚细胞定位等过程中作用关键，但其功能设计困难：基于结构的设计方法不适用于IDRs，而现有蛋白质语言模型在全长序列上训练，其先验偏向折叠结构域。因此亟需面向IDRs的专用生成与设计方法。

Method: 作者构建了IDiom——一个在IDiom-DB（从AlphaFold数据库整理的5400万条预测IDR）上训练的自回归蛋白质语言模型。为控制与功能相关的序列模式，提出RL-SAE：一种利用稀疏自编码器特征进行强化学习的后训练方法，奖励生成能够激活指定特征集的序列。

Result: IDiom生成的序列具有多样性，能再现天然IDR的组成、模式、基序及预测无序度。在8个IDR设计任务中，RL-SAE生成的序列平均激活30个目标特征中的90%，而激活引导（activation steering）仅为24%。RL-SAE还提升了生成IDR的预测亚细胞定位和转录活性，并可将不同生物功能相关特征组合在单条序列中。

Conclusion: IDiom与RL-SAE通过显式控制功能相关序列特征，实现了可解释、可组合的IDR设计；RL-SAE有望推广至其他可提供可解释特征作为设计目标的蛋白质设计场景。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered protein regions (IDRs) play central roles in cellular processes such as transcriptional regulation, signal transduction, and subcellular localization, yet their functional design remains challenging. Structure-based design methods do not readily apply to IDRs, and existing protein language models are trained on full-length protein sequences, thus learning a prior that is biased towards folded domains. Here, we present IDiom, an autoregressive protein language model trained on IDiom-DB, a dataset of 54 million predicted IDRs curated from the AlphaFold Database. IDiom generates diverse sequences that recapitulate the composition, patterning, motifs, and predicted disorder of natural IDRs. To control function-associated sequence patterns, we also introduce reinforcement learning with sparse autoencoder features (RL-SAE), a post-training method that rewards the generation of sequences that activate specified feature sets. Across eight IDR design tasks, RL-SAE sequences activate, on average, 90% of 30 targeted features, compared to 24% for activation steering. We demonstrate that RL-SAE improves the predicted subcellular localization and transcriptional activity of generated IDRs compared to steering and supervised fine-tuning, and enables features associated with distinct biological functions to be combined within individual sequences. Thus, IDiom and RL-SAE enable interpretable and composable IDR design through explicit control of function-associated sequence features. More broadly, RL-SAE could extend to other protein design settings where interpretable features provide useful design targets. Code is available at https://github.com/rotskoff-group/idiom.

</details>


### [322] [Trust the Direction, Search the Step: Zero-and-First-Order Methods for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02190)
*Cristian McGee,El Houcine Bergou,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZFO框架，将方向选择与步长解耦：由信任的一阶优化器确定下降方向，再沿该一维子空间做少量零阶函数评估来选取曲率感知的自适应步长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经网络优化中步长选择是关键难题：步长保守会导致收敛缓慢，步长激进则可能引起训练不稳定。传统线搜索代价较高，需要一种更轻量且自适应的步长选择方式。

Method: ZFO结合零阶与一阶优化思路：利用一阶优化器给出搜索方向，仅沿该一维方向进行零阶评估。借助当前梯度信息和两次额外目标函数评估，构造方向上的局部目标模型，并在有界搜索区间内选择曲率感知的步长，其开销低于完整线搜索。

Result: 理论上证明共享样本评估可给出可靠的有限差分曲率估计，局部模型能在搜索区间内选出近似最优步长，且ZFO收敛到驻点邻域。实验覆盖多种语言模型与数据集，ZFO相较固定步长一阶基线常能改善优化过程与最终性能，改善幅度及最优局部模型依目标函数而异。

Conclusion: ZFO以低成本实现自适应步长选择，兼顾收敛速度与稳定性，具有良好的理论保证与实证效果，且代码已公开。

Abstract: Step-size selection remains a central challenge in large-scale neural network optimization; conservative steps slow convergence, while aggressive steps can destabilize it. We combine \textbf{Z}ero-and-\textbf{F}irst-\textbf{O}rder optimization~(ZFO) and propose a lightweight framework that decouples direction selection from step-size. ZFO uses a trusted first-order optimizer to determine the direction and performs zeroth-order evaluations only along this one-dimensional subspace to choose how far to move. Using the current {gradient information} and two additional objective function evaluations, ZFO instances construct a local model of the objective function along the proposed direction and select a curvature-aware step within a bounded search interval. This yields an adaptive step-selection mechanism that costs less than a full line search. We provide theoretical guarantees to show that shared-sample evaluations produce reliable finite-difference curvature estimates, that the induced local model selects a near-optimal step along the search interval, and that ZFO converges to a neighborhood of a stationary point. Across the evaluated settings, language models and datasets, ZFO frequently improves optimization and final performance relative to fixed-step first-order baselines, with the magnitude and preferred local model depending on the objective. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/nizswan/Zeroth-First-Order-Framework.

</details>


### [323] [The Missing Primitive: Diagnosing and Repairing Mathematical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02191)
*Shuo Xing,Zilin Dai,Chengyuan Qian,Fangzhou Lin,Wenjing Chen,Ping He,Pan Lu,Alvaro Velasquez,Mohit Bansal,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"数学基元"概念与四维评测基准，系统诊断LLM的数学理解能力，并据此设计基元优先的自蒸馏框架以提升数学推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在前沿数学问题上表现突出，但其是否具备支撑解题的结构化数学理解仍不明确，需系统研究这种理解能力。

Method: 提出"数学基元"(Mathematical Primitive)概念，构建从发现、生成、消化、执行四个维度评测数学推理的基准；进行系统性能力诊断与后训练分析；提出基元优先的自蒸馏框架(ABS)，选择性将基元引导的推理迁移到学生模型。

Result: 诊断显示：解题准确率掩盖了不同能力画像；基元可释放大量潜在执行能力；发现是数学推理的主要瓶颈；受限于发现能力的失败更易修复。实验表明ABS在多种模型规模和困难基准上稳定提升数学推理性能。

Conclusion: LLM的数学理解可被结构化刻画与诊断，基于基元的自蒸馏方法能有效提升数学推理，为后续研究提供了新视角与工具。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated striking capabilities on frontier mathematical problems, it remains unclear whether they possess the structural mathematical understanding underlying their solutions. In this paper, we take a first step toward systematically studying mathematical understanding in LLMs, from diagnosing its distinct capabilities to leveraging these findings to improve post-training. First, we introduce the notion of Mathematical Primitive to probe structural mathematical understanding and propose \hlei{}, a novel benchmark that evaluates mathematical reasoning along four distinct dimensions: Discovery, Generation, Digestion, and Execution. Second, our systematic diagnosis shows that solution accuracy masks distinct capability profiles, primitives unlock substantial latent execution capacity, and Discovery is the dominant bottleneck in mathematical reasoning. Our post-training analysis further shows that discovery-limited failures are particularly amenable to repair. Finally, building on these findings, we introduce \abs{}, a primitive-privileged self-distillation framework that selectively transfers primitive-guided reasoning into the student model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \abs{} consistently improves mathematical reasoning over baselines across model scales and challenging benchmarks.

</details>


### [324] [Cost-augmented Schrödinger bridges on graphs are exactly solvable: a Feynman-Kac tilt replaces learned control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02195)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在图上的广义Schrödinger桥问题的精确求解方法：通过Feynman-Kac倾斜将状态代价并入参考过程，使代价增强桥成为普通桥，从而无需时间差分惩罚；用两次端点重标定（各为稀疏矩阵指数运算）交替精确计算桥，收敛率仅由端点耦合决定；对二次拥堵代价，阻尼最佳响应等价于强凸函数上的梯度下降且残差可界定误差；在蛋白质折叠和百万级路口路网上验证了有效性与线性内存增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上广义Schrödinger桥需要在移动质量的同时对访问状态收费，已有方法依赖学习受控连续时间马尔可夫链的速率并引入时间差分惩罚来恢复代价，既需学习又需时间离散，效率与精确性受限。作者希望找到无需学习、无需时间离散的精确求解途径。

Method: 利用Feynman-Kac倾斜把状态代价折入参考过程，使代价增强桥等价于针对倾斜参考过程的普通桥，从而免除惩罚项。桥通过交替两次端点重标定精确计算，每次仅为一次稀疏矩阵指数作用，不做时间离散或学习；交替收敛率仅由端点耦合决定。对时间平均占据上的二次拥堵代价，采用围绕精确桥的阻尼最佳响应，证明其等价于强凸函数上的梯度下降，残差可界定误差。

Result: 在蛋白质折叠模型上，自由能代价降低了折叠路径的期望势垒；在学习方法的道路网络上，精确桥的roll-out在采样误差内匹配目标；在拥有数百万交叉点的网络上，内存呈线性增长。

Conclusion: 该工作为图上广义Schrödinger桥提供了精确、无学习、无时间离散的求解框架，收敛率仅取决于端点耦合，并在复杂代价与大规模网络上验证了其精确性、可扩展性与内存效率。

Abstract: The generalized Schrödinger bridge on a graph moves mass between two distributions while charging a cost for the states visited. It has been approached by learning the rates of a controlled continuous-time Markov chain, with a temporal-difference penalty that restores the cost. A state cost folds into the reference process as a Feynman-Kac tilt. The cost-augmented bridge is then a plain bridge against the tilted reference, and the penalty is unnecessary. The bridge is computed exactly by alternating two endpoint rescalings, each one sparse matrix-exponential application; nothing is discretized in time or learned. The alternation converges at a rate set by the endpoint coupling alone. For a quadratic congestion cost on time-averaged occupancies, damped best response around the exact bridge is gradient descent on a strongly convex function, and its residual bounds its error. On a protein-folding model, a free-energy cost lowers the expected barrier of the folding paths. On the learned approach's road network, roll-outs of the exact bridge match the target within sampling error, and on networks with millions of intersections its memory grows linearly.

</details>


### [325] [FERPO: Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02198)
*Sebastian Sanokowski,Alireza Sarmadi,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FERPO是一种在线最大熵强化学习算法，利用critic的价值（而非动作梯度）进行策略改进，通过前向KL目标拟合由熵和KL正则化导出的最优目标动作分布，在MuJoCo Playground和ManiSkill上表现具有竞争力且样本效率更高，actor更新速度快于REPPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续控制在线强化学习方法多依赖学习到的critic的动作梯度来改进策略，但critic通常被训练用于预测回报，准确的价值预测未必能带来准确的动作导数，可能导致不可靠的策略更新。

Method: 提出FERPO，一种在线策略（on-policy）最大熵强化学习算法，不通过对critic关于动作求导来进行策略改进，而是使用critic价值。算法从经熵和KL散度正则化的策略改进目标中导出最优目标动作分布，然后通过最小化前向KL目标将actor拟合到该目标分布，并用自归一化重要性采样（SNIS）以rollout策略采样的动作进行估计；KL正则化限制目标分布与rollout策略的偏离，从而保持重要性权重良好。相比反向KL目标可能偏向目标分布的少数模态，前向KL目标鼓励覆盖多个高价值模态，从而促进探索。

Result: 在MuJoCo Playground和ManiSkill上的实验与消融显示具有竞争力的性能和样本效率提升；计算基准测试表明actor更新速度比Relative Entropy Pathwise Policy Optimization (REPPO)更快。

Conclusion: FERPO通过使用critic价值而非动作梯度进行策略改进，并结合前向KL拟合与KL正则化的SNIS估计，实现了可靠、高效且更具探索性的在线强化学习，在性能、样本效率和计算速度上均有优势。

Abstract: Several state-of-the-art methods for online reinforcement learning in continuous control improve policies using action gradients of a learned critic. However, critics are typically trained to predict returns, and accurate value predictions do not necessarily yield accurate action derivatives, potentially leading to unreliable policy updates. We propose Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization (FERPO), an on-policy maximum entropy reinforcement learning algorithm that performs policy improvement using critic values without differentiating the critic with respect to actions. FERPO derives an optimal target action distribution from a policy-improvement objective regularized by entropy and Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. We then fit the actor to this target by minimizing a forward-KL objective, estimated using self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS) with actions drawn from the rollout policy. By limiting the target distribution's deviation from the rollout policy, the KL regularization helps keep these importance weights well behaved. In contrast to reverse-KL objectives, which can favor a subset of the target distribution's modes, the forward-KL objective encourages coverage of multiple high-value modes and thereby promotes exploration. Experiments and ablations on MuJoCo Playground and ManiSkill show competitive performance and sample-efficiency gains. Computational benchmarks also demonstrate faster actor updates than Relative Entropy Pathwise Policy Optimization (REPPO).

</details>


### [326] [TACO: Ternary Absolute-max Column-wise One-sparse Optimizer for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02199)
*Jichao Jiang,Cristian McGee,El Houcine Bergou,Hanqin Cai,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACO是一种新型优化器，通过列方向1-sparse一阶更新大幅降低LLM微调的优化器状态内存，在保持精度与效率的同时支持单卡80GB H100微调30-32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调LLM的优化器状态内存开销巨大，限制了可训练模型规模。现有方法或压缩状态、或放弃一阶梯度、或改变更新几何，而Muon虽减少内存但几何与AdamW不同，微调AdamW预训练模型时性能下降。

Method: 提出TACO（Ternary Absolute-max Column-wise One-sparse）优化器。遵循Muon的算子范数最速下降视角，在维度归一化的1→1算子范数下计算精确最速下降方向：对二维权重矩阵每列选取最大幅值元素符号，仅保留每列少量低精度梯度分量，实现近乎可忽略的优化器状态。

Result: 在OPT-13B上，持久优化器状态相比AdamW8bit减少174倍（27.7GB降至0.16GB），峰值训练内存减少2.9倍（80.6GB降至27.5GB），精度与运行时间相当。可在单张80GB H100上全参数微调30-32B模型。

Conclusion: TACO在保留一阶梯度的同时将优化器状态内存降至近乎可忽略，兼顾精度与计算效率，使大模型全参数微调在单卡上成为可能。

Abstract: Full-parameter fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial optimizer state memory overhead, limiting the model sizes that fit on modern GPUs. Existing approaches either compress optimizer state, abandon first-order gradients, or change the update geometry while retaining dense state. The recently introduced Muon optimizer reduces optimizer memory through matrix-valued updates. Still, its geometry differs from AdamW and can lead to performance degradation when fine-tuning AdamW-pretrained models. To reduce optimizer memory without sacrificing accuracy or computational efficiency in LLM fine-tuning, we propose Ternary Absolute-max Column-wise One-sparse optimizer, or TACO, which follows Muon's operator-norm steepest-descent view but takes the geometric route further. TACO computes the exact steepest-descent direction under a dimension-normalized $1\to1$ operator norm by selecting the sign of the largest magnitude entry in each column of two-dimensional weight matrices. This retains first-order gradients while making optimizer state memory nearly negligible. Our practical TACO optimizer maintains only a small set of low precision gradient components per column, reducing persistent optimizer state by $174\times$ relative to AdamW8bit (from 27.7 GB to 0.16 GB) and peak training memory by $2.9\times$ (from 80.6 GB to 27.5 GB) on OPT-13B, while achieving comparable accuracy and runtime. TACO further enables full-parameter fine-tuning of 30-32B-parameter models on a single 80 GB H100 GPU across multiple model families and tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Supporting Perspective Acquisition and Opinion Formation on Societal Issues Through AI-Generated Japanese Rap Battle Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01179)
*Ryota Mibayashi,Toru Urakawa,Dai Takanashi,Tomoya Morohoshi,Kanata Yamagishi,Ryuho Sekikawa,Yasuhiko Nishimura,Yuta Takeuchi,Hideaki Tamori,Takehiro Yamamoto,Hidenari Kiyomitsu,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本研究探究简短AI生成的说唱对战式辩论能否促进社会议题的观点获取与意见形成。通过GPT-5.4-mini自动生成辩论并配以合成语音，6名参与者观看40个辩题，结果显示约1.5分钟的观看几乎使观点与讨论点数量翻倍，52.5%的案例中参与者重新考虑或改变了立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取多元视角并形成有依据的意见对社会议题的批判性思维与决策至关重要。观察对立观点的辩论虽有助益，但传统辩论耗时耗力，可及性受限，因此需要探索更轻量、易获取的替代形式。

Method: 采用说唱对战（rap battle）这一押韵对辩形式，在保留论证对话结构的同时提升趣味性；使用GPT-5.4-mini自动生成说唱对战式辩论，配以合成音频并经网页界面呈现；以全日本初高中辩论锦标赛的社会议题为素材生成40个辩论，招募6名参与者进行用户研究。

Result: 观看约1.5分钟的说唱对战式辩论后，参与者识别的观点与讨论点数量几乎翻倍；在受评估案例中有52.5%的参与者重新考虑或改变了原有立场。

Conclusion: 即便是简短的AI生成说唱对战式辩论，也能有效支持社会议题上的观点获取与意见形成，为低成本、易获取的辩论式学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Acquiring diverse perspectives and forming informed opinions on societal issues are essential for critical thinking and informed decision-making. Although observing debates between opposing viewpoints can promote perspective acquisition, conventional debates require substantial time and human resources, limiting their accessibility. This study investigates whether brief AI-generated rap battle-style debates can support perspective acquisition and opinion formation on societal issues. Rap battles are debate-style performances in which opposing viewpoints are expressed through rhymed verses, making them engaging while preserving the structure of argumentative dialogue. To conduct this investigation, we automatically generated rap battle-style debates using GPT-5.4-mini and presented them to participants with synthesized audio through a web-based viewing interface. We evaluated the educational effectiveness of this approach through a user study involving six participants who viewed 40 debates generated from topics on societal issues used in the All Japan Junior and Senior High School Debate Championships. The results showed that watching approximately 1.5 minute rap battle-style debates nearly doubled the number of opinions and discussion points identified by participants. Furthermore, participants reconsidered or changed their stances in 52.5% of the evaluated cases. These findings demonstrate that even brief AI-generated rap battle-style debates can effectively support perspective acquisition and opinion formation on societal issues.

</details>
